Lyra's "done" motion is her own (`fbSing91` + `waveOut91`; every agent's idle/working/done motion stays unique). Sketch's two panels are equal height on a computer (`.sk91` stretch).
- Tests changed: `agents.py` (nine, order with voice), `v90.py` (nine everywhere), `v72_features.py` / `v79_fixes.py` / `v80_hubs.py` / `voice_sketch.py` / `draw_world.py` (the new Sketch flow; Draw a scene opens it), `v84.py` (no Sketch / Draw pickers), `v81_calm.py` (three New badges), `flat_bots` label 9/9, `breakpoints.py` / `hidden_text.py` (text inside any closed section is skipped, not only the nearest one), `equal_layout.py` (`.edcmdx` is a deliberate scrolling row on phones), `tests/visual82.json` rewritten for the redesigned Sketch, crew and phone editor. Shipped translation line 177 = "Nine specialists."

## v90 One look — a colour per section, one header, one tile, one card · Audio section · All tools
- Client block `/* ================= v90` in index.html (before LOOP + BOOT); the rules are in docs/ROADMAP.md §10 and checked on every page by `tests/v90.py` — keep new pages inside them.
- Section colours: `SEC90` (hub → `image|video|audio|chars|world|edit|publish`, everything else `brand`), `sec90(view)`, `secKey90(menu key)`. CSS tokens `--c-<section>` (dark shades by default, darker ones under the light-theme selectors; each ≥ 3:1 on its 15 % tint) and `--hub`, set by `[data-sec90]` — on `body` (the open page), on each `.tool` card and each menu item (`look90` after `renderView`, `menu90` after `renderShell`). Choices stay neutral and the picked one is nooi green; the section colour only says where you are.
- Header: `look90` puts `.tchip90` (the tool's `TOOLS82` icon in `--hub`) at the start of the page's `h1` (not on Home); the h1 text stays the tool's name. Tool cards (`toolGrid`, Home + All tools): `.tool .ico` in `--hub` instead of v86's gradient per tool.
- One scale: corner roundings `--r-s` 8 · `--r-m` 12 · `--r-l` 16 (pills/circles aside); titles h1 22 (27 above 820 px; `.h82` follows) · h2 16 · h3 14; icons 14 beside small text · 16 beside normal text · 20 standing alone — the rules use `!important` because many icons carry their own inline size. Option tiles: `.tile:not(.brand) .gl:has(>svg)` 32 px square, 20 px icon (also `.ugcmode` cards).
- Tabs: `look90` scrolls the open tab into view (`scrollLeft` delta, works in RTL) and `tabFade90` sets `.fs90`/`.fe90` (a 28 px mask at the side with more tabs; mirrored for `dir=rtl`), also on scroll / resize.
- Map: `HUB82` gets `apps` ("All tools", after Explore) and `audio` ("Audio", after Video); `voice` and `subs` move to `audio` in `TOOLS82`; `HUBS`, `ALLNAV`, `NAVS` rebuilt; `ALIAS82.audio="voice"` (the menu's Audio opens Voice & audio). Edit has five tabs now. `VIEWS.apps`: every enabled tool with a `TOOLDESC`, grouped by `APPSEC90`, as `toolGrid` cards, and a filter box (`[data-apps90]`, filters the cards in place — no re-render, focus kept). Home's Tools title has "All tools" (`hfHome` wrapper). Menu words "Audio" / "All tools" are lines 378–379 of `i18n/*.txt`.
- The AI agents are never "bots" in anything a visitor sees (English "AI agents", Arabic "وكلاء"; code names like `BOTS`, `.sbot`, `hfbots` stay). One order everywhere = the order the crew runs: `ORDER90` writer → casting → director → cine → sound → editor → producer → proof (the Producer prices the Editor's final cut and picks the model per shot; Iris checks last). `BOTS` and `HF_BOT_IDS` are sorted by it (the home box had Director first and the Character Designer after the Cinematographer). One job line per agent: `AGENT90` sets `BOTS[i].job` and `CREW_INFO` (hover cards, landing page) to the same words. nooi Studio shows "Who does what — in the order they work" (`roles90`, inserted before `.crewgrid`); `hfStudioPlanHTML` is rewritten in that order (the Cinematographer writes the prompts, as its job says; `li[data-bot]`); `botStrip` titles say name · role — job. Menu line 314 of `i18n/*.txt` says agents.
- Tests: `tests/v90.py` (browser; en phone light, en computer dark, ar phone dark, en phone dark — also: no "bot" word on any page, one agent order everywhere, who does what); `tests/v80_hubs.py` expects the new menu and five Edit tabs; `tests/v86.py` checks the tool cards' tinted icon squares.

## v89 Icons never show a number · the home box lists every model · failed previews · results kept on the server · dead code removed
- Icons: no icon shows a number. `glyphFor` → `starsGlyph(path,k)`: a count (`COUNT_PATHS`: takes, pictures per prompt) is that many stars (`STAR_SPOTS`, 8 = two rows), any other number the star; the number goes in the label (`labelFor`: "1 take", "2 takes", "16:9 · Landscape"; a ratio is an empty shape). Loaders are the stars only (thumbnail `.veil.v80`, the big preview `sveil81`, the home box's `.hfrph` with `STARS("load")`) — no percent, no "Usually 1–3 minutes", no elapsed timer, no progress bar (`data-pct` is gone; the chapter list keeps its plain bar). The home box's model list puts a star (`.mrk` with `STARS()`) on the strongest three, not 1·2·3; a busy crew member shows the star in `.sjobs`, not a count.
- The home box's model list (`hfModels` → `railOf(VRAIL|IRAIL, MODELS|IMG_MODELS)`) has every model the Video / Image pages offer, the rail's order first, then the rest (Seedance 2.0 Mini, Kling 3.0 Turbo, Grok Imagine 1.5, HappyHorse, Gemini Omni Video, NVIDIA Cosmos …), each with its logo.
- Failures: `errText81` says a dropped connection in plain words (`netErr88` / `NET88`), also for jobs saved before with the browser's "Failed to fetch"; the home box's failed card uses it too. A failed (or being-made) preview has no play / pause button; the preview's caption is the client's words (`previewCol` wrapper with `shown88`). `API.req` errors carry the HTTP `status`; a job the server no longer has (the app's own 404 "Not found" twice within a minute — deleted on another device; never a web-server page while the site restarts) stops being followed (`goneJob` in the three polls) and says so (`ERR_MSG.gone`, no "Try again" toast).
- Thumbnails: the length (`.thumb .t`) is top left (inline-start; the × to delete is top right). A poster frame is set only when it loads (`posterOk`), and videos keep `preload="metadata"`, so a video whose frame can't be made still shows its own first frame.
- Server: `download()` (lib/media.js) has a time limit on the whole download (a hanging file server never holds a job "being made" forever) and tries a busy server (5xx/429) or a dropped connection again twice. `lib/v89.js` `keepMedia()` (30 s after start, then every 30 min): finished results from the last two days that still point at an engine's (expiring) link are copied here (three tries each); the page's `syncServer` takes the server's `/media` address for a finished job. `/v1/llm/test` is the owner's only (`requireAdmin("providers.manage")`); the public `/v1/config` says `llm:{on:true}` — never the provider, model or address (the owner sees them after "Test connection" in Connections). The video analysis page tells customers only "isn't available right now" (the setup line with provider names is the owner's).
- Dead code removed (≈141 KB of index.html): 31 functions replaced whole later (their first bodies and the wrappers in between could never run — the replacement became the declaration), unused functions / consts (`cardH`, `editTick`, `toolSVG`, `eyesSVG`, `botFxSVG`, `BLOB_*`, `SPIKY_*` …), logo pictures set two or three times (the earlier ones were never shown), 196 CSS rules for classes nothing uses (`.bot2`, `.uni*`, `.w3*`, `.micbtn`, `.nvbadge` …) and 22 animations nothing plays; server: `elClone`, `giftStats`. The scratch tools that found them are not part of the repo; `tests/v89_api.mjs` keeps checking "no function nothing calls" and "each logo set once".
- Tests: `tests/v89.py` (browser, desktop + Arabic phone), `tests/v89_api.mjs` (downloads, keeping results, config / text AI test privacy, code checks).

## v88 Creating works on phones · clean prompt boxes · pictures in every list · delete with a confirmation · a photo becomes a World
- Client block `/* ================= v88` in index.html (before LOOP + BOOT). Server: `lib/v88.js` (`registerV88(app, requireUser)` after `registerWorld`); `server.js` logs `unhandledRejection` / `uncaughtException` instead of dying (a crash restarted the service and every request in flight failed with "Failed to fetch").
- Phones ("Failed to fetch"): `loadSlot` wrapper reads a picked file into memory at once (`prep88`: a photo over 2048 px or 1.5 MB, or HEIC/AVIF/BMP → a JPEG up to 2048 px (a logo keeps PNG transparency); other files are copied into memory; Android can't read a gallery file later) and uploads it in the background; `API.upload` keeps one upload per blob (`UP88`, a WeakMap) so the job reuses it; `API.req` tries POST `/v1/uploads` and `/v1/jobs` again twice (1.5 s, 4 s) on a network error (the server answers the same request with the same job) and shows `NET88()` instead of the browser's words.
- Prompts in English for the engines (`jobHooks.before` in `lib/v88.js`, kinds video/chapter/image/look/sheet/3d): non-Latin words → `toEnglish` (the quick text AI, 6 s, memo + one request for the same words at once; quoted words and lines someone says aloud kept as written; the original words when the text AI fails or answers in the same script; `meta.said` keeps the original; `meta.keepWords` skips it). The job's title keeps the client's own words. Video / chapter prompts (not Motion Control) get `NO_TEXT` unless the words ask for text (`noTextLine`; never on an empty prompt — an empty request is still refused with nothing charged; a spoken line is not text). Motion Control always sends a prompt (`kieInput`: the words + "The person in the picture performs exactly the same movements …"; Kie answered 422 "prompt is required").
- Prompt boxes: no microphone (`addMics` empty, `.micbtn` hidden), no resize grip (they grow with the text), the site's own instructions never show in a box (`INTERNAL88`, `words88`, `tidyPrompts88` on every `renderView`; a result card shows only the user's words), and the box is empty after a start (`after88` for `makeVideo`/`hfSend`; `makeImages` without an argument; Motion Control's words after its job).
- Pictures in lists: `openSel` / `enhanceSelects` buttons show a picture (`pic88`): faces for character lists (an empty silhouette when there is none), logos (`logoFor`) for engine lists, thumbnails for Motion Control's library; keyed by `dataset.p88`. Videos from the server get `poster="/v1/poster/<file>"` (`posters88`, a MutationObserver) — `GET /v1/poster/:name` makes one frame at 40 % with ffmpeg (`thumbnail` filter, ≤ 720 px), kept as `<file>.poster.jpg`, 2 at a time.
- Delete: × on finished videos / pictures (`thumb` wrapper, a `span role=button.del88` — some thumbs are inside buttons) and beside each character (`.crw88 .del88.c`), always through `confirm88` (a sheet with Delete / Cancel). `dropJob88` clears every place the job is used and calls `DELETE /v1/jobs/:id` (finished or failed jobs only, 409 while being made; the file stays).
- Removed (marked by the owner): "Powered by NVIDIA AI" (`nvBadge` empty), Motion Control 2.6 on the page (3.0 with its logo; `mc30` is on Pro now, server and client), the UGC name / price / "what makes it special" fields → one optional line `#ugcabout88` (`draft.ugc.desc`; an old name is kept in it). The Images page's Generate bar is no longer sticky (`.ipage .igen{position:static}`), so the page doesn't jump.
- A photo → a World you walk in: `wantsWorld88` (a world word and a walk / 3D word, or a world word + "from this photo" with a photo — "put my product in a world of flowers" stays a picture) sends the home box / one-box run to the World with the photo (`toWorld88`, no picture job). `nwGo` wrapper: photo only → the place read from the photo (`photoLook88`: sky and ground colours), the AI plan within 20 s else `localPlan`, an indoor photo gets an open floor, the ground takes the photo's colour; the photo is put around you (`photoSurround88` → a 2048×1024 surround, uploaded on the server) — words + photo: the words' world with the photo around you.
- Tests: `tests/v88.py` (browser, desktop + Arabic phone), `tests/v88_api.mjs` (Motion Control prompt, `toEnglish`/`noTextLine` units, a real server with a mocked Kie and text AI: English prompts, the no-text line, a busy text AI, delete 409/ok, poster frames).

## v87 Real characters everywhere · one video player · the 3D World starts empty and comes alive with real-looking people · Model Studio 3D
- Characters are real (block `/* ================= v87: REAL CHARACTERS` in index.html): every character has ONE real face — `face87(c)` = `c.face` (a server address: the photo it was made from, uploaded by `upFace87`; or a finished real look; or a ready presenter's portrait `c.av87`). The drawn previews are gone: `thumb("cp:"|"cb:"|"look:")` shows the face or an empty frame (`.face87.none`, a silhouette) with "Make it real" (`makeReal87` → a `look` job with `meta.real87`; the look prompt is written in full here with the photoreal words, `meta.lp79` skips the older rewrite) and "Make all real". Look jobs use `FACE87()` (Nano Banana Pro on Pro/Studio plans, else Nano Banana 2) — models that take several reference pictures.
- Looks of a character: with a face (or the photo it was made from) "4 real looks" / "Character sheet" are made FROM that face (`chRef`) with the quick picture engine `LOOK87()` (Nano Banana) — the same person each time, 4 × `lookCost87()`; with no face yet the 4 looks are candidate faces with the face engine `FACE87()` (Nano Banana Pro on Pro/Studio plans) and the first that finishes becomes the face (tap another to switch). Server: `priceOf("look"|"sheet", {model})` = that engine's picture price (never below the old 2 / 6), so the face, looks and sheet are charged what the button says.
- The face goes with the jobs: `withSlots87(map, fn)` fills job slots for one job (`{type:"image",remote:url}` or a File). Kie: `refImg` falls back to `iRef2`; Nano Banana 2/Pro `image_input`, Seedream, GPT Image 2 i2i, Flux 2 i2i get every reference (`[ref, ...iRef2..4]`). Google's own Nano Banana (`providers/images2.js`) sends all references as inline data, read from /media or a web address (it used to send only `data:` URLs — uploads never reached it).
- UGC ads presenter (`ugcMake79` wrapper; the presenter and the Video page's character are separate choices — both pages keep `S.draft.charId`, and moving between them swaps in `S.draft.ugcChar87` / `S.draft.vidChar87` (a `renderView` wrapper before LOOP + BOOT); a late ad step runs with its own presenter (`ugcStep1_87` wrapper)): step 0 only when the presenter has no face (its real face, `S.ugc79.wait87`) → step 1 a photo of THAT person with the product (`iRef` = product photo, `iRef2` = face; without a product photo `iRef` = face; `ugcFaceLine87`) → step 2 the video from that photo (Kling 3.0 Turbo `kling30t` when "Smart pick"). Presenter picker `presPicker87()`: "Your characters" + "Ready presenters" (the showcase avatars that exist, `GET /v1/avatars` now returns `who`, `total`, `making`); picking one makes a character `av87-<id>` with that photo; the owner sees "Make the presenters' photos" (`POST /v1/admin/showcase {group:"avatars"}`) while some are missing — customers never see letters.
- "@" in prompts (`PROMPTS87` = `hfq`, `dprompt`, `iprompt`, `nwprompt`): typing `@` opens the list (`.at87`), the "Characters" chip (`chip87`, home box and 3D World only) opens a sheet; the character picked in the Video / Images page's own list (`draft.charId` / `img.charId` → `meta.char`, which the server never used) is treated exactly like an `@Name`; a job that names `@Name` (`charsIn87`, whole name after @) carries that face: a picture puts the faces in free `iRef…iRef4` slots; a video without a first frame first makes a picture of the scene with those people (`meta.frame87`) and then the video starts from it (`S.flow87`); a character without a face gets one first. The @ is removed from the words sent (`plain87`). Jobs made by this block (`NO87`) and jobs with `meta.ugc|face87|chars87|a3|nw|sketch|ent|showcase` are not looked at again.
- One video player (`vpUpgrade87`, a MutationObserver upgrades every `video[controls]` outside the editor/World; a page redraw — a job starting or finishing — carries a playing player video into the new page instead of restarting it, the last `renderView` wrapper before LOOP + BOOT): play/pause, seek with the time, sound, full screen, keys; the bar hides while playing (tap shows it). Only one video with sound plays (silent looping previews never pause it); the viewer (`viewMedia80` wrapper) is fully black, stops and hides the page's videos (`body.mv87`) and nothing starts behind it. Results on a phone are compact (`.stage.st87`, width from the aspect and 48vh). No percentage numbers anywhere (`[data-pct]` hidden, VSS/localization/ingest texts stripped).
- 3D World (block `/* the 3D World starts EMPTY`): the page opens on an empty stage (`createEmpty()` in world.js: soft sky, floor rings, floating lights, the camera turning; the text "Write what you want — it comes to life here"); nothing is rebuilt from an earlier visit (a world made in this visit comes back: `NW.session87`). `nwGo`: the AI plan gets 12 s (25 s with a picture) — slower → the world from the words at once and "The AI's more detailed version is ready · Show it" (`NW87.better`). The build comes to life (`reveal()` in world.js: the land rises, things grow in from where you stand, life appears). "New world" → the empty stage. A 3D object or handed-over model with no world builds an open park first. Server: "Auto" time is no longer midnight (`+null` was 0).
- People (no box figures: `life.js` skips person/child/robe): `plan.people` = `[{look, gender, age, count, act (walk|stand|wave|talk|sit|run|dance|look|wander|point), target, task, char?}]` — from the AI (SPEC) or the words (`localPeople`: EN/AR, who/how many incl. Arabic duals/what they do/what to; `bench(es)?` fixed). `public/world/human.js`: `loadHuman(url)` normalises any person .glb (tallest side up, toes forward, feet at 0, 1 unit), fits joints from the shape (crotch, torso width per band, arms by distance from the shoulder, knees/ankles), builds a 17-bone skeleton, weights each point to its nearest bone on its side with a blend near joints, and `createPeople()` moves them (walk with hip sway/knee bend/foot roll/arm swing, run, stand + breathing, wave, talk, sit on a seat, dance, point; walk to the target, talkers meet, avoid each other and blocked cells, turn to you within 3 m, grow in). Who they are: your characters (`@Name` → `c.body3d`) or the site's people (`GET /v1/world/cast`, `castPick87` by look/gender/age).
- 3D bodies: a character's 3D body = full-body photo of that person from its face (`make3d87`, `meta.body87`) → `3d` job `mode:"body"` → `c.body3d` (Characters page "Make 3D · walks in your worlds" `b87-make`; World card "Make <name> 3D"). The site's people (`lib/world.js CAST`, 8 fictional people): the owner taps "Make the people" (World page) → `POST /v1/admin/world/cast` → Nano Banana Pro full-body A-pose photo → `jobHooks.done` starts the 3D job → `col("worldcast")`; made once, free for clients.
- Model Studio 3D (`providers/gen3d.js ms3d`, the customer never sees the name): the DashScope key (Admin → AI providers → Qwen: "3D model" empty = `Tripo/Tripo-P1.0`, `off` = not used; "3D API URL" for another region; env `DASHSCOPE_3D_MODEL`/`DASHSCOPE_3D_URL`). `POST {base}/services/aigc/video-generation/3d-generation` (async) `{model, input:{prompt}|{image: public URL}, parameters:{texture_quality}}` → `GET {base}/tasks/{id}` → `output.results[0].pbr_model_url` mirrored at once (the link lasts 2 h). First 3D engine for `engine:"auto"` (`ms3dOn()`); a failure moves the job once to Tripo/Meshy (`job.triedMs3d`). World caps `objects3d` counts it. Rendering: tiers draw sharper (low 0.7–1.25, mid 0.85–1.75 with antialiasing, high 1–2).
- Tests: `tests/v87.py` (browser, desktop + Arabic phone: faces/no drawings, make real, presenters, face on every UGC step, @ in four boxes, @video/@picture/no @, the player, the viewer, compact, no %, empty World, reveal, people walk/run/wave, skeleton fit for turned / Z-up files, your character sits on the bench, New world, Auto daytime, nothing sideways), `tests/v87_api.mjs` (Model Studio 3D shapes/routing/fallback, faces to Kie and Google, presenters count, Auto time, plan people, the site's people made end to end with mocked Kie + DashScope), `tests/human_glb.py` (test person models). Older World tests open the empty stage and build from words.

## v86 One 3D World (replaces 3D worlds, 3D Studio, Physics page, depth world, on-device 3D) · physics in every video · one picker with pictures · template drawings
- Product map and rules: `docs/ROADMAP.md` (why each page exists, what was merged or removed, what comes next). Every button and what it does: `docs/UI_MAP.md`, made by `python3 tools/uimap.py` — run it after changing pages.
- The page: `VIEWS.world` (block `/* ================= v86:` in index.html; hub id `world`, menu "3D"). Old ids `w3ai`, `studio3d`, `physics`, `w3d`, `l3d` and `?view=…`, `/world3d/`, `/studio3d/`, `/local3d/` all open it (`ALIAS82`, the `go` wrapper, a deep-link check, tiny redirect pages). The stage `#nwstage` stays mounted while the panel `#nwui` redraws. One prompt box creates a world or changes it (`isEdit`), simple options (time, weather, look Fast · free / Realistic), examples, then Photo · Video tour · Use in Create video · Make it a real photo · Save (12) · .glb · New; a picture (or a photo handed over by any tool through `setS3dFromDataURL` + `go('studio3d')`) becomes a world, and "one thing → 3D object" sends one `3d` job (engine auto).
- Engine `public/world/` (ES modules, import map → `/vendor/three-<ver>/…`, three.js r186): `engine.js` (one renderer: WebGPU when the adapter is real, else WebGL2; tiers low/mid/high from the device; resolution follows the frame time; nothing drawn while hidden; GPU loss or a failing WebGPU frame → the same scene goes on in WebGL2 and that browser starts there for 7 days (`nooiWorldBackend=webgl:<time>`); `guardTextureViews()` drops three's identity `swizzle:"rgba"` that some Chrome builds reject and keeps expected device-loss rejections out of the error log), `plan.js` (plan v2 + `cleanPlan` shared with the server, local EN/AR reading, `editPlan`), `assets.js` (80+ low-poly models, instanced per material in 48 m chunks), `terrain.js`, `sky.js`, `water.js`, `life.js`, `player.js` (walk/fly/orbit/tour, `face()`), `physics.js` (Rapier, the same `materials.js` table as MuJoCo; things held ride at chest height, E pick up / throw, Q kick or let go, balls have rolling resistance per ground, glass breaks above its speed), `splat.js` (World Labs splats with Spark — WebGL2 — and their collider mesh as the ground), `world.js` (builds it all). Phones destroy the engine when you leave the page.
- Server: `lib/world.js` — `GET /v1/world/caps` (ai, vision, worldlabs, objects3d, pano, cost {plan:1, worldlabs from the price table}), `POST /v1/world/plan {prompt, image?, lang, time?, weather?}` and `POST /v1/world/edit {plan, text, lang}`: the text/vision AI writes the plan (1 credit, staff free, refunded on any failure, then the plan is made from the words — never a dead end); without a text AI the local reading makes it, free. World Labs (Marble): `providers/gen3d.js` `worldlabs()` (header `WLT-Api-Key`, `POST /marble/v1/worlds:generate`, poll `/marble/v1/operations/{id}`; segments in a fixed order [splat 100k, splat 500k, collider .glb, panorama, thumbnail], all copied to /media; `finish()` no longer downloads a file that is already ours), Admin → Connections card `worldlabs`, health probe (a made-up operation → 404 = key accepted), `.env.example` WORLDLABS_*.
- Physics in the work: `lib/physicsdirector.js` (imported by server.js) — a `jobHooks.before` hook for `video`/`chapter` jobs: `physicsFor(prompt)` (EN/AR; whole words; vehicles, people, materials named or implied) → MuJoCo (cached, ≤2 runs at once, the job waits ≤2.5 s) → `sentenceFor()` talks about "it"/"the person" (never our test objects), refuses to contradict the words, rounds numbers → "Physical realism: …" appended; `meta.physics` keeps EN/AR; `publicJob().physics` shows it under "What the AI received" (atom icon). `meta.noPhysics` turns it off. The Physics page is gone.
- Old 3D code: `public/studio3d/*` app removed (it ran image AI on phones — the browser crashes); `lib/worldai.js`, `lib/depthworld.js` removed. The classic global-THREE viewers still in the page (`ensureThree()`: editor 3D tab, a3 meshes, …) load three r128 from the CDN, else `/vendor/three/…` served from the npm alias `three-r128`.
- Looks: template cards draw their own cover (`TPL_ART86`, SVG, unless a staff preview or a matching real clip exists); the v84 sheets for style / light / look / camera move show a picture per choice and moving camera diagrams (`ART86`, paused with reduce-motion); tool cards have colour app icons (`.tool[data-go=…] .ico`).
- Tests: `tests/v86.py` (browser: desktop + Arabic phone — budgets, words → world, change keeps the seed, save/open, photo, leave/return, grab/throw/kick/shatter, splat world on its collider, WebGPU on a software GPU keeps drawing, old doors, template drawings, picture sheets, app icons), `tests/v86_world_api.mjs` (mocked Google + World Labs + real server), `tests/v86_physics.mjs` (prompt reading EN/AR and look-alikes, sentences, the hook). Old tests now check the World (v72, v79, v80, v85, pro_tools, pro_media, world3d_studio, draw_world, brand_dot); `tests/studio3d.py` retired.

## v85 Physics — one physics engine for the whole site (MuJoCo)
- Engine: MuJoCo (Google DeepMind, Apache-2.0) from npm `@mujoco/mujoco` (WASM, exact version in package.json) runs on our server in a worker thread (`lib/physics.js` + `lib/physics-worker.js`; loads in ≈0.15 s, the worker restarts itself after a run longer than 20 s). Phones never download the 10 MB engine — the page only draws the motion it gets back. `POST /v1/physics/simulate {scene, opts}` → `{fps:30, dur, shapes, frames, cloth?, water?, ground?, events, facts, words:{en,ar}}`; free, one run at a time per person, 60 per 10 minutes.
- Scenes (`lib/physics-scenes.js`, one shared system: the same `MATERIALS`, `SURFACES`, water and air in every scene): `objects` (wood, steel, rubber, glass, stone, foam, plastic, ice — bounce measured per material (the `ZETA` table), hollow balls, glass/ice break above an impact speed into 8 pieces that fly apart), `water` (sample points with their share of the volume: Archimedes + drag on the area facing the motion; splash events), `air` (a cloth flag: pressure on the face + drag along the fabric, gusts; paper vs a ball with MuJoCo's ellipsoid air model), `car` (1.4 t body on spring/damper suspension, steering, rear drive, brakes; brake / launch / turn / ramp jump / crash into boxes on asphalt, wet, gravel, snow, ice), `body` (a 70 kg jointed person whose muscles hold the pose, react 0.15 s after losing balance — sit back, hands out, legs ready — and give way after the hit: pushed, trips, drops from a height, hit by a ball, into water).
- Facts are measured, never assumed (fall time, impact speed, bounces, stop distance, air time, first body part to touch, splash speed, floating depth, whether the head is out of the water); the sentence for prompts is built from them in English and Arabic.
- (v86: the page was replaced by physics inside the 3D World and in every video prompt — see v86.) Page was: 3D → Physics (`VIEWS.physics`, block `/* ================= v85:`): scene tiles + options (Material, Shape, Surface, the car, what happens, pushed, muscles, in the air — all through the v84 one picker with what each does), Simulate (free) → three.js player of the server's frames (Z up; play, ×¼ slow motion, scrub, follow/side/above camera, drag to turn), What happened (facts + events), the sentence, and: Make a video of it (recorded at real speed → library, `meta.physics`), Use in Create video / Cinema Studio (the English sentence is added to the description), Character does this (a body clip becomes Motion Control's movement video).
- The bots: `lib/skills/physics.md` (10 craft notes for the director: weight sets the timing, nothing moves without a push, the bounce tells the material, water, air and cloth, cars carry their weight, bodies protect themselves, breaking keeps the motion, slow motion still falls, one cause per shot). ChatGPT / Claude: MCP tool `nooi_physics` (facts + sentence).
- Tests: `tests/v85.py` (numbers against real physics: √(2h/g), √(2gh), bounce by material, glass threshold, floating depths, braking dry vs snow, ramp air time, paper vs ball vs no air, flag in calm vs wind, falls, water entry; free; limits; the page on computer and Arabic phone; the sentence into Create video; a clip into the library; a body clip into Motion Control).
## v85 Gemma 4 on Google's API (the site's text AI)
- `providers/localtext.js` has two routes. Google: Admin → AI providers → "Gemma 4 · Google AI Studio" (`gemma` settings: `apiKey`, `model`, `thinking`, `use`; or `GEMMA_API_KEY`/`GEMMA_MODEL`/`GEMMA_THINKING`/`GEMMA_USE`) → Google's own request `POST …/v1beta/models/<model>:generateContent`, key only in the `x-goog-api-key` header. Models `gemma-4-26b-a4b-it` (default, fast) or `gemma-4-31b-it` (strongest); `models/` prefix is dropped and anything that isn't a plain model name is refused. The system text goes in front of the user text (no `systemInstruction`), parts marked `thought` are skipped, pictures go as `inline_data` (data URLs only — an address is refused before sending), `thinking` = minimal/low/medium/high → `thinkingConfig.thinkingLevel` (+4096 output tokens of room). No key of its own → the Google key (`google` settings / `GOOGLE_API_KEY`) is used once a model is typed; `use=off` switches it off. A Google address typed in "Your own text AI" (with or without `/openai`) takes the same route; anything else stays OpenAI-compatible (Ollama).
- Order: Gemma on Google → your own text AI → NVIDIA → Claude/Qwen (a failure, e.g. Google's free-tier limit 429, falls through; `llm(…, {noLocal:true})` skips both). `localVision()` decides whether pictures go to it. Health: `gemma` probe `GET …/models/<model>` (400 → key rejected, 404 → no such model). "Compare text AIs" labels the column "Gemma on Google".
- Tests: `tests/gemma_google.mjs` (mocked Google server — request shape, header-only key, thinking parts, pictures, errors without the key, fallback, use=off, the Google key reused, health, Compare text AIs).

## v84 One picker for every field
- The same field is chosen the same way on every page (the idea of a prompt bar with chips; our own drawings and words): a chip shows the choice, and tapping it opens one sheet (`hfSheet`) of icon tiles, each with its name and what it does (English/Arabic). Fields (`PKF84`): format, camera, lens (lens type + focal length + aperture in one sheet), camera move, light, style, look, effects (several at once), weather & light, transition, text effect — on Create video, Create image, Chapter story, Cinema Studio, nooi Studio, Draw, Recipes, Video from pictures, UGC ads, Characters, Live sketch, Visual effects and the video editor. The home prompt bar's format sheet uses the same tiles.
- How it works (`/* ================= v84:` block): `pk84apply()` runs after every `renderView` (and on parts that redraw themselves, through a MutationObserver on `#main`). It puts a chip where a field's buttons were and moves those buttons and their label into a hidden `.pk84src`; the sheet's tiles copy their `data-*` attributes, so each page's own handler sets the same state as before. The chips of one `.panel` share one bar (`.pk84row`); outside a panel (Chapter story's columns) a chip stays where its choice was. One-choice sheets close on a pick; the lens and effects sheets stay open until Done.
- `PKV84` holds what each choice does and icons where a page had none (formats are drawn from their ratio); `PKA84` maps other ids to the same entry (`dolly-in` = `dollyIn`, slideshow `none` = cut). To add a field: one `PKF84` row + its `PKV84` words; `tests/v84.py` checks every page (no old buttons showing, one chip, icon + name + description on every tile, the same 9:16 tile everywhere, state set as before, Arabic phones fit).
- Cinema Studio: Format now sits with Camera, Lens, Move and Light; Make + Length share a row; Engine below.

## v83 New engines · Motion Control · Recipes · your own text AI · backup voice · Video from pictures
- New engines (request shapes read from HeliosGen's public code — facts only, no code copied): Kling 3.0 Turbo (`kling/v3-turbo-*`), Seedance 2.0 Mini (480p/720p), Gemini Omni Video (4/6/8/10 s), Grok Imagine 1.5 (starts from a picture — `checkJob` refuses without one), HappyHorse (`happyhorse/*`), Nano Banana 2 Lite (`image_urls`, matched before the Nano Banana 2 rule), Seedream 5 Lite, Z-Image (1000 characters). Registry `lib/models.js`, engine ids `KIE_BUILTIN`, inputs `kieInput`, lengths `kieDur`, plans `lib/plans.js`. Engine costs in `ENGINE_COST` are estimates on the high side — type the real ones in Admin → AI providers → Pricing.
- Motion Control (Video → third tab, view `motion`): a photo of the character + a video of the movement (3–30 s) → `kind:"video"`, model `mc26`/`mc30`, inputs `mcChar`/`mcMove`, `meta.consent` required (rights to the photo and video), priced per 5 s of the movement video (×1.5 in 1080p), never moved to another model on failure (another model can't copy the movement). The page uploads both files itself and sends them as `inputs83` (serverSubmit wrapper; `inputsUrl:{}` keeps the page's other slots out).
- Recipes (Explore → Recipes, view `recipes`): ready steps in a row (picture → video from it → sharper copy / voiceover). The first job carries the rest in `meta.chain` (cleaned by `lib/recipes.js`: known kinds, 4 steps, no nested chains); when a step is made `jobHooks.done` starts the next one from its result (`SLOT_OF`: video ← startImage, image ← iRef, finish ← fin), charged when it starts; `job.next` / `job.chainError` tell the page (the runner every 3 s adds the next job to the page). A failed step stops the recipe (its credits come back); an unaffordable step stops it with what was made kept. "Save as my recipe" keeps up to 12.
- What the AI received: the engine adapters return `sent` (words and settings only — file addresses are counted, never shown; `sentView` in `providers/kie.js`), kept on the job and shown in a fold under results (`details.sent83`, loaded when opened).
- Your own text AI (`providers/localtext.js`): any OpenAI-compatible chat API (Gemma with Ollama; Gemma on Google takes Google's own route — see v85) via `LOCAL_LLM_URL`/`LOCAL_LLM_MODEL`/`LOCAL_LLM_KEY` or Admin → AI providers → "Your own text AI". Runs text tasks first; NVIDIA/Claude/Qwen take over if it fails (`llm(…, {noLocal:true})` skips it). "Compare text AIs" (`lib/llmcheck.js`, `POST /v1/admin/llm-check`, admins only) scores both on five of nooi's own tasks.
- Backup voice (`providers/piper.js`): Piper voice servers on our own server (`PIPER_URLS="en=…,ar=…"`, `deploy/piper.sh` installs the Piper program + `deploy/piper-server.mjs` on 127.0.0.1; English LJSpeech by default, Arabic only with `--arabic` after checking that voice's licence). A voiceover whose engine voice fails is spoken by it — free (the hold is released); an avatar whose voice fails speaks with it.
- Video from pictures (Edit → second tab, view `slideshow`; `lib/slideshow.js`, `POST /v1/render/slideshow`): 2–12 of this site's pictures, zoom / pan / still, fade / slide / cut, 2–5 s each, optional music (faded out), captions drawn by the page (`capPNG83`, so Arabic joins) — ffmpeg on our server, free, one at a time per person. Only `/media/…` files.
- Connection limits (ChatGPT / Claude MCP and API keys, `lib/tokens.js`): each connection has `access` (`full` or `read` — read-only can look at the library but `lib/auth.js` refuses every non-GET and `lib/mcp.js` refuses the making/posting tools), `dailyCap` (credits per UTC day, default 500; `tokenCheck` before the hold → 402, `tokenSpend` after the job is made) and `approvePosts` (default on: a post from a connection is saved `status:"awaiting"` and only goes out after the owner presses Approve — `POST /v1/posts/:id/approve|reject`; the scheduler only runs `scheduled`). Rules are changed with `PUT /v1/tokens/:id`, only by the owner signed in on the site (a connection can't raise its own limit). Existing connections get the defaults. Every credit charged while a connection's request runs counts too (`requireUser` runs it inside `tokenCtx`, an AsyncLocalStorage; `billing.charge()` calls `tokenCharged`), and once the limit is used up `requireUser` refuses its non-GET requests (it can only look until tomorrow, UTC). A recipe a connection started keeps its rules: the job stores `tok` and the next steps are created as that connection (a deleted connection's next step is refused).
- Tests: `tests/v83.py` (engines, Motion Control, recipes, what the AI received, pages), `tests/v83b.py` (own text AI + comparison, backup voice, Video from pictures, safety: admin tools, cleaned words, every recipe step under the content rules), `tests/v83c.py` (connection limits + changes in the middle of a task: price changed, plan ended, credits ran out, connection deleted, picture deleted).

## v82 One clean system · Cinema Studio · the studio's craft notes
- One list of tools (`TOOLS82` in the v82 block of `public/index.html`: hub, view, icon, English, Arabic, other views of the same tool) builds the side menu, the phone menu, the tabs on each page, the page titles (`h1Sync82`: every page's H1 = the tool's name), the search and the home tools. Old pages are gone; `ALIAS82` sends old links to the one place (world/w3d/l3d → studio3d, tools → home). Phones: Home · Image · Video · Edit at the bottom and "Menu" opens the same side menu as on a computer (`menu82`).
- The logo is still (drawn once on the first visit, never beats). One icon set: no emoji on buttons; dialect flags are country codes (SA, KW…, `flag82`) like the interface languages; an icon inside a heading is letter-sized.
- Customers never see the server's settings: "Connections & APIs" (`connect`) is for the owner/admins (`STAFF82`, `staff82()`), and admin directions in a page ("… in Admin → …") become "This tool is being set up" (`custCalm82`); the server's "not connected yet" error says the same to customers.
- Company details live on the server (`lib/settings.js` `biz()`/`saveBiz`, Admin → Settings, `PUT /v1/admin/settings {biz}`, `/v1/config.biz`, `COMPANY_*` env as defaults): policies and invoices (`lib/invoices.js seller()`) use them; Arabic pages name the country/city in Arabic (`PLACE82`).
- One generation path (`lib/jobs.js`): `checkJob` refuses what can't work before anything is charged (empty prompt, missing face/words, a file that is gone, no public address); the same request twice within a short time returns the job already made (`dupJob`); a video is priced and made for the length the engine really makes (`kieDur`, `meta.durAsked`); credits are held (`hold`) → charged when it is made (`settle`) → given back by themselves when it fails (`release`, once). The page also locks a Generate button for a moment after a tap (`GEN82`). Formats the engine can't make (21:9 on Kling/MiniMax/Veo) go to the nearest one it accepts.
- One list of models (`lib/models.js` `REGISTRY`: id → name, kind, maker) → the price table and `/v1/config.registry`.
- Cinema Studio (Video → second tab, view `cinema`): camera, lens, focal length, aperture, move and light as tiles with small drawings, six ready looks (`CINLOOK82`), "What the AI receives" shows the exact wording (`cinPrompt82`, fixed tested phrases per choice); video (any video engine, 5/8/10 s) or a still; jobs carry `meta.cinema`.
- The studio's craft notes (`lib/skills/*.md` → `lib/skills.js`): cinematography (Lumi), story (Quill), directing (Max), sound + dubbing (Echo), art & style (Nova) — written for nooi, English + Arabic. Seeded into the agents' cards (`builtin`, never overwritten by web learning, counts kept); `skillsFor` gives each agent its 3 best learned cards + its 2 best-fitting notes; the dubbing translator reads the dubbing notes (`craftNotes`). Film crew page shows them ("nooi craft notes"). Format of a file: `# Craft`, `agent:`, `tags:`, then `## Title` cards with `ar:`, `how:`, `how_ar:`, `when:`, optional `tags:`.
- Clean empty states: nothing made yet → no progress steps (`pipeHTML` → ""), on a phone the form comes first (`.two82`); one "Clone a voice" per screen; the example story, its character and storyboard switch to Arabic for an Arabic visitor until they change them (`demoSwap82`).
- Tests: `tests/v82.py` (server: check → hold → charge / give back, one job per request, lengths and formats, registry, craft notes; every page × en/ar × phone/computer: one title = the tool's name, no oversized icons, no emoji, no made-up steps, still logo, one icon per name and one name per door, customers see no server settings; Cinema Studio; company details; picture comparison against `tests/visual82.json` — after a wanted visual change run `UPDATE_VISUAL=1 python3 tests/v82.py`).

## v81 Calm & clear · the avatar's own voice · preview before going live
- Engine ElevenLabs voices are chosen by voice ID (`providers/kie.js`: `elVoiceId`, `EL_IDS` name→ID, `KIE_VOICE_DEFAULT` = the engine's own default). A bare name ("Sarah") made every avatar fail with "Internal Error". `language_code` only on turbo/flash and only a two-letter code. `lipVoice` tries the avatar's voice on multilingual v2, then turbo 2.5, then the default voice; all fail → `userMsg` (one friendly sentence; `fail()` puts it in `job.error`), the technical list stays in the admin detail.
- The avatar's voice fits the face: a ready avatar (`M.hfRef.av`) puts its `gender` in the lipsync job's meta. `createJob` refuses a lipsync whose words are the "Bring this picture to life…" motion line (400); "Try again" on such an old job opens the avatar box asking for the words.
- Failures: `errText81(j)` = the server sentence in the page's language (known `USER_MSG`s → `ERR_MSG`), else its first sentence without the technical tail (also `failMsg79`). The notifier shows it once with "Try again"; toasts are ≤ 3 lines, full width on phones, with × and — when there is an admin detail — a "Details" fold (`opt.detail`; an inline "\nAdmin detail: …" is folded too). Never put provider detail in the toast text.
- Things being made: `drawItem` draws a calm moving colour field (`calmFrame81`) for anything not complete — never the old blocky low-res picture. The big preview shows `.sveil81` (stars + % + usual time), no pause button, no caption while making; steps = Request received · Creating with <model> % · Checked and saved (no fake "denoising" counts). Captions are one line on phones.
- Crew cards: "New" pill inside the card (`.newtag81`), solid borders, whole-word roles (`fitWords81`), equal heights, all 8 in one row ≥ 1000 px; genre tiles have their own icons (`OIP["crew.brief.genre|…"]`). Hub tabs are one sideways-scrolling row on phones.
- 3D Studio settings (`loadCfg` in `public/studio3d/app.js`) carry the photo: wait up to 30 s and ask twice before falling back to offline (8 s was too short when the studio is rebuilt or the phone is slow → "Not available on your account").
- Preview before going live: `deploy/stage.sh <zip>` runs the package as `nooi-stage` (port 8090, https :8443 with the live certificate) on a copy of `data/`, sharing `media/`, `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` = the live address (engines read uploads there). `NOOI_STAGE=1`: no payments (`enabledPayments()` empty), no emails, no social posts (scheduler off), yellow "Preview version" ribbon (`/v1/config.stage`). `stage.sh promote` = `nooi-update` with the same package; `stage.sh stop` removes it. Dry-run test: `tests/stage_sh.py`.

## v80 Hubs · photoreal 3D worlds · stars-only loaders
- Menu = hubs (`HUBS`): Image (create · Sketch to life [Live sketch + Draw a scene, `.skmode80`] · coloring), Video (create · UGC ads · nooi Studio = `crew` · story · storyboard), Characters, 3D (3D worlds `w3ai` · 3D Studio), Edit (editor · effects · voice · subtitles · enhance · video intelligence), Publish, Settings. Each hub page starts with `.hubtabs` (injected after render); the side menu marks the hub. `ALLNAV` keeps every tool (names, search, tools page); `NAVS` holds only hubs. The Video page has no "UGC" type (that is the UGC ads tab). Add new tools to a hub, not to `NAVS`.
- nooi Auto is its own card ("nooi Auto", nooi's own description) — never "nooi Auto · <engine>".
- Loaders: `--star79` is three stars each with its own SMIL scale/rotate/opacity rhythm; no CSS transform on the whole. Rendering/queued veils (`.veil.v80`) and badges show only the stars (+ % for rendering) — no "Rendering"/"Queued" words.
- Tap to view: every finished picture/clip thumbnail gets `.zoom80` → `viewMedia80` (full picture, clip with controls, download, Esc/× to close); autoplay preview clips pause on tap. Grids are compact on phones (looks 3 per row, templates ≤ 200 px tall).
- Sketch to Life speed: recognised while drawing (pointerup + 450 ms, de-duplicated per drawing), pause 850 ms, 768 px JPEG up, `meta.res:"1K"`, followed every second (client) and polled every ~1 s on the server (`meta.skLive`).
- 3D worlds (`w3ai`, `lib/depthworld.js`): "Photoreal world" (default) — Kie picture (16:9 "walk into it" or 21:9 "360°") → depth from `/v1/world/depth` (Admin → AI providers → Depth / `DEPTH_API_URL`, else fal.ai Depth Anything v2 with `FAL_KEY`) or, without one, an image-edit job that draws the depth map → `dwBuildMesh`: one displaced grid / sphere (inverse-depth mapping, sky pushed far), torn where depth jumps (ratio > 1.28), a blurred copy of the picture behind; drag to look, pinch/wheel/keys to walk inside limits, Tour, cloud video from the current view. "Shape world" = the v79 scene-language designer.
- 3D Studio on phones: the frame grows to the studio's height (`x3d-size`) and the studio sizes its 3D view to half the real screen (`x3d-vp` → `--vph`), so the page scrolls past it.
- Avatars (`lib/showcase.js`, `AV_STYLE = 2`): 15 fictional people, smiling head-and-shoulders on a soft pastel-pink background, 1:1, made for a circle; avatars made in the old style are made again by Admin → Showcase → Make avatars. UGC presenters show them in pink circles (initials until made).

## v79 Free period · fixes from the field · the sketch becomes what you drew · AI 3D world (cloud render)
- Free period: `lib/billing.js freeCredits()` — Admin → Features → "Free period" (key `freePeriod`, default on; `FREE_PERIOD=false` in .env when unset charges — not `FREE_CREDITS`, which is the welcome-credit amount). In the free period `charge()` takes nothing (ledger row `delta:0, free:n`) and `credit("Refund…")` gives nothing back unless a real charge with that `ref` exists. `/v1/config.freeCredits` + `/v1/billing.free` → client `crFree()`; every client credit check is `!crFree()&&…`. Prices, cost badges and the credit icon stay. The test runner exports `FREE_PERIOD=false` (the suite checks charging); `tests/v79_fixes.py` checks the free period.
- Avatar voice (`providers/kie.js lipVoice`): the site's ElevenLabs key first, else the engine's ElevenLabs voices — multilingual v2 twice, then turbo 2.5, 2.5 s apart; all busy → one clear "voice service is busy" error with `noFallback` (other avatar models would need the same voice). `KIE_FALLBACK.lipsync` = kling avatar standard → infinitalk → kling avatar pro (face photos only).
- Failed jobs: `pipeHTML` shows the reason (+ admin detail) and "Try again" (`data-act="job-retry"` → `retry79`, replaces the failed job); every failed `j:` thumb gets a `veil fail79` with Try again. `.stage .cap` is dark glass (the generic `.cap` rule made it a white box).
- Labels never break inside a word: `.tile .lb` (+ `.mcard .mtx`) no `overflow-wrap:anywhere`; `fitLabels79` shrinks the font (≥ 8.5 px, slight negative tracking, a little extra width) until the longest word fits, after every render and on resize.
- Loaders: `--star79` (animated SVG of the nooi stars, built from `STAR_D`) replaces every spinner (`.pipe .now`, `.btn.busy`, `.mchip.live`, `.mcard.live`, `.wview.loading`, `.hfspin`, rendering veils). Use `<i class="star79"></i>` for new loaders — never a CSS ring spinner.
- Character looks: the engine gets `charPrompt(c,"en")` ("Portrait of …", or a reference sheet), not just the name; the first finished real look becomes the character's look once (`c.autoLook79`), then the drawn previews hide.
- Sketch: on the live site "When I pause" is the default (`S.sketch.v79` migration). `POST /v1/sketch/see` (vision AI, free) reads the drawing when nothing is typed ("a red sports car next to a coiled snake"); the picture's result is drawn INTO the output panel `#skout` (`SKAI`, overlay `#skai79`) until the drawing changes. "Make it real" and Draw & create use the same recognition. The avatar tab clears the "Bring this picture to life…" motion prompt (an avatar speaks the typed script).
- AI 3D world (view `w3ai`, `lib/worldai.js`): `POST /v1/world/design` — the text AI writes a scene in a fixed scene language (sky, fog, ground, light, camera move, ≤ 80 objects from `TYPES` with count/spread scatter, ≤ 600 things); `cleanScene` whitelists and clamps everything (nothing the AI writes is run as code). The client builds it from shared simple shapes (instanced when scattered) with three.js r128 from `/vendor/three`, pixel ratio 1 on phones, renders only when the view changes. No text AI → `w3Local` keyword designer. "Real photo / video" send ONE snapshot to the cloud engines (image edit with `iRef`, image→video with `vStart`).
- 3D Studio cloud mode (`S.lite`, phones / ≤ 4 GB / no WebGPU, switch "Cloud mode" in "This device & the AI", remembered as `x3lite`): no SAM/depth download, the asset step uses the cloud engine directly (`makeCloud`: a cut-out card stands in), capture = "AI video / photo in the cloud" (`aiRender` → app op `X3OPS.airender`).
- UGC ads is its own page (`VIEWS.ugcads`, `S.view="ugcads"`, state `S.ugc79` + `S.draft.ugc`): 1 product (photos, name, price, what's special) · 2 how to market it · 3 who presents it · 4 the ad (what they say, CTA, where it runs → aspect, length, engine) → result beside it. With a product photo: presenter = 2 steps (image job with `iRef` = the photo → when done a video job with `vStart` = that picture, `meta.ugcStep` 1→2); product / motion start from the photo; still ad edits it. The Video page no longer embeds the marketing box (`ugcPanel` → a link to UGC ads) and never rewrites its prompt for ugc/ad/product types; the home "Product ad" task opens UGC ads.
- Templates page: each template shows its own preview (`/v1/tplprev`, made by staff from the template's prompt: Admin → AI providers → Showcase → "Make the template previews"), else a showcase item of the same kind, else a clean cover with its kind (`.frame.tplcov`) — never the logo placeholder or an unrelated clip.

## v78 One system (render review, export, localization, agents, live sketch, voice, animation, templates, pro media)
- v78a render review: `public/studio3d/review.js` (on-device checks: ground, real size, fits, geometry, loose parts, wheels, lit, back ≠ front via texture-space band-pass correlation + rendered views; fixes; `contactSheet` → AI). Vehicles get a painted rear (`paintRear` in localInference.js: rear window, tail lights, plate, bumper) — never the front image. Plan step "review". Server `POST /v1/vision/review` (3D or `mode:"video"`, 1 cr) in lib/vss.js.
- Editor: "Export video" button (`data-act="ed-out"`) → sheet (MP4, ProRes 422 HQ .mov, DNxHR HQ .mxf, H.265 10-bit, WebM, GIF, WAV 24-bit; fps; checks). `/v1/media/transcode?fmt=prores|dnxhr|hevc|gif|wav&fps=` and `/v1/media/probe` (ffprobe). After each export `vrCheck` samples the master recording (duration, aspect, black/frozen frames, exposure, loudness/clipping, text fit) + optional AI frame review; `vrFix` fixes (sound, text size, black edges) → "Fix & export again". Recording avoids MP4 when the browser can't play H.264.
- `.nst{direction:ltr}` keeps "nooi Studio 2.0" in order in RTL.
- v78b content localization (NVIDIA Content Localization blueprint): `lib/localize.js` — `/v1/localize` (+ `/quote`, `/transcribe`, `/:id`), records in `u.loc`. Pipeline: intake (ffprobe) → transcript with speakers (ElevenLabs Scribe `scribe_v2`→`v1`, Whisper, CAMB.AI, or the user's lines) → translation for the ear with a time budget per line + glossary + speaker genders → voices → lip-sync → mix → SRT/VTT → QA (file, timing, back-translation meaning score) → library job kind "dub". Engines: `nooi` (lib/voice.js natural voices, fitted with atempo ≤1.3, voice-over ducking via sidechaincompress), `elevenlabs` (POST /v1/dubbing → poll → audio/{lang} + transcript srt), `camb` (upload-url → PUT → complete → /dub → /dub-alt-format), `nvidia` (gRPC controller, protos copied in `proto/content-localization` with the Apache-2.0 license; admin `nvloc`: address, token, mode elevenlabs|camb|lipsync — lipsync = nooi voices sent as translated_audio_data with bypass_s2s). "Best value" = `quote()`: lips → NVIDIA, own voices → cheapest dubbing service, else cheapest. Own voices with nooi need consent → one ElevenLabs instant clone per speaker, deleted after. Prices per minute in `ENGINE_COST.loc` (admin Pricing). Remote sources must be public hosts (`publicHost`). Tests: `tests/localize.py` (mock ElevenLabs/CAMB/LLM + a real gRPC mock controller).
- `lib/voice.js` natural voice: `speak` (ElevenLabs ids with previous/next text, v3 tags, delivery per emotion; else the AI engine's voices by name), `castVoices` (one steady voice per speaker), `writeForEar`, `polish` (EQ, de-ess, compression, room, −16 LUFS), `cloneVoice`/`dropVoice`. Tool route `POST /v1/voice/natural`.
- Video intelligence hears the speech (`audio:true` → transcript per part, in captions, Q&A and search); "Translate & dub" on a result; dubbed videos → editor (clip + subtitles), publish, analyse.
- v78c the agents (`lib/agents.js`; 9 since v91 added Lyra): skill cards learned from trusted web pages per agent (admin `PUT /v1/agents/sources`, `POST /v1/agents/learn`, weekly auto; https + public host on every redirect hop; page text is data, never instructions; `AGENTS_ALLOW_HOSTS` only for tests) and from results (`learnHooks` in lib/learn.js: approve → wins, again/remove/fail → losses; cards ranked by (wins+1)/(wins+losses+2)). `POST /v1/agents/plan` (brief → each agent's decision with its best cards → Iris fixes → engine prompt, negative, bible, shots; 1 cr video, free image) and `POST /v1/agents/storyboard` (scenes with picture prompts + characters; 2 cr). Client: `AG` strip at the bottom of the home box — typing shows Quill, sending shows the agents one at a time (plan notes; Duke's models/credits from `hfStudio`; Iris watches the results). Jobs carry the plan prompt + `meta.skills`. Storyboard option (`agx-sb`): scenes → board frames + characters → a picture per scene on the best-value image model (`agImgModel`; first picture is the reference for the rest via `job.inputsUrl`) → board tools: pictures, "Make the video from the storyboard" (`meta.partImages` → each part starts from its picture, no chaining), natural voiceover. Film crew page: "What the agents learned" (+ admin sources/learn). Test: `tests/agents.py`.
- v78d layer animator (index.html v78d block): text layers + infographics get `ap` (in: fade/fly L R U D/zoom/spin/bounce/drop), `lp` (while on: float/pulse/sway/shake/slow zoom), `xp` (out), `rot`, `op`, `kf` keyframes `{t,x,y,k,r,o,e}` (auto-key: dragging an item that has keys at the playhead adds a key). Opacity multiplies the drawing code's own `globalAlpha` (`laAlpha` overrides the context's accessor while drawing). `laState(it,lt,len,base)` is the one source of truth.
- Templates: 24 infographic types (12 new: kinetic, quote, countdown, timeline, compare, price, cta, social, pin, rating, iconrow, endcard). Every type fits its words: `fitF` (one line, never overflows), `fitWrap` (≤ n lines), `wrapText` breaks long words and scripts without spaces (Intl.Segmenter words → graphemes; `kinsoku` keeps 、。」 off line starts), `keep()` holds the card inside the frame (except while the animator moves it). `tests/motion_templates.py` renders all 24 × 6 languages × 3 aspects × 5 positions × default/long words and checks every word is inside the frame and its card. Default words in other UI languages come from `/v1/ui-t/:lang` (structure like `2019:` / `spark:` kept).
- AI motion graphics: `lib/motion.js` `POST /v1/motion/code` (2 cr, refunded on failure; one retry with the rejection reason) → `function draw(ctx,t,W,H)`; checks: shape, size, syntax (`vm.Script` compile only — never run on the server), deny-list on code with strings blanked (network, eval/Function/constructor, page, storage, timers). Client `mgRun` runs it in a sandboxed iframe (opaque origin, CSP default-src 'none') and records WebM; `mgGuard` adds a step counter to every loop condition (2e6 per frame). 6 ready presets (`MG_PRESETS`) + 12 brief "ideas" (`MG_IDEAS`, patterns from the MIT "awesome-opus5-5-videos" list: showreel, logo, product ad, 5-scene explainer, kinetic type, data story, step-by-step, app demo, map, launch, 3D orbit, beat) that fill the prompt with the project title and a length (5–20 s). Agents: `agAssemble` puts a storyboard in the editor (pictures/videos, animated title, names, captions, end card, voiceover).
- v78d natural voice & dialogue: Voice page tool (`#ndlg`): "Name: (feeling) line" scripts → `GET/POST /v1/voice/natural` (voices, feelings, price; one steady voice per speaker, picked voices honoured, write-for-ear, v3 tags from (laughs), room). The editor's AI voice-over uses it when "Natural delivery" is on (shown only when the server has a voice engine; placed at the playhead via `VOP`). `ndMake` adds the job at once (rendering → complete/failed).
- Live AI sketch: `lib/sketch.js` — `GET/POST /v1/realtime/sketch`; engine = Admin "realtime" (custom URL) or "fal" (fal.ai, `FAL_SKETCH_MODEL` default fal-ai/fast-lightning-sdxl/image-to-image, sync_mode); 1 credit per `REALTIME_FRAMES_PER_CREDIT` (4) frames, charged before the frame and refunded if it fails, 402 when out. Client modes `S.sketch.ai`: off (free preview, never calls the model) · live · pause (one picture per pause with the cheapest sketch-faithful model `lskPauseModel`). Spending limit `S.sketch.aiCap`, camera/screen input (`lskInput`), session recording (copies #skout into its own canvas), keep a frame.
- v78e pro media in: `lib/ingest.js` `POST /v1/media/ingest` (multipart, q=fast|full) + `GET /v1/media/ingest/:id` (async, progress). ffprobe facts (`describe`: codec, profile, bits per channel, fps, interlace, SAR, colour/HDR, timecode, audio ch/sr/bits), proxies: H.264 MP4 1080p/4K (yadif, square pixels, HDR tone-map when zscale exists), PNG (EXR via -apply_trc), camera RAW → biggest embedded JPEG (`rawPreview`), HEIC via heif-convert/ImageMagick, MP3 audio. Original kept in /media. Client: capture-phase `change`/`drop` interceptors convert unplayable files before the page sees them (DataTransfer swap, re-dispatch); maker RAW video (BRAW/R3D/ARRIRAW…) explained, not uploaded. An .mp4/.mov the browser can't play is converted only when the browser plays H.264 (`PRO_H264`) — otherwise the proxy wouldn't play either. Uploads up to 2 GB (activate.sh raises nginx).
- Drip: `DRIP` device profile (GPU, max texture, memory → low/mid/high) and `S.devQ` (auto/fast/best) → proxy quality and the SPA's three.js pixel ratio (WebGLRenderer wrapper in `ensureThree`). `public/sw.js` keeps /media/ pictures, sounds, 3D files on the device (cache-first, capped, never the app/API, never range requests). Account → "This device" panel. 3D Studio engine: textures go to the GPU a few per frame (`dripTextures`, meshes appear when ready), low tier gets ≤2048 px textures, adaptive pixel ratio from the measured fps (`E.quality`, `E.setQuality`). Any render outside the live loop (review, snapshot) and record/export first calls `E.dripFlush()` so checks and captures see the whole model; a part hidden meanwhile (loose piece) stays hidden.

## v77 One 3D Studio (replaces 3D Studio/SAM 3D, 3D worlds, 3D World Studio, On-device 3D)
- `public/studio3d/` static modules: index.html (UI), app.js (controller), planner.js (prompt → steps/techniques/costs, EN+AR), segment.js (SAM taps or instant geodesic colour/depth selection; 64-bit Dijkstra keys — 32-bit loops forever), localInference.js (worker: Depth Anything, SlimSAM, MODNet; reconstruct; `solidBody` = outline walls + back + vehicle hood/raked windshield/side glass/wheels), engine.js (three r128: sky→PMREM, orbit/walk with collisions, tour, record, glb/usdz), world.js (biomes, instanced plants, exact walkable plot), fx.js (rain/mist/rays/fireflies/leaves/snow + WebAudio ambience), bridge.js.
- nooi: v77 block — single nav `studio3d` (iframe kept across re-renders; rebuilt on language change or hand-over), `world`/`w3d`/`l3d` views redirect, `X3OPS` bridge (cfg, job3d, views, pano[+photo ref], skybox, vision, jobs, fetch, save, video, still, library, libItem). `/world3d/` and `/local3d/` redirect.
- server: `POST /v1/vision/locate` (lib/vss.js, 1 cr) → box for "find the car".
- Real-size objects bigger than the plot are shrunk to leave ~0.8 m to walk, with a "Real size · area N×N" button.
- Tests: tests/studio3d.py (new), world3d_studio.py / v72_features.py / pro_tools.py / draw_world.py drive the studio frame. Recording in headless needs ≥3 s.

## v76 On-device 3D (free, no API key)
- `public/local3d/` static tool (index.html, localInference.js, sceneSetup.js, shaders.js), served at `/local3d/`, embedded in nooi as view `l3d` ("On-device 3D (free)") and linked from 3D World Studio / 3D Studio.
- AI runs in a Web Worker with transformers.js (WebGPU → WASM fallback): Depth Anything V2 small + MODNet matte; model files cached in IndexedDB (`nooi-local3d-models`). `?models=` can point to self-hosted weights.
- Reconstruction: depth + distance field → closed mesh (front relief + smooth back), WebGPU compute (`RECON_WGSL`) with identical CPU fallback; all GPU buffers destroyed after each run. PBR albedo/normal/roughness maps.
- Viewer: three.js r128 (`/vendor/three`, jsm route added in server.js, CDN fallback), OrbitControls, procedural sky → PMREM, optional user .hdr, wind + snow shaders via onBeforeCompile, falling snow, .glb export, snapshot, "Save to my nooi library" (postMessage `nooi-local3d-glb`, origin checked).
- Honest limit: depth-based reconstruction (visible side + smooth back), not a full generative 3D model. WebGPU path untested in headless CI; CPU path tested by `tests/local3d.py`.

## v75 3D World Studio + 3D engines
- NVIDIA's hosted picture models reject inline base64 ("Expected: example_id, got: base64"): `nvAsset()` uploads the picture to the NVCF asset store (`POST …/v2/nvcf/assets {contentType, description}` → `PUT uploadUrl` with `x-amz-meta-nvcf-asset-description`) and the request uses `data:<type>;asset_id,<id>` + header `NVCF-INPUT-ASSET-REFERENCES` (TRELLIS, FLUX Kontext, Cosmos). `NVIDIA_ASSETS_URL` overrides the store URL. Kontext keeps the requested aspect for 360° jobs.
- `/v1/config.providers.sam3d` no longer turns true because of NVIDIA (that sent full-scene jobs to the GPU gate → "3D generation is not available yet"). 3D Studio's main button: objects / people → TRELLIS (or Tripo3D / Meshy) job; scene → on-device builder.
- `providers/gen3d.js`: Tripo3D (`/upload` multipart → `image_token`, `/task` image_to_model | text_to_model, poll `output.pbr_model|model`), Meshy (`/v1/image-to-3d` with a data URI, `/v2/text-to-3d` preview, poll `model_urls.glb`), Blockade Labs Skybox (`/skybox`, poll `/imagine/requests/{id}` → `file_url` + `depth_map_url` → job `segments[0]`). Keys: Admin → AI providers (`tripo`, `meshy`, `blockade`; env `TRIPO_API_KEY/BASE_URL`, `MESHY_API_KEY/BASE_URL`, `BLOCKADE_API_KEY/BASE_URL/STYLE_ID`). `gen3dRoute` in `adapterFor` (after NVIDIA): explicit `meta.engine`, else the first connected; failed NVIDIA 3D jobs fall back here. 3D jobs on these engines pass the GPU gate. `/v1/config.providers.gen3d`.
- 3D worlds: the AI-world button works without World Labs (Skybox if connected, else the 360° picture world); unconnected engine chips hidden; non-panorama pictures become a blurred surround with the sharp picture in front (no mirrored tiles); Skybox depth maps shape the sphere (`pwBuild` with `PW.depth`). Client job polls keep `segments`.
- 3D World Studio (`/world3d/` → `/?view=w3d`, menu "3D World Studio"): `public/world3d/app.js` + `styles.css` loaded on demand by `VIEWS.w3d` (`w3Load`); three.js r128 (OrbitControls, TransformControls, GLTFLoader/Exporter), ACES tone mapping, PMREM studio light box or the world panorama as sky + image lighting, soft shadows (desktop), pixel ratio ≤ 1.5 on phones, ResizeObserver, pause when hidden. Prompt bar (object | 360° world, attach a picture, engine chips: Auto / NVIDIA TRELLIS / Tripo3D / Meshy / nooi AI views; Auto / Skybox / nooi 360°), live progress chips + spinning placeholder per pending model, models fitted to 1.6 m on the ground and auto-selected, tap to select, move / rotate / scale gizmo, scene list, library re-add, scene .glb export, 8 s orbit → editor, view → video first frame. State `S.w3d` (filled in place — never replaced).
- Test: `tests/world3d_studio.py`; `tests/nvidia_e2e.mjs` checks the asset upload.

## v74 NVIDIA AI across the studio
- `providers/nvidia.js` (key "nvapi-…" in Admin → AI providers → NVIDIA AI, `cfg("nvidia")`, env `NVIDIA_API_KEY/NVIDIA_BASE_URL/NVIDIA_VLM_MODEL/NVIDIA_TEXT_MODEL/NVIDIA_STATUS_URL`): `nvChat` (OpenAI-format chat; reasoning models get `reasoning_budget`, `<think>` stripped), `nvAdapter(task)` for image (FLUX.1-dev `{prompt,mode,cfg_scale,width,height,seed,steps}` / SD 3.5 `{prompt,aspect_ratio,…}` / SDXL), edit (FLUX.1 Kontext, picture inline as data URL), 3d (TRELLIS → .glb), video (Cosmos → .mp4). Answers: JSON with base64 (`artifacts[0].base64`, `image`, `b64_video`, …) or binary / zip; 202 + `NVCF-REQID` → poll the NVCF status URL (job `remoteId`). Every URL editable (`imageUrl`, `image2Url`, `editUrl`, `trellisUrl`, `videoUrl`), and `use` switches capabilities (`text:on vision:on image:on edit:on 3d:on video:off` by default — Cosmos is for physical-world clips).
- Routing (`nvRoute` in `adapterFor`): explicit NVIDIA models (`nvflux`, `nvsd35`, `nvkontext`, `nvcosmos`) always; "nooi Auto" when the capability is on (the browser's `pickImage`/`pickVideo` pick the NVIDIA model then). Jobs on `nv@…` that fail are re-run once on the site's other engine (`fallback` in lib/jobs.js, `noNv`). 3D jobs with TRELLIS skip the GPU_FEATURES gate (not "scene" mode).
- Text AI: `llm()` uses NVIDIA when `text`/`vision` is on (stories, storyboards, bots, captions, prompt tools, vision checks, video intelligence), falling back to Claude/Qwen; `llmConfigured()` counts NVIDIA. `/v1/config.providers.nvidia/nvUse`.
- Client: NVIDIA models in the picture / video pickers (logo `LOGO_SRC.nvidia`), "Powered by NVIDIA AI" badge on pages whose AI runs on NVIDIA, AI full 3D uses one TRELLIS job (`a3Start` wrapper; the 3-view builder is the fallback).
- Voice, music, sound effects and lip-sync stay on the other engines (NVIDIA's speech models are gRPC-only, no REST to call).
- Test: `tests/nvidia_e2e.mjs` (mock NVIDIA catalog + mock Kie + real server).

## v73 Video intelligence (the NVIDIA VSS blueprint workflows, built into nooi)
- NVIDIA's "Video Search and Summarization" blueprint needs NVIDIA GPUs + NIM containers (VLM, LLM, Elasticsearch, Kafka) — it can't run on the nooi VPS. Its workflows are rebuilt with ffmpeg + the vision text AI (`providers/anthropic.js`, images), and an optional connector uses a real VSS server.
- `lib/vss.js` (`registerVss`): `POST /v1/vss {src (/media URL or http), job?, title, chunk (0 = auto: 5/10/30/60 s, ≤ 40 parts), scenario, events, objects, prompt, lang}` → record in `u.vss` (≤ 60), analysed in the background: per part a 2×2 contact sheet of 4 frames (ffmpeg `fps,scale,tile`) → vision caption JSON {caption, objects, tags, events[{name,present,confidence,detail}]} → aggregation {title, summary, highlights, timeline, alerts, report}. `GET /v1/vss`, `GET|DELETE /v1/vss/:id`, `POST /v1/vss/:id/ask {q}` (keyword-retrieved captions → answer + moments), `POST /v1/vss/search {q}` (all parts of all videos, keyword pool → AI rerank by meaning), `POST /v1/vss/verify {src,s,e,alert}` or `{frame (data URL), alert}` → {verdict confirmed|rejected|unsure, confidence, reason, seen}.
- Vision AI order: Claude/Qwen (text AI) when connected, otherwise **NVIDIA AI** (Admin → AI providers → NVIDIA AI, `cfg("nvidia")`: `apiKey` nvapi-…, `baseUrl` default https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1 → `/chat/completions` with `model` for pictures (default nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning — reasoning models get `reasoning_budget` 2048 + room for the answer; `<think>` / `reasoning_content` stripped) and `textModel` for summaries, questions and search (default nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b); or one model's URL like https://ai.api.nvidia.com/v1/vlm/google/paligemma, posted as is without a model id; env `NVIDIA_API_KEY/NVIDIA_BASE_URL/NVIDIA_VLM_MODEL/NVIDIA_TEXT_MODEL`). Pictures inline as `image_url` data URLs, shrunk under NVIDIA's ~180 KB limit. Plain-text VLM answers become captions; with no text model the summary is built from the captions; verdicts read yes/no.
- Credits `VSS_PRICE`: 1 per part, 2 summary, 1 per question / search / alert check / live check; staff free.
- Connector: Admin → AI providers → "NVIDIA VSS server" (`cfg("vss")`: `baseUrl` e.g. http://host:38111, `apiKey` bearer, `model`; env `VSS_API_URL/KEY/MODEL`). When set, new analyses go to `POST /v1/summarize {url, prompt, chunk_duration, scenario, events, objects_of_interest, enable_qa:true}` and questions to `POST /v1/chat/completions {id: video_id, messages}`; the provider test probes `/v1/ready`. `/v1/config.providers.vss`.
- Page "Video intelligence" (`VIEWS.vss`, Edit menu): Summarize & ask (upload or library video, part length, scenario, event chips, focus; summary, key moments that seek the player, alerts with Clip → editor in/out, timeline, detailed captions, Markdown report, chat with timestamps), Search (moments across all videos → Open / To editor), Alerts (live checks of camera or a playing video every 5–60 s with a beep + log, stops when leaving the page; alert verification of a clip).
- Test: `tests/vss_e2e.py` (mock vision AI + mock VSS server, phone + desktop).

## v72 Steady editor preview, AI full 3D, 3D world from a photo, AI effects
- Editor: `#main` uses `scroll-behavior:auto; overflow-anchor:none` (smooth scrolling animated every re-render from the top, and scroll anchoring nudged the page ~10 px per tap). On phones the tools panel is moved inside the preview's panel after each render (`.edtools72`) so the sticky `.edstage` stays pinned to the end of the page; the tab bar is one scrolling row stuck under it (`--edsh`).
- Timeline: overlapping sound effects, layers and blur boxes get their own lanes (`relane72`). Logo size is rounded and clamped 4–40 (pinch used to store raw floats like 5.3962965866); picking a corner clears a dragged `S.brand.x/y`.
- `serverSubmit` reads every file slot synchronously before the first upload (slots deleted right after `addJob` were lost). `SLOTS_FOR.image` again includes `skImg`/UGC slots (a v65 line had overwritten them — Live Sketch HD sent no sketch). New slot `chRef` (character face) for `look`/`sheet` jobs → `refImg` in `providers/kie.js` + generic image provider. `withRef72(slot,file,fn)` runs a job with a temporary reference and restores the user's own.
- Draw & create and Live Sketch "Make it real": sketch → strongest picture model (`bestImg72`: Nano Banana Pro on Pro/Studio, else Nano Banana) with a photoreal prompt (`sketchPrompt72`); results show under the live preview (`meta.skLive`).
- Characters: "Create a character from a photo" (new or current character). Photo: 320 px in `c.photo` (thumbnails `cp:`/`cb:`), 1024 px in IndexedDB `chph_<id>`. With the text AI, a vision call fills name, gender, age, hair/eye colour, look, outfit. Looks/sheet send the photo as `chRef`.
- AI full 3D (`S.a3` pipelines): 3 picture jobs (front, right side, back on white, `meta.a3`/`view`) with the photo as reference → `a3Mesh`: background flood-fill masks, per-row thickness from the side silhouette, circular profile from the distance field, front + mirrored back textures → closed 2-mesh model → local `3d` job (GLB in IndexedDB `x_<id>`, uploaded on a server). 3D Studio card ("Make full 3D with AI", 3 × picture price); Characters "Full 3D character (AI)" uses the photo or the chosen look. Viewer buttons: Send to editor (6 s turntable → `video` job → `edUseJob`), Use in a scene (snapshot → `vStart`).
- 3D world from a photo (`S.wld.pw`, `PW`): a 21:9 "seamless 360° equirectangular panorama" picture job continuing the photo → three.js sphere with ground-projected lower half (walkable within 6 m), drag/pinch/wheel/arrows; .glb, 8 s walk-through → editor, view → scene. Without a result image it mirrors the photo into a 2:1 panorama.
- AI effects: Effects tab "Describe an effect → Generate": `/v1/llm/json` designs 1–3 particle layers (shape, colours, count, size, speed, direction, glow, blend, tint); without the text AI a keyword parser (EN/AR colours, shapes, directions, emoji). Saved in `S.vfxLib`, registered in `VFX` as `c_<id>` and drawn by `vfxDrawLayer`. The prompt bar routes "effect / sparkles / bubbles…" words here too.
- Templates (Explore → All): one grid ordered by kind, equal cards (padding fixed, placeholder fills the frame).
- Test: `tests/v72_features.py` (own server + mocked picture engine returning front/side/back/panorama PNGs; phone + desktop).

Each has a studio model id (routed to Kie), aspect, category, and `slot` 0–5 for the six demo tiles.

A 4 s watcher turns finished jobs into records (`col("showcase")`) and posts each to Explore as featured, author "nooi", with its prompt (`showcase` field). `DELETE /v1/admin/showcase {ids}` removes records + posts. Per-row remake (`sc-one`), "Retry failed", live status.

## Slash commands (in .claude/commands)
`/test` full check · `/audit-i18n` translations & RTL · `/deploy root@IP` update the VPS · `/connect-provider Kling` wire & verify a real provider.

## Status
Done & tested in browser: studio UI (video/image/characters/story/storyboard/editor/subtitles/dubbing UI/hooks/content plan/scheduling/coloring book/live sketch/floor plans → 3D (three.js)/motion tracking/fonts/UGC ads/admin dashboard/support/policies/dark-light), server logic unit-tested (billing, coupons, RBAC, encryption, MCP protocol, routing).

NOT yet verified against real services (no internet in the original build environment) — verify each in sandbox with the provider's current docs:
1. WAN 3.0 request/response mapping (`providers/index.js → wanBody`).
2. Seedance, Kling, DashScope adapters (`providers/extra.js`) — endpoints, model ids, Seedance text flags.
3. Payments: Moyasar/Tap/Stripe/PayPal/Airwallex create + verify + webhooks (idempotency already in place).
4. Firebase sign-in (required before inviting clients — without it the server runs as one local user).
5. Email (Resend/SendGrid) + SPF/DKIM for contact@nooi.ai.
6. Social OAuth apps & publishing.
7. Google Fonts loading and the real 3D floor-plan render on devices.

## Suggested order of work
1. Deploy: `bash deploy/install.sh <domain> <email>` on the VPS (Ubuntu 24.04). Check `journalctl -u nooi -f`.
2. Firebase auth → test sign-in end to end.
3. Text AI key (Claude or Qwen) → test story/storyboard/translation.
4. First video provider (WAN 3.0 or Kling) → real render end to end, then the rest.
5. Payments in sandbox → go live after the payment company approves.
6. Email, social publishing, backups off-site (rclone).
7. Refactor frontend into modules; add automated tests to CI (tests/*.py with Playwright).

## Rules
- Never commit `.env`, `data/`, `media/`. Keep secrets out of logs and chat.
- Keep Arabic/RTL correct: timelines & toolbars that represent time stay `dir="ltr"`; everything else follows the UI language.
- Mobile first: test at 360/390 px, iPad 768/1180, desktop 1440 (`tests/allviews.py`). No horizontal page overflow.
- After UI changes run `npm test` (= `bash tests/run_all.sh`): syntax, click every control (`[]` errors), layout on 4 devices, RTL in ar/fa/ckb, untranslated text, tracking accuracy.
- Never declare a top-level `function` with an existing name — a later declaration silently replaces the earlier one (this broke the audio lab once). `npm test` fails on duplicates. Prefix new helpers (e.g. `crewNormalize`, `alDrawWave`).
- Never compute `L()` at load time (the language may change) — build strings inside functions.
- Setup for tests: `pip install -r tests/requirements.txt && python3 -m playwright install chromium`.
