## v99 The editor's preview never blinks
- Why it flickered: each time a clip's video moved to another moment (dragging the playhead, the next clip starting, sliders that move the playhead, the paused preview catching up, a playing clip re-synced) the "Loading…" wrapper of `drawFit` painted a grey screen until the frame was ready (`readyState < 2` while seeking) — about half the frames while dragging (112 in 2 s in the test before the fix), more on phones / with network videos.
- Fix (block `v99: THE EDITOR'S PREVIEW NEVER BLINKS`): `keep99(m, main)` keeps a ≤960 px copy of the clip's last good frame (`m._k`; one clip copy at a time; while playing ≤ ~11 copies/s); the outermost `drawFit` wrapper draws that copy (as an image, same media object so motion/zoom/effects still apply) while the video catches up, up to `HOLD99` (1.5 s) — then "Loading…" as before. A clip with no frame yet uses the previous clip's (`ED.k99`). Video layers (`edxDrawLayers`) hold their last frame too. Export gets the held frame instead of "Loading…" in the video.
- Calmer jumps (`syncClipMedia` base + the layer sync): paused, a moment already asked for isn't asked again (`m._lt`; some browsers land a hair off it, which re-seeked every frame); while dragging a new jump waits up to 0.25 s for the running one (`m._st`).
- Test: `tests/v99.py` (port 8295, a VP9 clip — the test browser has no H.264).

## v100 A picture with no words still makes a video
- The error: `engine@kling-3.0/video: Error: AI engine 422: prompt is required`. `checkJob` accepts a video (or picture) with a start / reference picture and nothing typed, but every words step skipped an empty prompt (v88 English + no on-screen text, v96 intent lines), so the engine got no `prompt` (`drop()` removes empty fields) and Kling 3.0 refused it.
- `lib/words100.js` (imported in server.js after intent.js, but `jobHooks.before.unshift` → runs first): video / chapter / image with a picture (`hasPicture`: any input file, a ref with a url, `chainFrom`, `source`) and no words → `PICTURE_WORDS` (`lib/picturewords.js`, no imports) + `meta.pictureWords`; jobs that write their own prompt (sketch, Motion Control, Extend shot, editor commands, storyboard shots) are skipped. The v88 / v96 steps then add the no-text line and the page's style etc.
- Safety net in `kieInput` (providers/kie.js): a video with a start picture and an empty prompt, or a picture with a reference and an empty prompt, gets the same words.
- Test: `tests/v100.py` (ports 8296/8297; the stand-in engine refuses an empty prompt like Kling 3.0).

## v101 Every model keeps its own engine (the Admin default is for nooi Auto)
- The real cause of `engine@kling-3.0/video: AI engine 422: prompt is required` on Motion Control → "Replace the person": models with no direct provider (`directAdapter` → the generic `{key: cap}`) fell back to Kie with the Admin default model first (`kieDefault`), before their built-in id (`kieAuto` / `KIE_BUILTIN`). The Admin quick-start card saves `videoModel: "kling-3.0/video"` and `imageModel: "nano-banana-pro"`, so Character Swap (mcswap), Motion Control (mc26/mc30), Veo, Wan, Grok, Pixverse, Seedance Mini, Kling 3.0 Turbo… were all sent to Kling 3.0 text/picture-to-video (Character Swap with no words → 422), and Imagen / Ideogram / Seedream 5… to Nano Banana Pro.
- `adapterFor` (providers/extra.js): a model with its own `KIE_BUILTIN` id uses it; the Admin default applies only to "auto" / no model / a model without one. An id set per model in Admin → Models (`modelCat[m].kieModel`) still wins over both.
- Tests: `tests/kie_mock.mjs` (routing with the defaults set), `tests/v100.py` cases 6–7 (server started with `KIE_VIDEO_MODEL` / `KIE_IMAGE_MODEL`: Character Swap → wan/2-2-animate-replace end to end, Wan 3.0 keeps its engine, nooi Auto → the default).

## v102 Motion Control / Character Swap: asked once more, then a clear line
- After v101 the swap reached its own engine (wan/2-2-animate-replace) and the engine failed on its side; the client read the general "The AI provider is temporarily unavailable" (the engine's own words — `internal error (500)` — are hidden by `engineReason`, and the failure pop-up had no Details).
- `lib/jobs.js` `fallback()`: an mc26 / mc30 / mcswap job (never moved to another model) that the engine took (`remoteId`) and then failed with a 5xx or no words for the client is submitted once more to the same engine after `MC_AGAIN_MS` (20 s; `job.again102`). Still failing → `MC_FAIL.swap` / `MC_FAIL.move` ("an error on its side… nothing was charged… a photo that shows the whole person works best"), `errorType: "engine"`. An engine reason in words (e.g. "No person found") is shown as before, without a second try.
- Page: `SRVTX81` has the Arabic of both lines; the failure pop-up (statusChanged notice) uses `errText81` (the reader's language) and passes the owner's `detail` → "Details" with the engine's own words.
- Tests: `tests/v100.py` cases 8–9 (stand-in engine fails once → made, charged once; fails every time → the line, nothing charged); `tests/v83.py` (Motion Control failure: two tasks on the same engine, no other model).

## v103 Character Swap / Motion Control: a look at the photo and the video before anything is charged
- The swap engine (wan/2-2-animate-replace) answers only `Internal Error (500)` when it can't find one clear person: the owner's tries (a head-and-shoulders selfie, then a front / side / back sheet — three figures — with the same close-up video of a man holding a large model car) all failed that way.
- `lib/mcprep.js` `lookMotion` (called by `prepMotion`, before charging; only when the text AI is connected — every text AI path reads pictures): the photo (≤768 px) and three video frames (10 / 50 / 90 %, ≤640 px) → `llmJson` (tier quick, 40 s) → `{photo_people, video_people, video_person: clear|hidden|cut|none, note_en, note_ar, sure}`. Refused only when `sure`: no person / several people in the photo, no person in the video, several people in a swap video (`LOOK_MSG`, code 400, nothing charged). Otherwise a note → `meta.mcLook`. No AI / no answer → as before.
- `lib/jobs.js` `fail()`: an mc job that still fails on the engine's side with `meta.mcLook` → "The engine couldn't finish this. What may have stopped it: <note>…" + `job.errorAr` (Arabic); `publicJob` sends `errorAr`; the page's two job polls copy it; `errText81` shows `errorAr` in Arabic, the whole reason line in English, and translates the `LOOK_MSG` refusals.
- Test: `tests/v103.py` (ports 8301/8302; stand-in swap engine + stand-in text AI on the same mock: `/v1/messages`).

## v104 Character Swap / Motion Control: no Quality choice — always the best output, sharper input, the sound kept
- The owner: the swap failed at 480p and worked at 720p ("delete the resolutions because it effects the outputs"); the person was replaced but small things (products) came out a little different.
- Page (`mcRes93` / `mcK93`): no Quality picker; the swap is always 720p (×1), Make it move always 1080p (×1.5) — the price on the button. (Before, 480p showed half the price while the server charged the full one.)
- `lib/mcprep.js` `prepMotion`: sets `meta.res` (mcswap 720p, mc26/mc30 1080p) before the price is worked out, whatever an old page sends. `prepVideo`: a re-made movement video spends the engine's 10 MB on picture quality (libx264 medium, CRF 17, capped at 1.5–12 Mb/s by length, 128k sound) — products, text and small details are drawn from this copy; a video that already fits is sent as it is.
- `keepSound(result, mcMove)`: the engine's video comes back silent → the client's own sound is put under it (video copied, AAC 160k, trimmed to the result's length) → `<id>-snd.mp4`; nothing changes when the result has sound or the video had none. Called once in `finish()` for mc jobs (`job.sound104`).
- Limits: the engine redraws the whole frame, so small objects can still drift a little; keeping the original pixels outside the person would need a per-frame person mask (not built).
- Test: `tests/v103.py` cases 6–8 (an old page asks 480p → the engine gets 720p, charged the 720p price, the result has the video's sound; a silent video → the engine's own file; Make it move asked 720p → 1080p, ×1.5); `tests/v93.py` (no Quality buttons); `tests/v83.py` (Motion Control price ×1.5).

## v105 Character Swap on MiniMax H3 (reference-to-video)
- The owner: "Change the model to MiniMax H3, and still it's changing some outputs" (products came out a little different on wan/2-2-animate-replace).
- `providers/kie.js`: `KIE_BUILTIN.video.mcswap = "minimax-h3/reference-to-video"`. `kieInput`: `{prompt: swapWords(meta.mcKeep, product?), reference_image_urls: [new person, product photo?], reference_video_urls: [the video], aspect_ratio: "adaptive", duration: 4–15, resolution: "2K"}`. `swapWords`: replace the person in Video 1 with the person in Image 1; keep everything else exactly the same frame by frame — the listed products, every product's shape / colour / logo / label / text, what the person holds, place, light, camera, timing, every movement and lip movement; Image 2 = the product; nothing added, no text. A refused `resolution` (400/422 naming it) → sent again without it (the engine's default). `kieDur`: 4–15 s.
- Field names from docs.kie.ai/market/minimax-h3/reference-to-video (seen through search results — the site is blocked here; verify on the first real run): `reference_image_urls`, `reference_video_urls`, `reference_audio_urls`, `aspect_ratio` ("adaptive"), `duration`; 2K / 768P from MiniMax's own docs. Kie bills the clip it makes plus the video it reads: $0.065/s each at 2K → `ENGINE_COST.video.mcswap = 0.65` per 5 s (154 credits; 768P would be $0.40 → 95).
- `lib/mcprep.js`: `prepMotion` sets `meta.res = "2K"` for the swap; `prepVideo(url, 15)` for the swap (a longer video refused with the reason, nothing charged; the moves keep 30 s); the optional product photo `inputs.mcProd` → upright JPEG (swap only, dropped otherwise). `lookMotion` (swap) also asks for `keep_en` — the products, objects, logos and words seen in the video → `meta.mcKeep` → the engine's words. `keepSound(result, video, swap)`: the swap always gets the video's own sound (or none) and is cut to the video's length (H3 makes its own sound and at least 4 s).
- Page: the swap takes 2–15 s (priced 4–15 s), a "3 · The product (optional)" upload slot (`M.mcProd`, upload only), tip mentions the product photo; fallback price 154.
- Limits: H3 still redraws the frame; the list + product photo make it keep products much more closely, but pixel-exact products would need a per-frame person mask and compositing (not built).
- Tests: `tests/kie_mock.mjs` (the request, 4 / 15 s, keep words, product = Image 2, refused resolution → resent); `tests/v103.py` cases 6–6c (2K + video + person + product + keep list, 4 s price, the video's own sound and length, 16 s refused); `tests/v93.py` (product slot on the swap only), `tests/v95.py`, `tests/v100.py` (the H3 engine).

- The owner (screenshot: a character made from a front / side / back sheet): "add another field for the face … this face will be the avatar logo. Optional. If there is no image of the face, take the face from the picture and use it for the character."

Own face photo: the same finder, or the middle (a little high) without the AI; `c.faceOwn`.

## v107 Replace the person — the right person in long, crowded videos
- The owner: "put a picture to trace the person in the video to be replaced … the videos go more than 30 s and have many people … analyze the video and trace the person".
- Page (Motion Control → Replace the person): videos 2 s – 2 min (`mcMax105` 120, priced 4–120 s); step "3 · The person to replace": "Tap them in the video" (`frame107`: a frame of the video in `.pick107`, −2 s / +2 s, a tap → `M.mcWho` = the clean frame as `person.jpg` + `tap:[x,y]`, shown with a ring) or "upload a picture of them"; the product is step 4. Sent: `inputs.mcWho` + `meta.whoTap`. Uploads use the file's own name (`m.file.name`).
- `lib/mcprep.js`: `prepSwapVideo` — ≤ 15.5 s as before (`prepVideo`), longer → `n = ceil(dur/15)` equal parts, each its own engine-ready copy (`encodeMc`, the old encode as a function, ≤ 10 MB each), > 120.5 s refused. `traceSwap`: a tapped frame → `pickedPerson` (the text AI gets the frame with a red box at the tap → `{box, who_en}` → the frame cut to that person, `-who.jpg`; no answer → a tall box around the tap); a picture → `prepPhoto`. Then `partLook` per part (the person's picture + 3 frames → `{people, present, where_en, who_en, sure}`), all at once. Refused (nothing charged, only when sure): several people and nobody picked (`TRACE_MSG.who`, also `LOOK_MSG.videoMany`), the person in no part (`TRACE_MSG.gone`). `meta.swapParts = [{url, start, dur, swap, where}]` (parts > 1) or `meta.swapWhere`; `meta.swapWho`, `meta.swapSrc` (the client's whole video, for the sound). `lookMotion(..., target)` no longer refuses several people when someone was picked. No text AI → every part swapped, the picture as it is.
- `lib/jobs.js`: a swap in parts is priced for the seconds swapped (`body.dur`; `meta.durVideo`, `meta.durSwapped`), one child job per swapped part, all started at once (`payload.dur` = the part's engine length, `inputs.mcMove` = the part, `meta.swapPart`, `meta.swapWhere`); each child still gets the part's own sound and length (`keepSound`). The parent poller: all parts made → `joinSwap` (swapped parts + kept parts, scaled to the engine's size, 30 fps, each held / cut to its part's length, concatenated, the whole video's own sound) → `-swap.mp4`, settled; a part failing → the swap fails with that part's own words (and Arabic), nothing charged.
- `providers/kie.js` `swapWords({keep, prod, target, where, who})`: "Replace one person in Video 1 — the person shown in Image 2: <where> — with the person in Image 1 … Keep every other person in Video 1 exactly as they are"; images: [new person, the person to replace, product] (the product becomes Image 3).
- `lib/plans.js`: Motion Control / Replace the person aren't held to the plan's video length (they follow the client's own video and are priced by its length).
- Tests: `tests/v107.py` (ports 8307/8308; one Python stand-in for the engine and a text AI that really finds the coloured "people" in the pictures); `tests/v103.py` (125 s refused instead of 16 s); `tests/kie_mock.mjs` (the words with the person to replace).

## v108 Replace the person: the face really changes · no long silent wait
- The owner: "It didn't change the face + it takes very very long time to generate the video, check what causes this issue of loading".
- Face: the swap words were plain "Image 1" / "Video 1"; MiniMax's own H3 prompt guide (github.com/MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-H3, skills/h3-prompt-writing/references/ref-en.txt) names the files `<Picture N>` / `<Video N>` (in input order per kind), people and things `<Subject N>`, and has sections `subject_definitions` → `summary` ("[video editing] The target video is an edited version of <Video 1>.") → `retention_analysis` (`fully_preserved` / `partially_preserved` / `attribute_transfer` / `weak_reference`) → `detailed_description` → `overall_soundscape` → `non_diegetic_music`. Guides also report H3 keeping the source person when the roles aren't explicit. `swapWords` now writes that layout: <Subject 1> = the new person from <Picture 1> (fully_preserved, "never the face of the person it replaces"), the replaced person = attribute_transfer (only position / movements / expressions / lips kept), <Picture 2> = the person to replace (several people), the product = the next picture, <Video 1> fully_preserved except the replaced person.
- Slowness (what we found in our own steps): (1) everything before the engine — reading and re-making the video (x264 "medium"), the text AI looks (up to 40 s each), parts — ran inside the one "create" request: the page waited with nothing to show for as long as nginx allows (900 s); (2) joining a long swap re-made the whole 2K video (x264 "fast"): 34 s for 39 s of 2K on a 4-core test box, minutes on the live server; (3) the engine itself at 2K (1–5+ min per part, out of our hands).
- `lib/jobs.js`: Motion Control / Replace the person — the job is made at once (held at the page's price; swap length 4–120 s from the page), `prepAndStart` runs in the background: `prepMotion(body, step)` (job.stage "prep" → "look"), then the swap is priced for the real video (`lowerHold` / `raiseHold` in lib/billing.js; a swap in parts for the seconds swapped), job.stage "engine", the parts or the job sent. A refusal (code 400) fails the job with its reason (`userMsg`), the hold released. `job.times` {prep, look, engine, join} in seconds (staff only in publicJob); publicJob sends `stage`, `partsDone` / `partsTotal`.
- `lib/mcprep.js`: `mcBest(body)` (the best output before pricing); `encodeMc` preset "veryfast" (7.2 s → 4.3 s for 15 s here); `joinSwap` puts parts end to end without re-making them when every part came from the engine with the same size/codec/fps (0.24 s instead of 34 s), else re-makes them with "veryfast".
- Page: `stage108` line on the card ("Getting your video ready…", "Looking at the video and the photos…", "The engine is making it — 1 of 3 parts ready…", "Joining the parts…", Arabic too); both job polls copy `stage` and the parts; the Make button says "Uploading your files…" while a phone video is still uploading; the new refusals in Arabic (`errText81`).
- Tests: `tests/v107.py` (the job made at once with its step; held at the page's price, charged for the seconds swapped; refusals on the card; the step line in EN/AR); `tests/v103.py`, `tests/v95.py`, `tests/v100.py`, `tests/v93.py`, `tests/kie_mock.mjs` updated for the background flow and H3's layout.

## v109 Slow pictures and videos — what slowed every job, fixed
- The owner: "بطئ شديد في انشاء الفديوها والصور، اوجد حل" (very slow videos and pictures). The live server can't be reached from the work sandbox, and the Hostinger API host was blocked by the sandbox's network policy, so the causes were found in the code and measured in tests.
- Cause 1 — jobs a restart left behind: a job lives only in memory between being made and reaching the engine (files being made ready, a part waiting for the one before, the parts being joined). After every restart (each update) nothing picked those up: they stayed "queued" for ever. `lib/jobs.js` `recoverJobs()` (3 s after start, `RECOVER_MS`; jobs made since this start are skipped): a recent (< 2 h) job without an engine task is sent again (`job.restarted`), a join starts again (`joining` cleared), an old job or a Motion Control job still being prepared fails with `RESTART_MSG` (+ Arabic), credits back.
- Cause 2 — the page's 3 s status loop asked about every unfinished job one after another, and a slow round didn't stop the next one starting: with old unfinished jobs the rounds piled up, the newest jobs (last in the list) were asked about last — measured with the old page and 14 jobs answered in 1.5 s: in 14 s the 3 newest were never asked, the first was asked 5 times by overlapping rounds. Now (`poll109`): 4 at a time, never two rounds at once, a job under 10 minutes old every ~3 s, under an hour every 10 s, older once a minute; the prompt box's 1.5 s loop never runs twice at once (`hf109`).
- Cause 3 — the server asked the engine about each job every 2 s even when the engine calls back: a job sent with a callback (https) is now asked every 6 s as a safety net (the callback still answers at once) — less load here and at the engine.
- Admin → Overview → Speed (`/v1/admin/speed`, staff only, `speedReport()`): processor load / CPUs, the longest event-loop wait in the last minute, free memory, jobs being made (by step), jobs waiting > 10 min, jobs picked up after the restart, data file size, and for the last 24 h per kind (pictures, videos, Replace the person, Make it move) the usual time, our part (`sentAt − created`), the engine's (`doneAt − sentAt`), the slowest — with what to do in plain words. Jobs now record `sentAt` and `doneAt`.
- Test: `tests/v109.py` (ports 8309/8310).

## v110 Still slow after v109 — a preview left running, and the time nobody measured
- The owner: "ما زال بطيئاً جداً بعد التحديث" (still very slow after the update). Still no access to the live server from the sandbox, so: what the code shows, fixed; what only the server shows, measured and said in plain words.
- Cause — the preview copy (`deploy/stage.sh`) runs beside the live site on a copy of its data, and `promote` left it running. It checked every live job with the engine again, downloaded and smoothed/joined results again (same processor, same memory), and since v109 its `recoverJobs()` could send a live job to the engine a second time (engine credits spent twice). Now `liveJob(job)` (`NOOI_STAGE=1` and made before the copy was first started — `stage110.since`, kept in the copy, so a restart of the preview keeps its own jobs its own) is skipped by the poll loop, `pollOne` (also an engine callback), the parent/join loop and `recoverJobs`; the preview doesn't keep media (`startKeepMedia`), test providers every 15 min or run the weekly agent learning. What is made on the preview itself is made as usual. `stage.sh promote` now removes the preview after the update (`stop_preview`).
- Saving (`lib/store.js`): the disk write runs in the background (the JSON text is still made at once — `SAVES` times it); under constant changes it saves at least every 2 s (before, each change pushed the save back); SIGTERM/SIGINT save what is in memory first (`saveNow`) — an update no longer loses the last changes.
- Times per job (`job.times`): `words` — the words steps before the job exists (Arabic → English, scene, physics; the create request waits for them; up to ~6.5 s when the text AI is slow), `fetch` — bringing the result to this server, `after` — sound / frame smoothing. The speed card's "our part" now includes the words step; the engine's share excludes the download.
- Admin → Overview → Speed: `speedAdvice()` adds `advice` [{en, ar}] — a preview copy still running (asks `127.0.0.1:$STAGE_PORT|8090/v1/health`, 0.8 s), saving > 250 ms, the words step > 4 s, the download > 10 s, the engine's own time ≥ 70 % of the total (> 45 s) — and `server.saveMs`, `server.preview`; the card shows them first, and "Each save".
- `deploy/doctor.sh` — read-only check-up the owner runs on the VPS and sends back: load/CPUs, memory, disk, busiest programs, ffmpeg count, the nooi / nooi-stage / ollama services (restarts, memory), site starts in 24 h, local answer times, answer times of the AI services from the server, which speed settings are set (never values), jobs from `data/db.json` (being made by step; last 24 h per kind: usual time, our part, engine, words, download; last failures), warnings in the log (keys masked).
- Test: `tests/v110.py` (ports 8311–8314: a live server and a preview copy, each with its own stand-in engine and a slow stand-in text AI). `tests/stage_sh.py`: promote removes the preview.

## v111 Where the time goes — the check-up's numbers
- The owner's `deploy/doctor.sh` output: AI services answer in 0.1–0.2 s from the VPS, 8 users / 171 jobs (a small data file), nothing waiting; the one job of the day, a Replace the person: 730 s — 45 s ours (prepare + text AI), 685 s at the engine (MiniMax H3 at 2K, including its line). Not the server.
- The engine's time in two: `pollOne` sets `job.genAt` the first time the engine says it is making the job (Kie `waiting`/`queuing` → our "queued"); `finish` records `times.queue` (sent → making) and `times.making`; a long swap's parent takes its slowest part's and the first part's `sentAt`. Speed card + `deploy/doctor.sh` (each of the last 5 swaps / moves step by step: video length, parts, prepare, text AI look, engine line, engine making, bringing here, total).
- Advice: the engine's line ≥ 40 % of its time (> 60 s) → "the engine is busy then; nothing on this server shortens that"; a swap made in > 4 min → how to try 768P: Admin → Connections & APIs → AI engine (all models) → Extra inputs per model (JSON) `{"minimax-h3/reference-to-video":{"resolution":"768P"}}` (`extraInputs` overrides `kieInput`; not suggested once set). The price stays the same; 2K stays the default (the owner asked for the best output, v104/v105). Third-party pages say H3 takes about 2–5 min a video and 2K costs the engine more per second than 768P (no official timing).
- Our part shorter (`prepMotion`): the video, the photo and the product photo are made ready at the same time; `lookMotion` and `traceSwap` run side by side (both write `body.meta` with a spread right after their own await — no lost keys); refusals keep the old order (`Promise.allSettled`, the first rejected in order video → photo → product, then look → trace).
- Test: `tests/v111.py` (ports 8315/8316).

## v112 Still slow — the text AI's 42 s look, the engine's line, a one-tap 768P
- The owner's next check-up: the swap's own steps were prepare 3 s, text AI look 42 s (Gemma on Google with 4 pictures; each look waited up to 40 s for each text AI in turn — local → NVIDIA → Claude/Qwen), then the engine; a new swap was at the engine.
- `lib/mcprep.js` `lookAsk`: each look (`lookMotion`, `pickedPerson`, `partLook`) waits at most `MC_LOOK_MS` (15 s) in all; a slower one is left out like a failed one (no refusal, no keep-words, the tall box around the tap) and the job is sent.

The 768P advice now says to tap the switch. 2K stays the default.

- Test: `tests/v112.py` (ports 8317/8318).

## v113 The first frame ties the video to it · Generate closes the open panel · a busy engine · the screen turned off
- The owner: "I put the picture as first frame … but the outputs aren't related to it — the images and frames are not linked in the prompt" + "when you press Create or Generate, the prompt box remains on the screen" + (a swap failed) "when I close the screen, the generate is off and fails".
- First frame: the picture did reach the engine (`first_frame_url` / `image_urls`), but the words never mentioned it and the page's Style line ("2D hand-drawn animation…", default "Cinematic film look…") pulled the video away from it. `lib/intent.js` `frames113` / `frameWords`: a video with `vStart`/`startImage` and/or `vEnd` gets, first in its words, "The video starts exactly on the first-frame picture and continues from it, with the same place, subject, people, composition, colours, light and visual style as that picture…" (both: "…and ends exactly on the end-frame picture N seconds later…"; end alone: "The video ends exactly on…"); `intentLines(kind, meta, framed)` leaves the Style line out while a frame is set (type, camera move, motion, avoid, voice-over still go). Not for sketch / Motion Control / Extend shot / editor commands / storyboard shots, nor later parts of a long video (`chainFrom`/`chainAfter`/`part > 1`); `meta.frames113`. The frames panel says so (`.fr113`, EN + AR).
- Generate / Create (any `GEN82` action) closes the open option panel (`ST93().open = ""`) and blurs the focused text box (the phone keyboard goes) — the result is in view; the prompt empties as before.
- A busy engine: "Model response exception, please try again later | 模型响应异常，请稍后重试 (1012)" had no 3-digit status → classed "input" → never asked again, and the client read the engine's Chinese. `ENGINE_BUSY` (lib/mcprep.js): a busy / "try again later" failure on Motion Control / Replace the person is asked again twice (`MC_AGAIN_MS` 20 s, then ×3), then fails with "The engine was too busy to make this — we asked it again twice. Nothing was charged. Try again in a few minutes." + Arabic; `engineReason` drops CJK text.
- Screen off: jobs on the server go on whatever the page does; what the page itself does — uploading files and sending the job (`POST /v1/uploads`, `/v1/jobs`) — failed after three quick tries when a phone cut the connection with its screen off. Now, if the page was hidden (or offline) during the request, it waits for the screen and the connection and goes on (up to 6 more tries, 30 min); the server answers the same request with the same job. With the screen on, a lost connection still says so.
- Test: `tests/v113.py` (ports 8319/8320); `tests/v100.py` updated (a picture with no words: frame words + picture words, no style line).

Stage `lips` ("Matching the lips to the voice…"), polled every `LIPS_POLL_MS` (4 s) up to `LIPS_MAX_MS` (20 min); `job.lipsTask` lets a restart follow the same task; `times.lips`. - The owner: "the issue is from the engine — even on Seedance it still takes a long time (Higgsfield and OpenArt are fast)" + "deploy a tool that makes the engine so fast". The waiting is the engine provider's line and its GPUs; the site can pick the quickest tier and let a second provider be tried, and it measures which part is slow (v111 line / making).

Input = fal's Seedance 2.0 Fast reference-to-video schema: `prompt` (seedanceSwapWords), `image_urls`, `video_urls`, `resolution` 720p/480p, `duration` as a string, `aspect_ratio: "auto"`, `generate_audio: false`. Routed in providers/extra.js (`fal@bytedance/seedance-2.0/fast/reference-to-video`, `adapterByKey`); polled every 2 s (no callbacks); the busy-engine retry and the public-link check cover it too.
- Price: `ENGINE_COST.video.mcswapfal` 0.75 per 5 s (fal's $0.2419/s × 0.6 with a video input); `priceOf` and `priceTable().video.mcswap` use it while fal.ai is picked, so the page shows and charges that; the page reloads `/v1/config` after a switch.
- Speed card: four engines (Seedance 2.0 Fast · fastest, Seedance 2.0, Seedance 2.0 Fast · fal.ai — off until the fal key is saved, MiniMax H3) and each one's resolutions; `setSwapEngine("fal")` without a key → 400.
- A 16 s video on the Video page is made as two 8 s parts, one after the other (the second from the first's last frame) — that works with the stand-in engine; a real failure needs the engine's reason (staff: Details on the card, or deploy/doctor.sh's "last failures").
- Test: `tests/v116.py` (ports 8325/8326); v114 runs with `SWAP_ENGINE=seedance`; kie_mock: mcswap → Seedance 2.0 Fast.

- The owner: "improve the lip sync, or use the add-ons already included, or build a tool, or combine the techniques". Lip sync reads the mouth shapes from the voice; music and room noise in the track blur them, and one engine failing used to end the lips.

Both failing → the swap as it came + the v115 note, the lips not charged.

## v119 "Kie AI was very fast at first, now very slow — cache or code? Which add-ons slow the export? Would a bigger VPS help?"
- Nothing caches engine results; a cache can't slow the engine. The time is (v110/v111 measures): the AI engine's line + making (+ the lip sync, another engine job) — not changed by this server — and this server's own work: preparing the files, the sound, 60/120 fps, joining parts, editor exports.
- Measured on 2 cores (KVM 2): converting a 30 s 1080×1920 editor recording to MP4 29 s (4 cores: 16 s) — scales with cores; 60 fps for 5 s of 720p 86 s (4 cores: 83 s) — ffmpeg's minterpolate uses one core, so a bigger VPS alone didn't help it.
- `lib/fps96.js`: 60/120 fps made in `smoothParts(dur)` pieces (one per core, `SMOOTH_PARTS` overrides, pieces ≥ 1.5 s) side by side; each piece but the last reads 0.4 s further and is trimmed back; joined (concat, copy) with the source's sound. Same frames as one pass (the last fraction of a second is minterpolate's own limit); 86 s → 47 s on 2 cores, 24 s on 4.
- `server.js` `/v1/media/transcode`: ffprobe first; an MP4 request for an H.264 recording already at the format's size is only re-packed (`-c:v copy`, AAC sound, faststart); others converted as before. Each conversion → `lib/xstats.js` `noteExport` (ms, seconds of video, format, copy); the response says `copy` and `ms`.
- Page: the editor's server export uploaded the recording twice (`API.upload` in the base export, then the transcode); now only the transcode upload — the library job keeps the converted file (`resultUrl`); if converting fails, the original is uploaded then.
- Admin → Speed: `vps119(report)` → `vps` {cpus, busy, engineShare, ownShare, exportRate, exports, verdict}: "no" when the engine (line + making + lips) is ≥ 60 % of the jobs' time, "yes" when this server's own work (`times.prep` + after-the-engine minus lips + join) is ≥ 25 % or the 5-minute load ≥ 90 % of the cores; advice lines EN/AR for both and for exports (seconds of converting per second of video). `deploy/doctor.sh`: the same verdict from the last 24 h, and a 3-second x264 test of the processor (nothing saved).
- Test: `tests/v119.py` (port 8330).

All off with reduce motion (the word stays highlighted and underlined).

The agents step (`agents.py`) failed once in the full run (desktop: the typed brief never showed Quill) and passed twice alone — not reproduced.

## v124 Many customers at once
- The owner (with a screenshot of "High demand right now — we retried automatically…" in English on an Arabic phone): "1 client or 12,000,000 — make it fast and able to take them all". The cause: the AI engine takes only so many NEW tasks per account (Kie: 20 every 10 s; more get 429 and are dropped, not queued — its docs); the server sent everything at once, retried 3× in ~4 s, hopped to other models on the same account and failed the job. Plan and capacity math: `docs/SCALE.md`.
- `lib/line124.js` — the engine's send line. `inLine(name, fn)` (Kie's `call` wraps createTask / veo generate·extend / mj generate POSTs; reading a task doesn't wait). Pace = 90 % of the limit (`KIE_RATE` env or Admin → Speed → Engine limit, `platformS().line124`, default "20/10") over the window + `SKEW` (10 %, min 150 ms — a request reaches the engine a little after it leaves). Fair: the oldest task of the customer served longest ago (`job.owner`, set at creation; `JOBCTX` AsyncLocalStorage around `start()` tells the line which job). A 429 (or 433) → back to the FRONT, pause 0.5 s doubling only while the engine keeps saying it after a pause (`pausedAt`; requests already on their way don't grow it; a send that works resets it), pace −1 (never under half), +1 per window after 10 quiet seconds. Waiting jobs get `stage:"line"`, `line:{ahead,sec}` (publicJob shows `line` while queued). Over `LINE_MAX_MIN` (20) → rejected with `userMsg`/`userMsgAr`, `noFallback`; `fail()` copies `userMsgAr` to `errorAr`. `lineStats()` → Admin speed (`lines.engine` — the server renames the key "kie"), advice in words; `POST /v1/admin/speed/pace {engine:"engine", pace:"40/10"}`. Line timers are kept (not unref'd): waiting tasks keep a script alive.
- `lib/jobs.js`: "rate" no longer hops to another Kie model; a "rate" answer to a poll isn't counted against the job; `ACTIVE` map of jobs still being made (`track()` at creation, rebuilt every 15 s) — the 1 s poll loop and the 3 s parent loop look only at those; `pollEvery` 2/5/10 s by age (with a call-back 6/15/30 s, live sketch 0.9 s) and at most `POLL_RPS` (10) checks a second per engine, longest-unchecked first; `findJob`/`ownerOf` use `job.owner` / ACTIVE before scanning every customer.
- `server.js`: `GET /v1/jobs/live?ids=a,b` (max 50; unknown → null). `lib/static124.js` `fastFiles("public")`: the site's text files compressed once (brotli q11 / gzip 9, in the background) and kept in memory with an ETag; `no-cache` + 304; the page 2.6 MB → 636 KB br / 900 KB gz (nginx passes it on as it is); `warm()` compresses index.html at start.
- Page block `v124: MANY CUSTOMERS AT ONCE`: `ERR_MSG` is a Proxy over `ERR_F124()` (made at load it stayed in the language the page opened in — the screenshot's English message on an Arabic page); `line124(j)` text on the home result card (`small.ln124`, order 2 under the stars — v81 hides `.hfrph small`, overridden) and as `stage108`'s `line`; pollers copy `r.line` → `o.line124`; `API.req` coalesces `GET /v1/jobs/<id>` into one `/v1/jobs/live` request (30 ms; 15 s while `document.hidden`, at once on `visibilitychange`); a job the batch doesn't know is asked by itself (old behaviour — tests that fake `/v1/jobs/<id>` keep working); a 404 from `/live` (older server) → one by one for good. `#toast .tm`, a failed card's text and `.stage108 span` use `unicode-bidi:plaintext` / `dir="auto"`. Admin → Speed row `.ln124a` + `#pace124`.
- Tests: `tests/v124.mjs` (ports 8341/8342: mock engine 6/s + a burst of 429s; 60 jobs all done, 0 refused for being over, fair line unit check, too-long wait EN/AR, `/live`, br/gz/304, Admin) and `tests/v124.py` (8343). Updated: `kie_mock` (429/433 on a new task now waits and goes through), `v79_fixes` (fakes answer `/v1/jobs/live`; states set while no check is in flight — an answer on its way wrote "done" over them, also before v124).

## v125 Delete my account · cookies checked
- Phase 1 of `docs/FABLE_BRIEF.md`. `lib/account125.js` `deleteAccount(uid, confirm)` — `POST /v1/me/delete {confirm:"DELETE"|"حذف"}` (a connection/API token gets 403). Removes the user record (`dropUser`; plans are prepaid periods, nothing renews), every `/media` file the record points to (deep scan of the record + `u.uploads`, now noted on each `POST /v1/uploads` by `noteUpload`) unless another user or a shared list (explore, showcase, worldcast, tplprev, gifts, invoices) still points to it, their Explore posts and likes, their tickets. Jobs still queued/rendering become `cancelled`: the send line drops them unsent (`lib/line124.js`), `fail()`/`finish()` ignore them. The sign-in account goes with `deleteAuthUser` (needs `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT`), and for 2 h a still-valid token can't recreate it (`gone125`, not in local mode). Kept: invoices (own collection) and gift codes. `deletions125` (owner's list): time, counts, engine voice ids to remove at the voice engine — no name or email.
- `lib/store.js` `hasUser()` (no creation) / `dropUser()`; `lib/jobs.js` `activeJobs`, `findJob`, `ownerOf` use `hasUser` so the loops never make a deleted customer again (`user()` creates). `wrap()` also sends `errorAr`; the page's `API.req` keeps it on the error (`err.errorAr`).
- Page block `v125: DELETE MY ACCOUNT`: `#del125` panel (what goes, invoices kept, typed confirmation unlocks the red button) at the end of v122's "Sign-in & data" part (`PARTS122.account` wrapped). After the server deletes: no more saving, `localStorage` state and the media cache (`DRIP_CACHE`) cleared, sign-out, reload with `?deleted=1&l=<lang>` → the goodbye toast in that language.
- Cookies: the server sets none; no analytics or ad scripts (only three.js, Firebase sign-in, the payment provider's form) → no consent banner needed. The cookie policy claimed "analytics cookies only if you agree" — now says none are used (consent first if that ever changes), and that sign-in/payment pages set their own security cookies; privacy: how to delete the account yourself, deletion at once (backups within 90 days), "analytics" removed from the providers list. Page `LEGAL` and `docs/policies/*` alike; privacy and cookies dated 2026-10-22. Test: `tests/v125.py` (port 8345).

## v127 What the AI makes says so
- Phase 1 of `docs/FABLE_BRIEF.md`. `lib/label127.js` `labelAI(job, url, uid)` — called last in `finish()` (and after the swap join + lips in the parts path; a swap part waits for the joined video): (1) the visible "Made with AI · صُنع بالذكاء الاصطناعي" mark (`public/brand/ai-mark127.png`, bottom corner, ~20 % of the width, ffmpeg overlay in place; pictures stay RGB) — always on Replace the person (`mcswap`/`swap93`; if it can't be added the job fails, nothing charged), on everything else when the customer sets `u.aiMark127` (`GET/POST /v1/me/aimark`, Account → Preferences → AI label `#aim127`); (2) content credentials (C2PA 2.1) in PNG/JPEG/MP4/MOV — `c2pa.created`, IPTC `trainedAlgorithmicMedia` (or `compositeWithTrainedAlgorithmicMedia` when the job had inputs, a swap, or an editor export), softwareAgent and claim generator "nooi.ai" (no engine names). A file that already has credentials (from the engine) keeps them. WebP/GIF/audio/MXF are left as they are (MP3 signing didn't validate in the official reader). One file at a time; files over `C2PA_MAX_MB` (400) skipped; `C2PA=false` or Admin switch turns credentials off.
- `@trustnxt/c2pa-ts` 0.14.1 (Apache-2.0, pure TypeScript, no downloads at install) + `reflect-metadata` + `@peculiar/x509`, loaded on first use. Signer: nooi's own (a small CA + ES256 signing cert with SKI/AKI/EKU, made once, key encrypted in `col("label127").signer`) — readers show "unknown signer" until the owner pastes a certificate chain + PKCS#8 key from a C2PA-trusted CA (Admin → Features → AI labels `#lb127`, `GET/POST /v1/admin/labels {credentials, cert, key, reset}`; a certificate is test-signed first and refused if it isn't C2PA-profile). Editor exports (`/v1/media/transcode`, MP4/HEVC/ProRes MOV) are signed too (`credentials: true` in the answer).
- Tests: `tests/v127.py` (ports 8348/8349, a stand-in engine serving real files) checks every file with nooi's reader and the official C2PA reader (`tests/c2pa_read.mjs`, `@contentauth/c2pa-wasm` — a devDependency, not installed on the server).

## v130 Pictures with # names in the video prompt bar · Sketch checked line by line
- Video (nooi Studio) prompt bar: "+" opens a menu — **Pictures to use** (several at once, up to 6: slots `vRef1…vRef6`, kept in IndexedDB like the frames), First frame, End frame. A strip above the words shows the frames and the pictures; each picture has a name (`image1…` / `صورة1…`, renamed in place — the words follow), × removes it, a tap puts `#name` at the caret. Typing `#` opens the list of pictures (arrows + Enter / Tab, or a tap; with no pictures it offers to add one and puts its name in). The names are tinted in the words by a copy of the text laid exactly under the textarea (`.mr130`, same computed font / padding / direction / unicode-bidi, kept in place by a ResizeObserver; the textarea itself is never moved). A picture the words use is ringed. Adding a picture switches a non-Seedance engine to Seedance 2.0 (toast); Generate with another engine is refused on the page with the reason.
- The page sends `meta.refs130 = [{slot, tag}]` with every take made from the Video page (`makeVideo` → `addJob`); other videos (stories, ads, `addJob` elsewhere) don't upload the `vRef` slots (`serverSubmit` hides them).
- Server (`lib/refs130.js`): `asciiTags130` (first thing in `createJob`, before the English step) rewrites the names to `#ref1…` in the words and the list — the customer's own words stay the title; `checkRefs130` (after the model check) refuses an engine outside `REF_STUDIO130` (auto, seedance25, seedance20, seedance) with 400 + `errorAr`, more than 6 (400), drops them from a picture job, and moves a long video's `partModels` to Seedance. `providers/kie.js`: `bytedance/seedance-2(-fast|-5)` with pictures → `reference_image_urls` (the pictures in order, then the first frame and the end frame as more references: "The video opens / ends exactly on @ImageN"), no `first_frame_url` / `last_frame_url` (Seedance doesn't mix them), each `#refN` → `@ImageN`, the pictures not named → "Also use @ImageK as reference", "Keep every person, product and place exactly as they look in their reference pictures". Pictures and no words → "A natural, realistic video with @Image1, …". Another engine id (Admin → Models) gets the first picture as its start image and the names as plain words. Replace the person never takes them.
- Sketch (Sketch to life): marker / brush lines are drawn whole on their own layer at full strength and laid on once at the tool's strength (`skDrawStroke` replaced; while drawing, `skRedraw` keeps a copy of the paper from before the line and lays the whole line on it once a frame) — before, overlapping round ends made a row of darker beads. `pointercancel` ends the line where it was (before, the line never ended: moving the pointer anywhere went on drawing, Live waited forever); one pointer at a time (`SKL130.pid`: a palm or a second finger neither replaces nor adds to the line). On a computer the paper is `min(100%, max(320px, 100vh − 430px))` so it fits with its tools; a ResizeObserver redraws it sharp at a new size; when a picture lands on the paper out of sight the paper scrolls into view.
- Tests: `tests/v130.py` (8354/8355), `tests/v130s.py` (8356/8357).

## v131 The editor on a phone · open-source transitions, text motions, looks, banners & stickers
- A tap on the picture no longer stops the video: v71's handler (window capture, stops the event) sets `ED.playing=false` on every press on `#wk`, so `ED.playing` is an accessor that ignores that set while it plays and records the press (`PL131`); a tap (< 10 px, < 600 ms) with nothing or a clip picked toggles the clean view (`ED.clean131`: `edTick` runs with `S.ed.safe=false`, no pick, guide tabs as "clip"; chip "Tap to show the guides"); a tap on a text / banner still picks it. The same accessor is the only way to hear presses on the picture (long press, closing a menu).
- Tool row (`.edbar`): whole words, one line, scrolls sideways (fade on the far side, the left in Arabic); `tests/equal_layout.py` lists `.edbar` with the other scroll rows.
- Dragging the playhead: a clip's `currentTime` is hooked (`hook131`): a jump never cuts the one under way short (the newest place waits, `_q131`); while `TLX.s.scrub` the jump goes to a light copy (`m.px131`) and the clip reads as being there (else the sync asks every frame). The copy comes from `POST /v1/media/scrub {url:"/media/…"}` (`lib/scrub131.js`: ≤ 640 px, no sound, H.264 with a keyframe every 6 frames, no B-frames, kept as `<name>.scrub.mp4`; only existing `/media` files, never a `.scrub.mp4`, ≤ 15 min, 2 ffmpeg at a time; 404 + `errorAr` otherwise). Asked only when the browser plays H.264 and (for a phone's own file) ≤ 40 MB. While the copy is mid-jump its last frame is held (v99); after you let go the clip itself shows once it has landed.
- Long press (touch / pen, 520 ms; a mouse uses right click) on a timeline block, the picture or a made video's card (`.gc98`) → menu (`cmOpen131`: a sheet under 768 px, else by the pointer). Clip: split, duplicate, speed 0.5/1/2×, mute, move, extend with AI (price shown), replace…, settings, delete; music: replace, volume, remove; text / banner / effect: edit, duplicate, split, delete; card: open, download, edit in the editor, animate, delete. Menu lines that are the editor's own actions close the menu and then press a hidden copy of that button (`fire131`) — older window-capture handlers would otherwise act first and leave the menu open. The finger that opened the menu taps nothing when it lifts (its `touchend` is cancelled on the element it touched — the redraw detaches it, so window listeners never hear it). On the picture, v71 keeps a dragging finger's moves to itself, so the timer checks `PV.pts` (moved or lifted → no menu).
- Grok Imagine 1.5 starts from a picture: off the Video page's engine buttons, the home engine sheet (`.hfmodel[data-v=grok15]`), Recipes and Cinema Studio unless a picture is attached (`M.vStart` / `S.draft.startJob`, `M.hfRef` not a video) or it is already the chosen one.
- New in the editor (sources and licences: `docs/THIRD_PARTY.md`, `public/editor131/LICENSES.txt`): 22 transitions from gl-transitions (`editor131/transitions.json`, `glRun131`, WebGL 1; in the Transition sheet with what each does; over the last ≤ 0.8 s of the clip; the next clip's first frame is asked for ~1.5 s before and kept as a picture — `ff131` — since a paused clip that hasn't shown has no frame to copy; until it is ready, a plain cut); 20 text motions after Animate.css (`AC131`, Text tab "Motions", `tx.anim = "ac_<name>"`, looping ones repeat with a 0.5 s rest); 15 looks after CSSgram recipes (`EDX_LOOKS` index 5 = gradient / colour layers, `grad131`, dots `.lk-*`, descriptions in `PKV84.look`); banners (10, 8 styles: pill, button, tag, news bar, quote card, badge, pin, social — `type:"bn131"`) and stickers (36, `type:"st131"`, optional caption) drawn with Material Symbols paths (`editor131/icons.json`) in the Infographics tab. All are drawn on `#wk`, so the export (which records `#wk`) has them.
- Tests: `tests/v131.py` (8360); `tests/editor_real.py` scrolls the tool row to a button before tapping it.

## v132 Long videos (up to 2 minutes) and Extend, with any engine — the agents and the engines together
- What didn't work: a video longer than one engine shot was only allowed up to 30 s; its parts were never joined (the result was the first part with the others beside it); parts counted as running jobs (the 2nd part could hit the plan's limit); Extend failed on the server (the Finish tool's extend and the page's Extend never reached a video job); the Engine and Length chips sat behind Generate in nooi Studio; the slider stopped at 30 s; the crew made separate shots, not one film.
- `lib/long132.js`: `partMax132(model)` (the longest good shot per engine: 15 s for WAN 3.0 / Seedance / Kling 3.0…, 10 s Kling / Hailuo…, 8 s Veo / PixVerse / LTX 2.3), `partsFor132(body)` (a video longer than its engine's shot → parts, ≤ 16, ≤ 120 s; not Motion control / Replace the person), `planLong132` (one text-AI request: Writer, Director, Cinematographer, Continuity supervisor → `{title, bible, shots}`; checked, padded; else `offlinePlan132`: opening / moves on / final), `partPrompt132` (the shot + "Continuity: <bible>" + "continues seamlessly from the previous one … begins exactly on the previous shot's last frame"; the first-frame sentence only on part 1, the end-frame sentence only on the last; `stripFrames132` keeps them out of the agents' idea), `joinLong132(urls)` (ffmpeg: each part at the first part's size and frame rate, the repeated first frame of the next part dropped, sound kept — silence where a part has none — short fades at the joins, H.264 CRF 18 + AAC, faststart → `<id>-long.mp4`).
- `lib/jobs.js`: `createJob` marks a long video `meta.long132` (after the Replace-the-person duration line) and `partsStart132` makes the plan (`job.stage = "plan"`, `job.plan132`), then the parts (each `chainAfter` the one before → starts on its last frame; the end frame only on the last part; storyboard pictures when the crew gave them). `continuity132` in `finish()`: part 1's last frame is the anchor; with a vision AI, each later part is checked against it (`scenes.js checkScene`, imported when needed) and made again once with the differences if it left the film. A failed part is tried again (`PART_AGAIN_MS`), the second time on another engine. The parent: progress, `partsDone/partsTotal`, stage "join", `joinLong132` → one file (`segments` kept, `longDur`), AI label, charged once; a part that can't be made fails the video with "Part i of n couldn't be made… — nothing was charged" (EN/AR). `recoverJobs` restarts a long video left mid-plan. Extend: `extend132` (`meta.extend132` = the job id, or the Finish tool's extend on the customer's own video / an existing `/media` file) → a video job of 3–15 s on the same engine, from the last frame (`chainFrom`), words `EXT_WORDS132`; `extendJoin132` joins it to the video (kept as is if the engine already returned the whole length). `publicJob`: `plan132 {by, shots}`, `longDur`, `ext132 {joined, kept, dur}`, `extendOf`. Running count (`lib/plans.js`) leaves parts out.
- Plans (`lib/plans.js` and the page's `LIMITS`): Basic 30 s, Pro 60 s, Studio 120 s (free 5 s). Price is linear in seconds; `lib/billing.js` counts up to 16 parts.
- Page block `v132`: nooi Studio puts Length then Engine first in the chip row; Length chips 5 s … 2:00 (locked past the plan) + slider to the plan's cap, with "n connected parts · the agents plan it" and the cost; WAN 3.0 "up to 15 s a shot"; the home box's length goes to 2:00 (ticks 0 · 30 · 1:00 · 1:30 · 2:00). Gallery cards / preview: "Long video · n parts joined · m:ss" or "Extended · +n s", and in the lightbox "What the agents planned". "Extend…" in the lightbox and in the made-video menu (v131 long press / right click) → sheet +5 / +10 / +15 s, optional words, cost (`extSheet132` / `extGo132`). The crew (Studio): "One continuous film" (`crewFilm132`: the shots as connected parts, their engines, a bible from the cast and the look).
- Tests: `tests/v132.py` (ports 8372/8373; a stand-in engine and text AI: plan, frames, words, retry, the vision remake, one joined file with sound, silent stays silent, charged once, Pro 60 / 90 → 402, restart pickup, Extend, the Finish tool, the page on desktop and an Arabic phone). `tests/home_results.py` (bots off keeps 30 s), `tests/v131.py` (the made-video menu has Extend…).

## v133 Every engine for 1–2 minutes · joins that don't show (the seam engine)
- What was wrong: the parts were split by a fixed table, not by what each engine makes (Veo makes 8 s only — 1 minute came out 48 s; MiniMax 6/10 s — 45 s came out 30 s); a part after the first stayed on the engine's text-to-video model although it started from a picture (`I2V` was only chosen when the job was made); the next part started on the literal last frame (often blurred or fading); a plain join showed the engine's fade to black, the still frames at the start of a part, and jumps in colour and loudness.
- `lib/long132.js`: `shotLens133(model, routeKey)` = the lengths the engine really makes (from `providers/kie.js kieDur`, up to `PART_MAX`; `veo`/`veofast` 8 added); `planDurs133(total, lensOf, want, wantDurs)` = each part a length its own engine makes, together the asked length (more only when fixed shots don't add up: 60 s on Veo = 8 × 8 s), fewest parts, even, the shortest last; `longPlan133(body, keyOf)` (per part's engine when `meta.partModels`; the crew's `meta.partDurs` kept) → `meta.durs133` (each child's `dur`); `shotsTable133` → `/v1/config` `shots133` for the page. The agents' plan asks for shots that end mid-action; each continuation part's words say its first frame is already in motion.
- `lib/seam133.js`: frames read as small copies by frame number after the frame-rate step (exact times; a seek lands a frame early). `handoff133(url)` = the last clean, sharp frame (not fading, not a flash) → a full-size picture + where the part is cut; `lockColour133(img, anchor, strength)` = that picture brought part of the way back to the first part's colours (each colour's level and spread; 0.75 when the vision AI says it's the same film, 0.5 without one, none when it says the place changed) so drift doesn't pile up; `adherence133(url, img)` = did the part start on the picture it was given (≤ 0.16 apart, colour-evened); `joinSeamless133(parts)` = per join: where the next part matches the hand-off frame (its repeat and any still frames before the motion left out), the next part starts in the colours of the one before and eases into its own over 2.5 s (`SEAM_EASE`), a one-frame cut where they match, a short dissolve where they still differ (`SEAM_FADE`), sound crossfaded and levelled (±8 dB); `joinParts133` falls back to the plain join.
- `lib/jobs.js`: `createJob` plans with `longPlan133`; `start()` uses the part before's `hand133.img` (an Extend uses `handoff133` of the video and keeps `meta.baseCut133`), and switches a `kie@` text-to-video id to its picture-to-video model (`kieVariant`) once the part has a start picture; `continuity132` (finish of a part): hand-off frame → start check (miss → made again with `ADH_WORDS133`, a second miss on `FIRST_FRAME133` = Seedance 2.0 / WAN 3.0 / Kling 3.0) → vision check (v132) → colour lock; the parent joins with `joinParts133` (`seams133`, `join133`); Extend joins the same way (`ext132.seamless`). `publicJob`: `seams133 {n, matched, fades, colour}` (staff: `seamsDetail`).
- Page block `v133`: `shots133(m)` (server table, else `SHOT133`), `durs133` = the server's planner line for line; `parts132` and `longNote132` use it ("8 connected parts of 8 s — 1:04 in all", "5 connected parts (10 · 10 · 10 · 10 · 6 s)", Arabic); the home box plans up to 16 parts (`hfStudio`, the addJob wrapper, the Director's plan); the crew film sends its shot lengths (`partDurs`); the opened long video says its joins were matched (`.sm133`).
- Test: `tests/v133.py` (ports 8374/8375; a stand-in engine that makes each part from the picture it is given, with fades, still starts, colour drift, a loud part and ignored pictures).

## v134 Extend that can't be seen · another angle · voices & sound · the phone's prompt box folds away
- **Extend, two ways** (`lib/extend134.js`, `lib/jobs.js extend132/start/extendJoin132`): `meta.extMode134` = `same` (default) or `angle` (+ `meta.angle134`: auto, close, wide, side, over, top, low, reverse). Same movement: `lib/seam133.js motion134(url, cut)` reads the camera at the end (the simplest move that clearly fits frames 6 apart: still / slide / slide + zoom → push in, pull back, pan, tilt, and how fast) and how lively the action is → words added to the new part ("The camera keeps pushing in slowly…"); long videos do the same for every next part (`hand133.motion` → `p.motionFrom134`). Another angle: before the video, `anglePicture134` makes a picture of the same moment from the new camera position from the clean last frame (the picture engine, `nano`; stage `angle`; its price `ANGLE_PIC134` is added) and the video starts from it; joined with a clean cut (`joinSeamless133` part `{hard:true}`). Words, characters (`meta.chars134` → "Characters: name — look") and pictures (`refs130` → Seedance 2.0, chosen in `extend132`, which now runs before `checkRefs130`). `meta.extNoJoin134` (the editor): only the new part comes back, with `ext134 {cut, head}` (`headOf134`: the repeat of the frame and still frames skipped); a picture as the base (a clip that isn't on the server) starts the part directly. `kept` (an engine that returned the whole video) is checked after that.
- **Voices & sound** (`lib/sound134.js`, `lib/engine134.js`, `soundJob134` in `lib/jobs.js`): a video job with `meta.sound134 {job | src, scope: all | shot {from,to} | moment {at}, want {voice, sfx, amb, music}, lang, who: auto | narrator | characters, say, lipsync, keep}`. The agents (one text-AI request with 2–4 frames of that part: Director, Dialogue & Voice, Sound) write who speaks, what (in the language, for the ear), the feeling (`DELIVERY`), when, on screen or not, the place's ambience, up to N effects, a music cue; without a text AI: the customer's words by a narrator + the ambience. Voices: `lib/voice.js speak` (one steady voice per speaker, `castVoices`), each line fitted to its slot (≤ 1.25×, moved earlier or faded so it never runs past the shot); sounds via `runEngine134` (the engine for `sfx` / `music`, no separate charge), one that fails is left out (note) and not charged; lips: `lipRanges134` (only where someone on screen speaks, ≤ 4 stretches of ≤ 30 s) → the engine's video lip sync → put back in place; mix: the video's own sound ducked under the voices (or removed in the range), ambience, effects at their times, music ducked, −16 LUFS. Priced before (`POST /v1/sound134/quote` → total, parts, missing engines, the long video's `cuts`), held, lowered to what was really made. Stages look → voice → sounds → lips → mix; after a restart: failed, nothing charged. `publicJob`: `snd134 {plan, notes}`, `cuts134` (where a long video's parts meet), `ext134`.
- **Page block `v134`**: the phone's prompt box folds with an arrow on its top edge (`.fd134`, `S.fold134`, a 0.45 s slide; a swipe on it too; the box's content hidden after the slide, a slim tab stays; none on a computer). Extend sheet (`extSheet132` / `extGo132` replaced): Same movement / Another angle cards, angle chips, +5/+10/+15, the words, pictures (# names) and characters, the price (Seedance 2.0 and the angle's picture included). The editor's Extend button and the clip's menu open the same sheet for the clip (`extEditor134`; "extend: …" typed still extends at once); the new part lands after the clip, the clip ends at its clean frame, the new part starts where it really begins. Voices & sound sheet (`snd134Sheet`): Whole video · One shot (the long video's parts, or from–to) · One moment (a slider), what to add, language, who speaks, lips, the words, keep the video's sound, the price from the server; offered on the opened video, a made video's long press and a clip in the editor (the clip takes the new video). Cards say each stage; the opened video lists "What you hear".
- Tests: `tests/v134.py` (ports 8376/8377; a stand-in engine that makes voices, sounds, music, lip sync and angle pictures, and a stand-in text AI as the sound team). `tests/v131.py`: the made-video menu has Voices & sound….

## v135 Merge videos so the join can't be seen · edit a video with words · the playhead follows the finger · the folded prompt box goes away
- **Seam engine (`lib/seam133.js`)**: where two parts are the same scene but still don't quite meet, a **morph** instead of a dissolve — `MORPH_N` (env `SEAM_MORPH_FRAMES`, default 4) frames from each side are replaced by frames worked out between the two with motion interpolation (`bridge135`: ffmpeg `minterpolate` on the picture sequence A,A,B,B; the next part's frame colour-matched); the parts overlap the bridge on identical frames (no frame lost); a bridge whose steps are bigger than the video's own motion allows (`lim`) falls back to the dissolve. `SEAM_MORPH=false` turns it off. **Every crossfade in the chain now pads the video so far with copies of its last frame** (`tpad`): a part whose last frame fell a hair before a 1-frame join used to end the whole picture there (a 1-minute Veo video came out with 15 s of picture).
- **Merge (`meta.merge135`, `mergeJob135` in `lib/jobs.js`)**: items `[{job | src, in, out}]` (the caller's own finished videos, or a file on this server), 2–12, up to 10 minutes; style `seamless` (default) or `cut`. A video whose extension is also picked is left out (the extension holds it — `dropped`); a part the editor made from a video goes on from its `ext134.cut` / `ext134.head`. Each video trimmed to in–out (`trim135`), cut at its hand-off frame (seamless), joined by `joinParts133`; free; stages `ends` → `join`; `merge135` on the job (n, style, seamless, morphs, cuts, fades, dropped).
- **Edit with words (`meta.edit135`, `editJob135`, `lib/edit135.js`)**: `{job | src, from, to}` + the words (the change) + pictures (`refs130`, # names → @Image1…) and characters (`chars134`). The seconds chosen (at least 4; whole video by default, up to 2 minutes) go to **Seedance 2.0** as `reference_video_urls` (`@Video1`, providers/kie.js branch for `meta.edit135`) with `editWords135` ("Edit @Video1: … Change only that…"), in parts of up to 14 s one after the other (`editPlan135`; each part sent whole seconds when the video has them, the part before's last frame as one more picture). Each made part fitted to the seconds it replaces (`fitPart135`), put back with a 0.5 s dissolve where the edit starts, between parts and where it ends, the **video's own sound** kept (`assemble135`). Priced like Replace the person (`basePrice("video","mcswap")` per 5 s sent), shown first (`POST /v1/edit135/quote`); nothing charged when it fails; stages `cut` → `edit` (parts) → `join`.
- **Film strip (`POST /v1/media/sprite`, `lib/scrub131.js sprite135`)**: small frames of a clip (longer side 320 px, up to 12 a second, one JPEG ≤ 3840 px a side, made once). The editor shows the strip's frame while the playhead is dragged (no video jump at all) and until the video lands where the finger stopped (`sp135`, `hook131`/`px131` replaced, a `drawFit` wrapper). The light copy (v131) stays the fallback when there is no strip.
- **Page block `v135`**: Merge videos… (the opened video, a made video's long press): your videos as tiles in a fixed order, picked in order (numbers), the two ways to join, free. Edit with words… (same places, and a clip in the editor — the clip takes the edited video, its trim kept): the words, the whole video or from–to, pictures and characters, the price. Editor clip menu: Edit with words…, Join with the next clip, seamlessly, Merge all clips into one (one clip takes their place). The play button follows a drag that stops the video; the timeline, picture and transport can't be selected by a long press. Phone: folded, the prompt box slides away completely (no strip over the videos or the menu); a small arrow (`.fu135`) floats just above the bottom menu and brings it back (a tap or a swipe up). The v91 rule that gave editor jobs the project's shape no longer touches extensions, merges or edits (an extension keeps the clip's own shape).
- Tests: `tests/v135.py` (ports 8378/8379; a stand-in engine whose edit is a tinted copy of the video sent). `tests/v131.py` (the menu has Edit with words… and Merge videos…; the copy check runs without a strip), `tests/v134.py` (unfold with the new arrow), `tests/v91.py` (Extend goes through the sheet).

## v136 Films made faster and never lost · Extend that really continues · merges at one frame · the agents in order · the prompt boxes
- **Fast films (`lib/jobs.js` `FAST136`, `keyframes136`)**: a film in several parts (meta.long132) no longer waits part after part (six Kling 3.0 parts took over an hour). The first picture of every shot is drawn first on the picture engine (`runEngine134("image",{model:"nano"})`, all at once: the first from the customer's first frame or made, the others from it and the customer's # pictures), then every part starts at the same time from its own picture (`pimg`, meta.fast136), joined with clean cuts (`hard`). Not charged (part of the film). Off with `LONG_FAST=false`, or per film with meta.continuous (the page's "One continuous take" in the length sheet / the Video page's length section: every part from the last frame of the one before, slower). Stage `frames` while the pictures are drawn.
- **A film is never lost (`partialFilm136`)**: a part that fails (after its retries) no longer fails the film — once nothing is being made, the parts that were made are joined and only their share of the price is charged (`lowerHold` → `settle`); `job.partial136 = {made, of, missing:[part numbers], reason, secs, full}`. `againQuote136(job)` prices the missing parts (their share of the full price; 0 for staff); `POST /v1/jobs/:id/again136` holds it and makes them again (stage `again`), then the whole film is joined and that price charged; a part that fails again is released.
- **Extend continues the shot (`extendCheck136`)**: after the engine, the new part's first frames are compared with the frame it was given (`adherence133`); a new shot is made again once from the same frame, on an engine that keeps a first frame (`FIRST_FRAME133`); a second miss is joined with a clean cut (meta.extCut136, said on the card). Off with `EXT_CHECK=false`.
- **Merges at one frame (`lib/seam133.js`)**: two different shots meet with a clean cut (before: a ⅓ s dissolve — a double picture); a dissolve only where the frames nearly meet (`SEAM_NEAR`, 0.07), a failed morph → a cut.
- **Page block `v136`**: stage lines (`frames`, `again`, "N of M shots ready — all made at the same time"); a film with a part missing says so on its card and the opened video with "Make part N again · price"; the agents work one after another (`step136`: Quill, Nova, Max while planning, Lumi on the first pictures, Lyra, Echo, Remy as the work goes on, Duke and Iris last; `busyBots` no longer lights only the Director and the Cinematographer; ✓ on the ones before), their row wraps and shrinks to stay inside the box. Home box: no Agent tab (Video first), the nooi chip reads "nooi Auto"; a row at the top holds the start frame, the # pictures (`refs130`, shared with the Video page), "+ Picture", Clip (starts where one of your videos ends — its last frame) and Characters, the words below at full width; typing # lists the pictures inside the box (`hp136`). nooi Studio: the same row at the top (+, Clip, Characters); the folded box no longer flashes open when the page is drawn again (`nt136`); the words keep the cursor when the page is drawn again.
- Tests: `tests/v136.py` (ports 8380/8381; a stand-in engine that makes several at once, refuses a marked shot, ignores the frame on one engine). `tests/v132.py`–`v134.py` run the continuous-take engine (`LONG_FAST=false`; v132's engine returns the same clip whatever frame it gets, so `EXT_CHECK=false`), `tests/v130.py` (the row at the top is always there), `tests/v121.py` (the box opens on Video).
- **Replace things (`lib/scan136.js`, `lib/replace136.js`, `lib/jobs.js` `replaceJob136`)**: `POST /v1/scan136 {job|src, lang, hear?}` → what is in a video or a picture: a few frames to the analysis agents (the vision AI — the Continuity supervisor lists people, animals, vehicles, buildings, objects, places and writing: a name in the customer's language, an exact English description, the frames, a box → where it is in words, when it is seen, a small picture), the sound measured here (loudness, spectral flatness, syllable pulse every ¼ s → speech / music / ambience / effect; the Sound agent names them); without a vision AI a short list from the words it was made from (`by: "basic"`). Kept next to the file (`<file>.scan136.<lang>.json`). `hear` adds the words said (speech-to-text, priced per minute like a transcript, held then charged; 501 when no speech-to-text is connected). `POST /v1/replace136/quote` prices the changes; a job with `meta.replace136 {job|src, lang, changes:[{id, to | remove | pic | char | voice, say}]}` makes them: a video's seen things in the seconds they are seen (`changeSpan136` → `editPlan135` → `editSeconds135` with `visualWords136`: "Edit @Video1 — change only these things: 1. Replace … (where) with … — the same movement, gaze and lips…", pictures as @ImageN, characters), then the sounds (`soundWorks136`: a voice in another voice, new music or sound at the same level, a sound taken out — the original lowered softly there; `soundMix136`); a picture with the picture engine (picture 1 + up to 3 pictures). After: `check136` (the Continuity supervisor, before/after pairs) → `replace136.checks`. Price: the edit's (Replace the person per 5 s) for the seconds + each sound (voice/music/sfx); a picture: one picture. Stages `cut` → `edit` → `join` → `sound` → `mix` → `check` (a picture: `picture` → `check`).
- **Page**: "Replace things…" on the opened video and picture, a made video's or picture's long press, a clip in the editor (the clip takes the result). The sheet: what is in it, grouped (People, Animals, Vehicles, Buildings, Objects, Places, Writing, Sounds), each with Keep / Change / Remove (a person can become one of your characters; any thing from a picture; speech: new words and a voice); "Hear what is said · price"; "What changes" as a numbered list; the price; Make. The result says "What was changed", each with the supervisor's check.

## v137 The look of your pictures, clips and words kept · Replace things that sees with any vision AI

- **Style (`lib/intent.js` hook, `STYLE_MATCH136`, `NAMES_STYLE136`, `pics136`)**: the hidden page defaults (`S.draft.style:"cinematic"`, `S.img.style:"photo"`) no longer add style words over pictures — a job with reference pictures (video: `startImage`, `vStart`, `vEnd`, `vRef1…6`; image: `iRef…iRef4`, `chRef`, `eRef`) gets "Keep exactly the visual style of the reference picture(s): a photo stays a photo, a cartoon stays a cartoon, a 3D animation stays 3D…"; a style the customer picks (`meta.styleChosen136`, set by the page `STY136` when a style tile is tapped) or names in the words ("anime", "pixar", "oil painting", "realistic", Arabic too) wins. The first-frame words (`frameWords`) say "the same kind of image". No forced "Photorealistic" any more: film first pictures (`keyframes136`), Edit with words (`lib/edit135.js`), Replace things (`lib/replace136.js`) say "exactly the visual style of the first picture / @Video1". - **Replace things analysis (`lib/scan136.js`)**: the frames go to the vision AI as ONE contact sheet (`<file>.s136.grid.jpg`, ffmpeg `tile`, frame 0 top left, white gaps) — vision models that read one picture per request (NVIDIA) now work; boxes in any form (`box136`: 0–1, 0–1000, pixels, x1y1x2y2, Gemini `box_2d`); a flat (one-colour) crop is cut again from the whole frame (`flat136`). A failed look is not kept (no cache), the sheet says so (`failed`), staff see the reason (`why`); a kept "basic" list is looked at again once a vision AI is connected. Sounds show as sounds with a time bar. "Something not in the list": up to 4 things described in words (`x1…x4`, `custom`), changed or removed in the whole video. Words in Arabic ("to what") are put into English before the engine (`english136`). `check136` sends one before/after sheet (a row per change).
