## v138 What the server check-up showed: the engine out of credits, engine hiccups, EACCES while updating

- **Out of credits (`lib/alert138.js`)**: a job that fails with the engine's "Credits insufficient" (402) marks that connection Out of credits at once (`settings.status.kie`, no wait for the 15-minute check) and keeps `settings.alerts138.kie {since, last, count}` (a film counts only its parts). Staff get it in `GET /v1/me` (`alerts138`, read as `engine`): a red line at the top of Admin (how many jobs failed, since when, customers not charged, what to do) and a short note once a visit (page block `v138`). The owners (`ADMIN_EMAILS`) get one email at most every 6 h when email is set up (never from the preview). It clears when a job is made on that engine again, or the Admin connection test passes. The connection test reads an empty balance (`data <= 0`) as out of credits, not "online". Customers: `USER_MSG.quota` is now "The AI engine can't take new work right now — the site's team has been told…" (Arabic on the page and in `errorAr`); a film whose part failed for that reason says that sentence, not "Part 1 of 6 couldn't be made… or a shorter video".
- **Engine hiccups (`lib/engine134.js` `again138`, `passing138`, `NO_CREDITS138`)**: an engine step the engine fails on its own side ("Internal Error, Please try again later", 5xx, busy, a dropped connection) is asked again twice (4 s, 12 s; `AGAIN138_MS` scales it in tests) — every `runEngine134` step and the voices of Voices & sound and Replace things. Never on out of credits, a refused key, a wrong request, a refusal, an unsafe picture or "took too long".
- **Updates (`deploy/update.sh`, `deploy/stage.sh`)**: `mktemp -d` makes a root-only folder (700) and `rsync -a NEW/ APP/` gave `/opt/nooi` the same until `chown` — the running site got "EACCES: permission denied, stat /opt/nooi/public/v1/posts" for those seconds. The unpacked folder is now 755 first. `update.sh` takes `APP`/`BINDIR`/`PREV` from the environment and installs its own `nooi-update`/`nooi-backup` after a good update (written beside, then moved in place); `stage.sh promote` runs the update steps of the version being put live (`$STG/deploy/update.sh`).
- **Check-up (`deploy/doctor.sh`)**: a last section "What needs you" in plain words — the engine out of credits (refusals in 24 h, film parts that stopped, what to do), EACCES during an update (nothing to do from v138), the engine's own failures, the site's own out-of-credits mark and the last connection check; "✓ Nothing above needs you." when all is well.
- Tests: `tests/v138.py` (ports 8384/8385; a stand-in engine that can run out of credits and fail a voice; update/preview/check-up dry runs with stand-ins for the root-only tools). `tests/providers_mock.mjs`: the plan lengths are v132's (Basic 30 s, Pro 1 min, Studio 2 min).

## v139 The editor exports frame by frame · projects on your account · the check-up reads the engine's balance

- **Export (page block `v139: THE EDITOR EXPORTS FRAME BY FRAME`)**: the old export played the timeline and recorded the screen (MediaRecorder): as slow as the video, softer than the preview, broken by a phone going to sleep. `edRender139(total)` (called first by the base `edExport`; on any failure or without WebCodecs the recording runs as before) draws every frame with the editor's own drawing (`edTick` with `R139.mine`): for each frame `needs139(t)` lists the clip under the playhead (`edAt`, ramps included), the next clip near a transition and the video layers; `fetch139` decodes those exact moments with mediabunny (`dec139`/`at139`: one `CanvasSink` per piece of media, frames read in order; a file it can't open → the video element moved there and copied, `el139`); an outermost `drawFit` / `edxDrawLayers` wrapper swaps the video for the decoded frame (as v99's held frame does) — a moment drawn that wasn't foreseen is read and the frame drawn again (`R139.miss`); the GPU transitions get the next clip's first frame in `ff131`. During the export the loop doesn't draw (`edTick` wrapper) and the videos aren't moved (`syncClipMedia` wrapper). Size: the format's own (up to 4K) — nothing has to keep up in real time; VP9/VP8 (software on most devices) at most 1920 on the long side. Codec: H.264 in MP4 when the browser can also play it, else VP9/VP8 in WebM; `latencyMode:"realtime"` ("quality" mode made ~1.5 frames a second on real pictures). Sound: `mix139` (OfflineAudioContext, 48 kHz stereo) — clips (volume, speed with the pitch kept: `stretch139`, overlap-add), music looped, the voice-over, sound effects, layer sound; laid in second by second. A finished MP4 already at the exact size goes up as it is (no second encode, v119 wrapper); a WebM is converted by the server as before. `R139.stats` = how the last export was made. Library: mediabunny (MPL-2.0, unmodified) at `/vendor/mediabunny.mjs` → `/vendor/mediabunny-<version>/`.
- **Projects on your account (`lib/projects139.js`, page block `v139: EDITOR PROJECTS KEPT ON THE SERVER`)**: `GET /v1/projects`, `GET|PUT|DELETE /v1/projects/:id` (`[a-z0-9]{6,24}`; ≤ 200 projects, ≤ 4 MB each; not through a connection token). The document `{v, ed, look, media}` is the editor state minus the selection/tab/render-check keys, every file as a `/media` address (`doc139(upload)`: a file only on the device is uploaded at "Save to my account"; autosave marks it `local`); clips made here carry `src.url139` for a device that doesn't list that result. Files: `data/projects139/<sha1(account)>/<id>.json`; the list in the account's record (`projects139`); deleting the account deletes them (`account125.js`). Page: a Projects button beside Export video (icon only under 560 px) → sheet (save, list, open, delete with a second tap); `open139` keeps the work open now in the device's projects (PJ) and builds the media from the addresses (`slotUrl139`); a project on the account saves itself 6 s after a change (one timer, nothing sent when the document didn't change).
- **Check-up**: `credits139` reads the engine's balance as a number, text or inside an object (the live answer gave no plain number, so "OK" with no balance; an empty balance never showed); `deploy/doctor.sh` says when the last refusal was and "already solved" when the connection was checked fine after it.
- Tests: `tests/v139.py` (port 8386: colours per second → frame-exact, 550 Hz kept at 2×, the delivered MP4, the mix, the recording without WebCodecs, the projects API, two devices, autosave, account deletion, the check-up). `tests/v93.py`: the export size is checked on the recording or on the frame export.

## v140 The editor, combined (part 1) — eight groups · every AI tool in one place · film LUTs and colour by colour · clean voices · subtitle files
The owner asked to take the best of three open editors and put them together in ours (ours stays the base: it works on phones and keeps every AI feature). Ideas only, written in our own code: OpenCut (MIT) — tools in a few groups, subtitle files; DonkeyCut (Apache-2.0) — every AI tool in one place, LUT and per-colour grading; Concat (AGPL — nothing of its code) — one switch that cleans up voices. Page block `v140: THE EDITOR, COMBINED (part 1)` (before LOOP + BOOT).
- **Groups (`G140`, outermost `VIEWS.edit` wrapper)**: the 18 tabs in eight groups (AI · Clip · Text · Audio · Color · Effects · Layers · Export; an unknown tab goes to Effects). The category row `.cat140` (`data-act="cat140"`) goes above the tab row; the tab row shows only the open group's tabs (the others stay in the page with `data-h140`, hidden — tests and code that set `S.ed.tab` keep working); a group with one tab has no sub-row (`one140`). A group remembers its last tab (`S.ed.last140`, not saved in projects). Phones: the groups are a bar at the foot, above the menu (`--bn140` = the menu's height), CapCut-style; the sub-row scrolls with the tools. Also fixed: `--edsh` (the pinned preview's height) is followed with a ResizeObserver (`RO140`) — it was measured once, before the video loaded, so what was pinned under the preview slid behind it.
- **AI tools (`ai140` tab, `panel140`, `EDX_PANELS.ai140`)**: cards with a price pill each (a number · Free · "Priced first" for tools that quote in their own sheet): Extend (`ed-extend91`), Edit with words (`editSheet135`), Replace things (`rpSheet136`), AI edit a frame (→ `ai` tab), AI voice-over (`edai-vo`), Voices & sound for this clip (`snd134Sheet`), sound effect / music from words (`#edaisfx`/`#edaimu` + the existing `edai-sfx`/`edai-mu`), Clean up voices, Auto captions (→ `words` + `edx-cap-auto`), Translate (`#edtrl`/`#edtrtx` + `edai-tr`), Dub (→ Subtitles page, dub tab, `S.subs.src` = the clip's job), Lip-sync (→ Voice page with `M.lsV` = a copy of the clip's file: its own blob URL, so the page can let go of it). Tools that need the clip on the server use `base140(c)`: a clip made here is its job; a clip you uploaded is uploaded once (`API.upload`, kept in `m._srv139`) and registered by **`POST /v1/clips140`** (server.js): only a `/media/<name>` file in the account's own `uploads` list, the same file gives the same job back (`clip140:true`, status done, kind video/image by extension); the page keeps a local job (`meta.clip140`, `c.srv140`).
- **Pro colour (`c.cg.lut`, `c.cg.lutAmt`, `c.cg.hsl`)**: ten built-in film LUTs (`LUTS140`, colour formulas `lutFn140` turned into 17-point LUTs, ids `b:<name>`) and your own `.cube` (3D only; `cube140` reads it — TITLE as its name — and keeps at most 17 points a side, 8-bit, base64 in `S.ed.luts140["u:<id>"]`, at most 8 per edit; saved in projects with the edit). Colour by colour: 8 bands (`HSL140`, hue ±30°, saturation and lightness ±100) for the picked band (`S.ed.band140`). Drawn by WebGL1 (`gl140`/`grade140`: the frame as a texture, the LUT as a strip texture n²×n with blue between slices, HSL weights by hue distance × saturation) in an outermost `drawFit` wrapper on `#wk` only: a clip drawn fully is graded in place; a clip drawn faded (cross-fade) is drawn on its own canvas first (`GL140.o`, id `wk` so the inner wrappers still treat it as the preview), graded, then laid down with the caller's fade. Same in the export (v139 draws through `drawFit`). No WebGL → the colour tab says so and nothing is graded.
- **Clean up voices (`S.ed.clean140`)**: switch in the AI tools and at the top of the Audio tab. Preview: each clip's video element goes through `audioNode` → high-pass 85 Hz → −3 dB at 280 Hz → +3.5 dB at 3.2 kHz → −1.5 dB shelf at 9 kHz → compressor (−18 dB, 3:1; the browser adds its own make-up gain) → ×0.8 (`vcBuild140`), only while the audio engine runs (a tap starts it) and only for media it may read (blob/data/same-origin/crossOrigin). Export: a `mix139` wrapper mixes everything else as before (clip sound off for that call) and adds the clips' sound through the same chain plus `denoise140` — a spectral gate (1024-point FFT `fft140`, half overlap, √Hann in and out; the noise measured on the quietest tenth of moments; gain ≥ 0.15, held 0.6 per step and smoothed across 3 bins; nothing done below −80 dB). Test: ≥ 10 dB less hiss where nobody speaks, the voice within 2 dB.
- **Subtitle files (Auto captions tab)**: Import `.srt`/`.vtt` (`subsIn140`: BOM, CRLF, hh:mm:ss or mm:ss, `,` or `.`, cue settings, NOTE blocks, tags removed) → words spread over each cue (`edxSpread`), captions on; Save `.srt`/`.vtt` (`subsOut140`: a cue per pause > 0.6 s, 7 words or 42 characters) through `saveFile`.
- Tests: `tests/v140.py` (port 8390). `tests/v82.py`: the agents' faces (`sav`) are artwork, not icons; `tests/visual82.json` refreshed (home since v136, the editor's new rows). `pro_tools.py` now takes ~340 s (the AI tools tab adds clicks) — give it P≥400 in batches.

## v141 The editor, combined (part 2) — waveforms · snapping · opacity & volume keyframes · soft layer edges · fonts · shapes
Page block `v141: THE EDITOR, COMBINED (part 2)` (ideas from OpenCut and DonkeyCut, own code).
- **Waveforms (`paintWf141`, after every `renderView` in the editor)**: clip blocks with sound, the music block (looped over the timeline), the voice-over and each sound effect get an SVG of their sound (`wfGet141`: the loudest sample per 1/50 s; decoding `decode141` shares `AUD139` with the export; sound effects through `edxBuffer`). Drawn once decoded, never re-renders the page.
- **Snapping (`snap141`, called from the timeline drag handler "5) timeline: drag a block…")**: while moving or trimming, the edge snaps within 8 px to 0, the playhead, the end of the timeline (not for a clip's own trim), clip edges, texts, infographics, effects, layers, regions, sounds and caption words; a dashed line (`.snap141`) shows where. The magnet (`data-act="snap141"`, toolbar) turns it off (`S.ed.snap141=false`).
- **Opacity and volume keyframes**: `edxMo` also returns `o` (0–100) and `vk` (0–200), interpolated like the transform keys (old keys count as 100); Motion & zoom has Opacity and Volume sliders (`mo.o`, `mo.vk`; volume for video clips only). Opacity: an outermost `drawFit` wrapper fills black and draws the clip with that alpha (inside a cross-fade the alphas multiply). Volume: the preview sets the playing clip's element volume; the export runs the clips through v140's `vcClips140(total, clean)` with a gain curve (`setValueCurveAtTime`, 50 points a second) whenever a clip has volume keys or Clean up voices is on.
- **Layers (`edxDrawLayers` base edited)**: shape `oval`; `Ly.feather` (Soft edge, 0–100): the layer drawn on its own canvas and cut by the shape blurred (`lySoft141`); `lyPath141` draws every shape (also the border, now rotated with the layer).
- **Fonts**: 38 more Google Fonts in `FONTS` (14 Arabic, 24 Latin) — 102 in all, in the font sheet.
- **Shapes (info items `type:"sh141"`)**: rectangle, circle, triangle, star, arrow, line, speech bubble, heart — in the Infographics tab (`data-act="sh141-add"`, class `sh141t`, not the stickers' `st131t`); fill = the item's colour, `line` outline colour, `lw` outline width (the line's thickness), `ar` width, `rot`, `op`, `rad` corners (rectangle), `fill:"none"` for an outline only, words inside (`t1`). Drawn by `shDraw141` through an `edInfoDraw` wrapper; draggable on the preview (`ED.hit`).
- Also: `/v1/posts` and `/v1/accounts` no longer fail for an account record without those fields.
- Tests: `tests/v141.py` (port 8392).

## v142 The new editor page (part 1 of the rebuild) — one page in one piece
> Its layout (groups rail, `.ne142`, `G142`, `tests/v142.py`) was replaced by v143's CapCut / DonkeyCut layout the same day; what stays from it: the preview's size in the HTML, the tool row's fixed label height, the page held while it is drawn again, labels that wrap.
The owner asked for the old editor page (37 stacked layers of HTML patches, overlaps and glitches) to be deleted and the editor built again as one clean page, keeping every working tool. Done in stages: v142 is the new page; v143 rebuilds the tool settings and the timeline as clean functions and deletes the old `VIEWS.edit` chain. Page block `v142: THE NEW EDITOR PAGE` (before LOOP + BOOT).
- **`VIEWS.edit` (new)**: the page is `.view.ne142` → `.ne-top` (h1 `.ne-h` + `.ne-act`: format `.fmtbtn`, Projects `.pj139b`, Export `.edout`) → `.ne-body`: `.ne-rail` (8 groups, `data-act="cat142"`), `.ne-stage` (`.edstage` with `#wk`, `.transport`, `.ne-cmd` = the AI prompt bar `.edcmd`/`#edcmdq` + `#edplan91`, on every group), `.ne-tl` (`.edbar` + `.tlwrap`), `.ne-side` (`.ne-sub` tabs of the open group only, `[data-h142]` hides the rest; `.ne-panel[data-tab]`). For now the settings, the toolbar and the timeline are still taken from the old page (`OLDED142()` rendered into a `<template>`) — the DOM contracts (`#wk`, `#tlin .blk`, `data-set="ed.tab"`, `.edbar` buttons) stay as the handlers expect.
- **Groups (`G142`)**: Media (`media`: `media142()` = add bar + the clips, `data-act="clip142"` opens a clip's settings), AI (`ai140`, `ai`; the command chips `.edcmdx` on top), Edit (`clip`, `motion`, `track`), Text (`text`, `words`, `info`, `logo`), Audio (`audio`, `sfx`; Record voice + Add music row on top), Color (`color`, `look`), Effects (`vfx`, `erase`, `layers`, `3d`), Export (`export`). Names of the sub-tabs in `T142`. Back to a group → the tool used last in it (`S.ed.last142`, kept in `UI139`).
- **A tap on a timeline item** opens its settings when the open tab isn't one of its own (`OWN142`: a text → Text, a layer → Layers, a sound → Sound effects, …).
- **Layout**: computer — grid `78px | 320–400px | 1fr`, rail and settings sticky on the left (right in Arabic), preview with the timeline under it. ≤1023 px — one column (preview, timeline, rail, settings). Phone (≤767) — `.ne-stage{display:contents}` so the preview (`.edstage`) stays the pinned element; order preview · player · prompt bar · timeline · settings; the rail is a bar fixed at the bottom just above the site's bar (`bottom:var(--bn140)`); a slim title row (18 px) over format (the size, e.g. 1080×1920; the name is the button's label) · Projects · Export; tapping a group scrolls its settings right under the pinned preview (where the preview sits once stuck: `#main` top + sticky `top` + its height).
- **The page never moves when it is drawn again**: `#wk` gets the player's size in the HTML (it used to start at 300×150 and grow a frame later, so `#main` was shorter for a moment and its scroll was clamped — a right-click menu then closed on that scroll), the toolbar labels keep their height when the price turns into a credit icon (`min-height:14px`), and an outer `renderView` wrapper holds `#main`'s height while the editor is drawn.
- Labels never cut: rail names and the phone title wrap (`script_fonts.py`: the editor page has no clipped text in any script).
- Tests: `tests/v142.py` (port 8394, ~65 s). Updated for the new page: `v91.py` (slim header ≤ 84 px, chips in one row on the AI group), `v140.py` (groups on `.ne-rail`), `pro_tools.py` (clicks inside `.ne-side`); `tests/visual82.json` refreshed (editor pages).

## v143 The editor in the CapCut / DonkeyCut layout (rebuild, part 2)
The owner asked for the editor to look and work like DonkeyCut and CapCut with every nooi feature kept (comparison and stages in `docs/EDITOR_PLAN.md`; ideas only — DonkeyCut is Apache-2.0, CapCut closed: nothing of their code, artwork or words). Page block `v143: THE EDITOR — CAPCUT + DONKEYCUT LAYOUT` replaces the v142 block in place (no new layer on top).
- **Computer (≥1024 px, `.ed143.desk`)**: grid `rail | add panel | player | details` with the timeline under the last three, the rail down both rows; `.e-app` is sized to the window (`size143`) and every panel scrolls inside. Top bar `.e-top`: h1, project name `.e-name` (`data-bind="ed.title"`, saved with server projects), format · Projects · Export. Rail `TABS143` (`data-act="lt143"`): Media · Audio · Text · Stickers · Effects · Transitions · Captions · Filters · AI; each with its panels (`.e-sub`, `data-act="ls143"`; names in `N143`; last panel per tool in `S.ed.ls143`). Details `.e-details` follow the selection (`kind143()`): clip → Edit (`clip`) · Motion · Tracking (`dt143`, remembered in `S.ed.dt143`); text, graphic, logo, effect, region, overlay, sound effect, music (`aux143="music"`), captions (`aux143="cap"`) → their panel (`KIND143`); nothing (or `S.ed.tab="export"`) → Project (`export` panel). × (`desel143`) deselects. The timeline's ruler stays on top while lanes scroll (desktop), the playhead knob above it.
- **Phone & tablet (<1024 px, `.ed143.mob`)**: player pinned (`.e-player{display:contents}`, the old sticky `.edstage`), play row with guides · full screen · undo/redo (moved from the tool row; no `.edbar` on phones), prompt bar, timeline, then the **sheet** `.e-sheet` (the panel you work in, ✓ `pclose143` closes it — `S.ed.pclosed143` = the panel it was closed on, so any other panel opens it again), and the fixed **tool bar** `.e-bar` above the site's bar: the add tools + Project + Snap, or — with something selected (`.e-bar.ctx`) — its own tools (`CTX143`): clip → ‹ · Edit · Split · Speed (opens Edit at Speed) · Volume · Motion · Filters · Transition · Freeze · Mirror · Duplicate · Earlier · Later · Extend (with its price) · Tracking · AI · Delete · Snap; text / graphic / effect → Edit · Split · Duplicate · Delete; overlay / region / sound / logo → Edit · Delete. A tap on a clip shows its tools (its settings stay closed unless you were in them — `TAP143` = the panel before the tap); a tap on a text, graphic, effect, overlay or sound opens its settings.
- **Panels**: still the old page's (`OLDED142` → `pane143(tab, nopick)`; the ADD view is drawn with only the clip picked). New ones: Media `media143()` (add bar, your finished videos and pictures as tiles `lib143` → `edUseJob`, the clips `clip143`), Transitions `trans143()` (the selected clip's `edclip.trans` tiles alone), Assistant `chat143()` (DonkeyCut idea: `S.ed.chat143`, saved with the project — an `edGo91` wrapper logs what you asked and what it did from its toasts; the chips under it). On a computer a panel never shows twice: the same panel left and right only for text, sounds and overlays (`SAME143`), and no element id twice (`ids143`), else the left says it is open on the right.
- **Selection**: `kind143` takes the panel you work in with its own old-style selection first (`selText`, `selLayer`, `selReg`, `selSfx`), then `S.ed.pick`, then music/captions, then the clip; an `edSetPick` wrapper keeps one selection at a time (picking a clip lets go of the text, …).
- **Guides** (DonkeyCut idea, `guides143`): off → thirds → centre → safe margins → short-video keep-out zones, an SVG over the picture (`placeGuides143`). **Full screen** `fs143`.
- Tests: `tests/v143.py` (port 8394, ~75 s; replaces `v142.py`). Updated: `v140.py` (rail and details, phone tool bar), `v91.py` (header ≤ 96 px, chips with the assistant), `v131.py` and `editor_real.py` (the phone's tool bar instead of the tool row), `pro_tools.py` (all 21 panels, `.e-body[data-tab]`), `equal_layout.py` (`.e-bar`, `.e-sub` scroll sideways); `tests/visual82.json` refreshed (editor pages).
- Known: `editor_pro.py`'s phone step "effects: tile, AI edit and prompt bar" lost the first effect in 2 of ~9 runs (passes alone and in batches otherwise; not reproduced with tracing) — watch it.

## v144 Drag everything · news tickers · text styles · smoother transitions · background · voice
The owner asked: "drag and drop effects, banners, line effects, sound isolation, AI-powered audio, background removal, background addition, smoother transitions and news tickers — drag everything". "Line effects" was read as text/font effects (text styles). Page block `v144: DRAG EVERYTHING …` (before LOOP + BOOT) plus small edits in place:
- **Drag & drop** (`DND144`, `DK144`, `DRAG144`): pointer events on the window (capture). Mouse: a drag starts after 6 px; touch/pen: a 320 ms long press (a finger that moves first scrolls). A ghost follows (`.dg144`), the timeline shows a drop line (`.dl144`) or outlines the clip (`.dz144`), the picture gets a dashed outline. Drop on the **timeline** → `t=(x−#tlin.left−40)/zoom`; on the **picture** → the playhead and where you let go (infos get `x,y`; a text gets top/centre/bottom). How a drop applies: `ED.t` set, the clip picked when needed, then the tile's own `click()` (so every existing handler does the work). Kinds: `media` (`lib143` → inserted between the clips there), `clip` (looks `edx-look`, film looks `lut140`, clip effects `edx-vfx` — never turned off by a drop — zoom moves `edx-zoom`), `trans` (`edclip.trans` → the clip before the nearest cut), `text` (`ed-addtext`, `ts144`), `info` (banners, stickers, shapes, infographics, tickers), `time` (`edv-add`, `edx-sfx-add`, `mg-pre`). A click right after a drop is swallowed (`DND144.block`); Escape cancels. Tiles in a **picker sheet** (`.pk84s`, phones and the details panel) drag too: the sheet hides (`body.dnd144s`) and its hidden source tile on the page (`pkSrc144`) does the work. On a **computer the add panel shows its tiles** (`pk84apply` skips `.ed143.desk .e-assets`), and while an effect or a graphic is selected the add panel keeps its tiles (`SAME143` + vfx/info; the details then hold only `.edsel`).
- **News tickers** (`TK144`: breaking, live, sports, markets, weather, offers; info type `tk144`): band + label on the reading side (right for Arabic-script text) + headlines (`t2`, one per line or •) crawling in the reading direction (`sp` 1–10), top/bottom (`tk144-pos`), band colour (`tk144-bg`), label colour = the info colour. 8 s at the playhead (`tkAdd144`). Timeline label "Ticker".
- **Text styles** (`TS144`, 14): font + weight + colour + effect (`efx`, `hl`) + motion; an Arabic-script text gets the style's Arabic font. A tile in the add panel adds a text in that style (`tsAdd144`); in the details/sheet it restyles the selected text (`tsSet144`). Shown under Add text.
- **Transitions**: each clip's length `c.trd` (0.2–2 s, default 0.6, at most 45 % of either clip) — `trW144(c,next)` — and smootherstep `ease144` in all three transition engines (the plain fade/dip/slide in `edTick`, the motion ones in the EDX `drawFit`, the graphics-card ones). Transitions panel: length slider + "Use on all clips" (`trd144-all`).
- **Background** (clip details tab `bg144`, phone clip tool "Background"): `bgRun144(c)` samples the clip (3 frames a second, 2–48) and runs the on-device "bg" model (`laiCall`, free) → `BG144[c.id].frames` (cut-outs, in memory; done again by itself when the page is opened again). An `edxSmooth` wrapper draws this frame cut out with the nearest mask over: a colour (`col`), a gradient (`BGG144`), a blurred copy, or a picture (slot `bgp_<clip>`, saved with server projects via `slots139`). "Show the original" turns it off.
- **Voice / AI audio** (Audio → "AI audio" panel `aiaudio144`): **Isolate the voice** — `iso144Run` uploads the clip (`base140`), sends job kind `isolate` with `meta.from/to`; the server hook (`lib/isolate144.js`) cuts that part's sound to an mp3 (refuses a clip without sound, before any charge), sets `meta.secs144`; `KIND_TO_CAP.isolate` → the AI engine's voice isolation (`kieAuto`); price = `ENGINE_COST.fixed.isolate` (estimate $0.05) per started minute (`priceOf`), shown on the card and the button first. The result lands under its clip as a voice sound (`voice:true`) and the clip's own sound goes off (`c.vol144` keeps the old level). **Music ducks under voices** (`S.ed.duck144`, level `duckLv144`, default 30 %): speech found in voice sounds (50 ms loudness windows, `voiceSpans144`) → the music's volume in the preview (`edTick` wrapper) and in the export (`mix139` wrapper mixes the music itself with the same gains). Voice-overs are marked as voices; any sound can be ("This is a voice"). Also there: clean up voices, AI voice-over, sound effect, music.
- Tests: `tests/v144.py` (port 8395 + stand-in AI engine 8396, ~200 s; stand-in on-device AI). Updated: `v143.py` and `v140.py` (Background in the clip details and phone tools), `v84.py` (the editor's add panel on a computer shows tiles, no chip).

## v145 The clip's settings rebuilt clean · new clip tools
Stage 3 of `docs/EDITOR_PLAN.md`. Page block `v145: THE CLIP'S SETTINGS, REBUILT CLEAN` + edits in place.

Transition tiles come from the lists (`transTiles145`: cut/fade/dip/slide + `EDX_TR` + `GLT131`, icons from the picker registry), also in the Transitions panel (`trans143`). Phone clip tools (`CTX143.clip`): Edit · Split · Speed · Volume · Animation · Motion · Filters · Transition · Crop (`clip#crop`) · Rotate · Reverse · Freeze · Mirror · Duplicate · Earlier · Later · Extend · Background · Replace (`clip#replace`) · Extract audio · Tracking · AI · Delete; `showSheet143` opens a sheet at any `[data-anc]`; the bar's labels stay on one line.
- **Crop & rotate**: `c.crop={ar,z,x,y}` (aspect `CROPS145` or free, zoom 1–4×, position −1…1 → `cropRect145`) and `c.rot` (0/90/180/270), drawn by an `edxSmooth` wrapper (outermost: after smooth frames and the v144 background) into one canvas ≤ 1920 px — preview and export.
- **Canvas behind a clip** `c.cv145={mode:auto|fill|fit|blur|color|pic,col}` (picture slot `cvp_<clip>`, saved via `slots139`): the plain `drawFit` reads `CUR145` (the clip the EDX `drawFit` is drawing).
- **Animations in & out** `c.ain/c.aout={k,d}` (`AN145`, 0.1–3 s, at most half the clip): `anim145()` adds scale/position/turn/blur in the EDX `drawFit` (next to the motion and transitions) and a see-through `drawFit` wrapper (over black). A tap plays the moment.
- **Audio**: fades `c.afi/c.afo` (0–5 s) — preview in a `syncClipMedia` wrapper, export in `vcClips140` (one gain curve with the volume keyframes); **voice changer** `c.vc145` (`VC145`: deep −5, high +4, chipmunk +9 semitones — a two-delay-line pitch shifter `pitch145`; robot ring modulation; echo; radio/phone band + soft clip; big hall convolver) — preview: the clip's element through `vcFx145` (`vcRoute145`, coexists with clean-up's routing), export: the same nodes on the OfflineAudioContext in `vcClips140` (the v140 wrapper routes there when `fx145()`). **Extract audio** `xa145`: the clip's part decoded (speed kept via `stretch139`) → a WAV sound at the clip's start, the clip muted (`c.vol144` keeps the level).
- **Replace** `rep145`: a file (`data-rep145`) or one of yours (`repSheet145` → `rep145-pick`) — length (capped at the new media's), effects, look, motion and transition kept; masks and reverse cleared.
- **Reverse** `rev145`: the clip goes up (`base140`), `POST /v1/edit145/reverse` (`lib/edit145.js`: only the client's own file, ≤ 2 min, cut in 4 s pieces, each `reverse`/`areverse`, joined last-first, H.264/AAC, registered like a clips140 job) → the clip points at the reversed file (`c.src0`, `M["rv0_<clip>"]` keep the original); Reverse again gives it back with no server call.
- Tests: `tests/v145.py` (port 8402, ~95 s). Updated: `v143.py`, `v140.py` (the tabs and phone tools), `editor_real.py` (volume in Audio), `v99.py` (speed in Speed), `v84.py` (the transition chip on a clip with a next one), `pro_tools.py` (26 panels; `rev145`, `bg144-run`, `iso144`, `aa144` not clicked — it now takes ~480 s: run it alone with P≥560).

## v147 AI extras · auto reframe · stabilise · remove silences · background on the AI engine · @time and @selection
Stage 5 (the last) of `docs/EDITOR_PLAN.md`. Page block `v147: AI EXTRAS` + `lib/edit147.js` + in place: the crop wrapper passes `lt` to `cropRect145`; `ownFile` exported from `lib/edit145.js`.
- **Auto reframe** `reframe147(ar)` (9:16 · 1:1 · 4:5 · 16:9, in the AI tools under *Fix & reframe*): the format changes and each clip of another shape gets `c.crop={ar,z:1,x,y,px147:[[lt,x,y],…]}`. `subj147` samples 3 frames a second at 96 px: column/row profiles of edges weighted by movement (or the removed background's mask when there is one), the peak's centre; a centred moving average (a steady pan stays exact, the ends too) and a 2 % dead zone. `cropRect145` follows the path in time; moving the position slider drops it. Busy flags live in `BUSY147` (not in the project).
- **Stabilise** `stab147(c)`: the clip goes up (`base140`), `POST /v1/edit147/stabilise {url,from,to,strength 1–3}` — ≤ 3 min, `format=yuv420p` first (vid.stab reads planar YUV only), two passes `vidstabdetect` → `vidstabtransform` (smoothing 15/30/60, `optzoom=1`), or `deshake` + a small zoom when this ffmpeg has no vid.stab; H.264/AAC, registered like a clips140 job. `c.stab0` / `M["st0_<clip>"]` keep the original (Show the original). An ffmpeg failure answers 422 with a plain message (free tool: no "credits refunded").
- **Remove silences** `sil147(c)`: `decode141` the clip's sound, RMS per 20 ms, threshold 12 % of the 95th percentile (≥ 0.004), pauses longer than `E.silMin147` (0.3/0.5/1 s), 0.12 s kept each side → the clip replaced by its spoken parts (`edCopyMedia91`; fades in/out on the first/last, the transition on the last). Undo puts it back.
- **Background on the AI engine** `bgEng147(c)` (Background panel card `.bge147` and the AI tools): 3 frames a second (pictures: one, 1536 px), nine 512 px tiles to a contact sheet (8 px grey gaps), one `bg` job per sheet (`meta.bg147`, price `PRICE_TABLE.fixed.bg` per sheet, shown first); the `edUseJob` wrapper cuts the returned sheet into masks (`bgSplit147`) into `BG144[clip]` (`eng147:true`) — the v144 drawing uses them as it uses the device's. `c.bg144.eng147.sheets` keeps the URLs: opened again, `bgReload147` reads them back free (also via the `bgRun144` wrapper).
- **The assistant's @** `at147(q)` in `edGo91`/`edGoInfo91` wrappers: `@0:05`, `@0:05-0:08` (new texts/effects/graphics get that start and end), `@playhead`, `@selection`/`@التحديد` (delete · duplicate · move to @time · group · ungroup — `selRun147` — or a clip change run for each selected clip through `edPlan91(... + " clip N")`), `@clip 2`, `@<marker name>`/`@marker 1`. Arabic digits read. The plan line says what it will do; the thread keeps the text with its @. An @ button (`at147-open`) and typing @ open a list (`atList147`: the playhead, the selection, the markers, the clips).
- Tests: `tests/v147.py` (port 8406 + a stand-in engine on 8407 that cuts out bright pixels; ~110 s; the steady copy is checked with ffprobe — the test Chromium can't play H.264).

## v148 AI agents edit the editor's projects · several timelines in one project · a brush for the cut-out
The three Concat ideas still missing (from its feature list only — none of its code: AGPL; see `docs/THIRD_PARTY.md`). Page block `v148: AI AGENTS EDIT THE PROJECT` + `lib/edit148.js` + in place: `save139` sends `opN`, `open139` clears `seq148` before the opened project, `restoreMedia` keeps the files of every timeline (`allClips148`), `pvTools` is off while the brush paints, the v144 background step calls `brush148`, the v143 page puts the Timelines button in the tool row (computer) or its own row (phone, 2+ timelines), `nob146` has Timelines.
- **Agents (server)** `lib/edit148.js`: 11 MCP tools in `lib/mcp.js` (`TOOLS148`; the writes in `WRITE148` obey read-only connections): `nooi_editor_projects`, `nooi_timeline_read` (size, length, clips, texts, markers, other timelines), `nooi_preview_frame` (ffmpeg JPEG ≤ 640 px of the clip at that moment, as MCP image content), `nooi_clip_add` (a finished video/picture of the user's, trimmed, at a position), `nooi_clip_trim` (in/out/speed/volume), `nooi_clip_move`, `nooi_clip_remove`, `nooi_text_add`, `nooi_text_edit`, `nooi_marker_add`, `nooi_format_set`. A clip is its `clip_id` or its number (1 = first). Each change is an operation `{n, op, args, at, by, tl}` applied at once to the saved project (`applyOp148`) and kept in `data/projects139/<user>/<id>.ops.json` (last 100); `GET /v1/projects/:id/ops?after=N` → `{seq, ops}`. `HOOK139.beforeSave` (in `lib/projects139.js`): a PUT with `opN` lower than the latest gets the newer operations applied on the way in, so a save from an editor that hadn't seen the agent's work keeps both. Operations are idempotent (ids checked; set values). `tl` = the timeline open when the project was last saved: applied to that one (`edOf`) even after the editor switched.
- **Agents (page)**: `poll148` every 4 s while a project on the account is open in the editor (not while a pointer is down, a stroke, a menu, a sheet or typing; 30 s pause after an error) → `op148(op)` (the same rules; `clip_add` loads the file with `slotUrl139`) → a toast and a line in the assistant thread ("Your AI agent added …"); undo works on it. `S.ed.opN148` = the last operation seen (UI-only: in `UI139`); `open139` reads `seq` first. The assistant panel says agents can edit the project (link to Integrations); Integrations lists the editor tools with an example.
- **Several timelines** (`E.seq148={cur, list:[{id, name, data}]}`, made on first use): each timeline has its own `SEQF148` fields (clips, texts, sfx, vfx, infos, layers, regions, caps, format, fit, marks146, groups146); the music, voice-over and colour look belong to the project. The open one lives in `S.ed` as before (`data:null`); switching (`seqGo148`) swaps them, deselects, playhead 0, fresh undo. New: empty (`seqBlank148`) or a copy (`reid148`: new ids for every item, groups remapped; `dupMed148` copies their files — `c_ ly_ sx_ bgp_ cvp_ st0_`, IDB, decoded sound, background masks). Rename in place (`[data-seq148n]`, Enter/Escape), delete with a question (its own files go too), up to 12. One button `.seq148b` (the open one's name + how many) opens the menu (`seqMenu148`). `slots139`/`doc139` wrappers put every timeline's files and job-clip addresses on the account; a clip whose file came from the account keeps it (`c.src.srv148`, `srvBack148`) so a reload finds it. Export, the agents' `nooi_timeline_read` and the project list use the open timeline.
- **Brush** (Background panel, once removed): Off · Keep · Remove, size 2–24 %, Undo the last stroke, Clear. Strokes `c.bg144.brush148=[{m:"keep"|"cut", r, p:[[x,y]…]}]` in the picture's own 0–1 positions (r = radius / the long side; ≤ 200 strokes, ≤ 800 points). `brush148(y,el,w,h,B)` after the mask: Remove = `destination-out`; Keep = the frame's own pixels inside the stroke drawn back; in order, every frame, the export, device or engine masks. Pointer → picture: the outermost `edxSmooth` wrapper records the crop and turn (`MAP148`), the `drawSrcAt` wrapper the canvas transform and box; `srcPt148` inverts them (a 90° clip: the top of the preview is the left of the picture). The selected clip must be under the playhead (else it moves there). A ring shows the brush (lime keeps, red removes).
- Tests: `tests/v148_agents.mjs` (server, port 8412, 26 checks, ~2 s) and `tests/v148.py` (port 8414, ~110 s: live agent changes, saves keep both, undo; timelines copy/switch/rename/empty/delete, on the account and on another device, after a reload; the brush with mouse and finger, a turned clip, undo). `tests/v143.py`: the phone bar has Timelines.

## v149 What the owner found on a phone: captions, translation, other devices, the phone editor, sticker groups, sound for the whole edit
Page block `v149: WHAT THE OWNER FOUND ON A PHONE` + `lib/edit149.js` + `lib/sound134.js` (stems) + in place: `save139` (automatic saves upload the files too and send `base`; a 409 asks), the `API.req` wrapper keeps a 5xx reply that carries `errorAr` (the server's own words — before, every 5xx became "The AI provider is temporarily unavailable", which hid why translation failed), TLX pointer handlers (`tlTouch149`), `edVfxDraw` lightning/dust, `lib/projects139.js` (`devAt`, `base` → 409).
- **Captions**: `edxDrawCaps` measures each word slot (widest of the word and its stroked/boxed form + padding) and shrinks the size when a line doesn't fit — words never run into each other (every style, Arabic too). `edxAutoCaps` hears the whole edit in parts of at most 26 s cut at the quietest 20 ms frame (`win149`), skips silent parts, in the chosen spoken language (`E.caps.lang149`, "Spoken in" select).
- **Translation** (`tr149`): this device first — the browser's own translator (`self.Translator`, the language downloads once; every step has a time limit, `tmo149`), else the on-device model (`local-ai-worker.js` task `translate`: Opus-MT pair, then M2M-100), else `POST /v1/translate149` (numbered lines, asked again for missing ones, 60 a part, any text AI that is set up; plain 503 when none). The original words are kept (`E.caps.orig149`, `t.orig149`) with "Back to the original words". The panel says which way it will translate.
- **Other devices** (`DV149`): a signed-in edit with clips goes to the account by itself 5 s after the first change (switch in Projects: `S.cloud149`), its files too. `sync149` (entering the editor + every 30 s): the open project newer elsewhere → brought in when this device has no changes of its own since, else a banner (Open the newer one · Keep mine); an empty editor opens the newest project; another project changed in the last 7 days → banner. The server keeps `devAt` (last save from a device; the agents' changes move only `updated`) and refuses an automatic save whose `base` is older (409) — that device asks instead of saving over.
- **Phone editor**: the play row pinned under the preview, `#main` scroll-padding so what is brought into view lands below it; the timeline at most 46 vh with its own scroll; empty lanes 22 px; a touch on the timeline stops playback (the playhead can be dragged back), the playhead at the edge scrolls the timeline (`edge149`). Effects that did nothing: Invert and Comic without a colour look, Lightning (a real flash and bolt), Dust (more, bigger).
- **Stickers** in 7 groups (`STG149`; 51 new Material Symbols — 89 icons in all); **sound effects** play on a tap and go in only with Add (a hold-and-drag still drops them); export sizes on one line (`fmtTile`).
- **Sound for the whole edit** (AI audio panel, `sdCard149`): voices, actions, ambience, music for every clip — each clip one part of its file on the v134 sound team with `stems:true`; the server returns separate tracks (`stems149`: voice cut to the clip, ambience looped with fades, music, each effect) + the lip-synced picture; they land on the timeline at the clip's place (`sdLand149`), lines become captions when there are none. Price first: `POST /v1/sound149/quote` (free for staff).
- Tests: `tests/v149_sound.py` (ports 8424/8425, mock engine + text AI, ~25 s) and `tests/v149.py` (port 8422, ~120 s: caption gaps EN/AR × 4 styles, 40 s heard in parts, translation + back + failure message, effects, two devices — autosave, open elsewhere, update, ask, Keep mine, the behind device asks; phone checks + scans). Older tests updated for the new behaviour: v131 (sticker groups), v139 (an autosaved project in the list), v146/editor_real (toasts, pinned play row), editor_pro (translation route).

- **Motion scenes** are infographics of type `mg150` (`sc`: logo · trail · phone · counter · board · call · nodes · words · end; `t1`, `t2`, `items` "a | b", `val`, `ic`, `accent`, `bg` dark|none, `ics` icons of the nodes) drawn by `mgDraw150`/`SC150` under the other graphics (`edInfoDraw` wrapper) — preview and export alike; `bg150` is the dark background with the accent's glow. Edges on the music's beat when it has one (`beatsAt150` from `S.ed.beats146`, `snap150` within 0.45 s); "Fit to the beat" (`rebeat150`) after music is added. `S.ad150` keeps the brief.

- **Mixer** (Audio tab, `EDX_PANELS.mix150`): clips, music (`musicVol`), voice-over (`voiceVol`), effects and master — fader, mute, meter; `E.mix150={clips,sfx,master,mute}`. Preview: the last `syncClipMedia` wrapper multiplies the element volumes and sets `EDX.bus`; export: `mix139` wrapper scales the levels while it mixes and puts them back. Meters read the v141 peaks (`WF141`) at the playhead × the fader (`lvl150`, no extra sound graph).

## v152 Frames, creative filters, new cuts, camera, title motions, sounds — and the editor's agents
The owner asked to take from OpenMontage (AGPL — ideas from its public description only, none of its code read), cinetic (MIT) and Cutia (MIT) what nooi didn't have: "filters and frames, features, functions, sounds, motions… the way to deal with agents". Page block `v152: FRAMES · CREATIVE FILTERS · NEW CUTS · CAMERA · TEXT MOTION · SOUNDS · THE EDITOR'S AGENTS` (before LOOP + BOOT) + the shared module `public/editor152/studio.js` + `lib/review152.js`.
- **Frames** (`FR152`, `frame152`, an `overlayVignette` wrapper on `#wk` — under the words, preview and export): `S.ed.fr152 = {id, col}`; polaroid, card, phone, old TV, film strip, neon, viewfinder, vintage, comic, border, double line; colour swatches for neon/border/double/polaroid. Panel `fr152` in the Effects tab (`TABS143`), saved with the project (not in undo).
- **Creative filters** (`F152`, one WebGL shader `gl152`, `fx152`): a `drawFit` wrapper after the v140 colour pass, per clip `c.f152` + `c.f152a` (amount, the `data-edx="f152a"` slider); duotones, colour pop (hue), halftone, posterize, sketch, thermal, night vision. Tiles in Filters (after the looks) drawn from the clip itself (`fxThumbs152`).
- **New cuts** (`TR152`, `trCompose152`): a `drawFit` wrapper composing this clip's picture and the next clip's first frame (`ff131`) — wipes and slides in 4 directions, iris, bands, dot, horizon rise, polarity flip, zoom out, pixel dissolve; tiles appended by a `transTiles145` wrapper, descriptions in `PKV84.transition`.
- **Camera** (`CAM152`, `camKf152` keyframes via an `edxZoomPreset` wrapper): breathing zoom, pull-back reveal, push with a roll, impact kick here, drift, crane down, handheld, whip in — inserted before Reset by an `EDX_PANELS.motion` wrapper (keeps v141's opacity/volume keyframes; never reassign `EDX_PANELS.motion=edxMotionPanel`).
- **Title motions** (`TM152`, a `drawTextLayer` wrapper for `anim:"t152_<id>"`): scramble, mask rise, blur rise, one-word slam, slice glitch, tracking in; they end on the plain text. Tiles `.tiles.tm152` after the Animate.css ones (v131 still counts exactly 20 there).
- **Sounds** (`EDX_SFX` 18 → 30: tick, tock, pluck, bell, thump, snap, key, swell, subdrop, sparkle, zip, paper; an `edxSynth` wrapper, `"pluck:k"` = a pentatonic note, normalised to ≤ 0.9) and the **sound pass** (`soundPass152`, presets calm · crisp · minimal in `PRESETS152`): sounds on the cuts, each text and graphic, the last card; items carry `sp152`, a new pass replaces the old one, the user's own sounds stay; `edxSfxName` reads `"pluck:3"` as Pluck.
- **The editor's agents**: expert roles `RL152` (director → story, design, editing, sound — Cutia's director idea) as chips at the top of the assistant (`chat143` wrapper, `S.ed.role152`, each its own examples `RX152`), and the **Review** panel `rv152` in the AI tab: `review152()` (shared) gives findings `{id, lens, sev P0/P1/P2, t, en, ar, fix:{op,args}}`, a score and a ship gate (cinetic's critic); the moment button seeks, a fix is one tap (`applyFix152`: text_add/time/pos/size/font, clip_mo, trans_all, mix, sfx_remove, sound_pass; `open` switches tabs), "Fix all" works in up to three rounds (a fix can cause another finding) with one undo step.
- **Agents over MCP** (`lib/review152.js`, imported by `lib/mcp.js`): `nooi_editor_guide` (no project — handled in `mcp.js` before `call148`), `nooi_review` (read-only), `nooi_review_fix` (rounds, ids made on the server so the open editor gets the same), `nooi_sound_pass`, `nooi_style_set` (transition/filter/frame checked by `applyStyle152` against `TRANS152`/`FILTERS152`/`FRAMES152`); ops `review_fix`, `sound_pass` (explicit `sfx`), `style_set` in `OPS148` and in the page's `op148`. `timeline148` now shows a clip's `filter` and the `frame`.
- Tests: `tests/v152.py` (port 8442, ~150 s: phone EN/AR, computer + an export checked by colour), `tests/v152_agents.mjs` (port 8436). Updated: v151 (30 sounds), v140 (the AI tab lists `rv152`). Known flake, also on v151's code: v145 "slide in from the left" on the computer — the test browser's video player stays stuck mid-seek on that page.

## v153 Style kits, more review checks, letter roll & typing, shutter/split/whip cuts, split at scene changes
What was still missing from OpenMontage (AGPL — public description only) and cinetic (MIT) after v152. Page block `v153: STYLE KITS · MORE REVIEW CHECKS · …` (before LOOP + BOOT) + shared code in `public/editor152/studio.js` + `lib/studio153.js` (imported by `lib/mcp.js`).
- **Style kits** (`KITS153`, `applyKit153` shared; panel `kit153` in the Effects tab): clean, flat, minimal, cinema, retro, neon, social, doc — each sets the clips' `trans`/`trd`, `f152` or `cg.look`, `S.ed.fr152`, every plain text's `anim` (not shapes with `kind`) and a sound pass (`sp152` items replaced); `S.ed.kit153`. Tiles drawn from the clip (`kitThumbs153`, `frame152(ctx,W,H,t,F)` now takes a frame override) with a sample title in the kit's colour (`KA153`). One undo step; `snap71` now keeps `fr152` and `kit153` and a `histGo` wrapper restores them.
- **Review checks** (in `review152`): `slideshow` (editing, P1 — ≥4 clips, two of: ≥60 % still, lengths within 15 %, ≥50 % pictures (`opts.pics`, page from `clipMedia`, server from `doc.media`)) → `clips_mo` (camera moves that alternate, `moveKf153`); `wordy:<id>` (>12 words) → `text_split` (ids: `mkId` on the page, `args.id2` = `uid()` on the server); `safe:<id>` (vertical, a text placed by hand at y<0.1, y>0.8 or the right edge) → `text_move`; `cliche:<id>` (`stock153`, EN + AR stock phrases) → `open` that text.
- **Title motions** `t152_roll` (letter strips rise, RTL aware) and `t152_cursor` (typing with a blinking bar) — an outer `drawTextLayer` wrapper; **cuts** `shutter`, `split`, `whipblur` (a `trCompose152` wrapper; in `TRANS152`). **Sound pass**: typed titles (`TYPING153`) get "typing"; crisp has a riser (`big`) 1.5 s before the 60 % cut (kept next to the cut's whoosh).
- **Split at scene changes** (clip Edit row, `data-act="sc153"`, video clips): `sceneCuts153` samples 48×27 frames every ≥0.2 s (≤300 samples), luma + colour-histogram distance, local maxima over max(0.12, 4×median), refined by 3 halvings; `splitAt153` uses `edSplit91` (keyframes split) and re-keys the media slot to a given id. Agents: `nooi_clip_split_scenes` (ffmpeg `select='gt(scene,t)'`, sensitivity fewer/normal/more, the server's file only) → op `clip_split {id, cuts:[{t,id}]}`; `nooi_style_kit` → op `kit_set {kit, sfx}`. The guide lists the kits.
- Tests: `tests/v153.py` (port 8444, ~140 s: phone EN/AR, computer + export), `tests/v153_agents.mjs` (port 8437, a real 3-scene file). Updated: v152 (8 title motions).

## v154 Replace the person holds to the end, a real face, phone layout fixes
The new person sometimes stayed only for the first frames (Seedance makes the whole video again and drifted back to the old person). Server: `lib/mcprep.js`, `lib/jobs.js`, `providers/kie.js`, `providers/fal.js`, `server.js`; page block `v154: Replace the person — …` (before LOOP + BOOT).
- **Face up close** (`faceCrop154`, run with the look in `prepMotion`): the text AI finds the head box (`FACE_SHAPE`); not when unsure or already a close-up (box area > 0.3); ffmpeg crops a square 1.7× the head (`min(min(iw,ih),…)` — ffmpeg's `min` takes two values) to 640×640 `-face.jpg` → `inputs.mcFace`, `meta.face154`. Seedance (kie + fal) gets it as the last reference picture and the words name it (`@Image<n> is the same new person's face up close`).
- **Words** (`seedanceSwapWords`, H3's summary): the new person from the very first to the very last frame, never the old one again (turns, fast moves, out of view and back, partly hidden); a real face (skin texture, pores, eye moisture, no smoothing/plastic look, the video's own sharpness, grain and noise).
- **The check** (`holdCheck154`): photo of the new person, the old one (`mcWho` or the video's middle frame) and 4 result frames (12/38/64/90 %) → `HOLD_SHAPE {frames: new|old|other|none, sure}`; `held` unless sure and ≥1 "old". In `finish()` (one-part swaps only, before the sound): `hold154()` sets `stage:"check154"`, and when it didn't hold makes it once more on `TRACK_MODEL` = `wan/2-2-animate-replace` (Wan 2.2 Animate Replace, frame-by-frame tracking; `job.first154`, `job.remade154`, `job.prov` changed, `start(job)`). Not when: the text AI can't look / isn't sure, a person picked among several (`mcWho`), no Kie key, already on Wan, the switch off. Charged once (the engine's cost of the remake is the site's). The remake failing → `fallback()` finishes with `first154` and `note154 {en, ar}` (`back154`). `publicJob`: `remade154` (while it isn't back), `note154`.
- **Admin → Overview → Speed**: `hold154` row (On/Off, `POST /v1/admin/speed/hold154 {on}`, `providers.manage`; `HOLD154=false` turns it off by default on a server); `wan` in the engine list (`SWAP_MODELS.wan`).
- **Page**: stage texts `check154` and the remake ("follows the person frame by frame"); `note154` on the card; a `line132` wrapper keeps Motion Control jobs' own step texts (v134's "The agents are watching and listening…" had replaced "Looking at the video and the photos…"); results in their real shape — `job.ar154 = [w, h]` from the video/poster (`loadedmetadata` / `load` capture listeners) used by `previewCol` (width `min(100%, 70vh × ratio)`) and `card98` (gallery) — a phone video no longer sits in a wide black frame. Phones: `.go118` (a tool's pinned main button) on a solid bar (was a see-through gradient over the words), an empty preview a 120 px strip; editor top bar: `.e-name` min 84 px, Export's second word (`.xw154`) hidden ≤440 px, the bar never wider than the screen (EN + AR); `.e-sub` tab rows fade at the edge that goes on (`fe154`/`fs154`) and scroll the open tab into view (`subs154` in `after151`).
- Tests: `tests/v154.py` (ports 8451/8452, ~70 s: stand-in engine that can fail Wan, stand-in text AI, a customer charged once, page on a phone EN/AR). Updated: v103, v111 (the face call is one more text AI call).

## v155 A result never left "being made"; the engine's words keep what was asked
- **Lost jobs** (page block `v155: …`): a phone that closed or reloaded the page while a request was on its way left the page's copy without `remote` — "being made" for ever (Home's agents stuck on Remy via `live136`/`step136`, "1 in progress"), while `syncServer` added the finished job as a separate `meta.synced` card. The page already sends `cid` = its job id (`serverSubmit` wrapper of v96); `publicJob` now returns `cid`. `link155()` (every 20 s, 1.5 s after load, on returning to the page; only when there are candidates): a job with no `remote`, queued/rendering, sent through `serverSubmit` (`sub155`) or of a server kind (`KINDS155`), not being sent now (`SEND155`), older than 15 s → found by `cid` in `/v1/jobs` → takes `remote`, status (`st155`), url, progress, stage; the sync's copy (`meta.synced`, same remote) is removed. Not on the server after 10 min → `failed`, `errorType:"unsent"`, en/ar message (nothing was charged).
- **Only what was picked** (`lib/intent.js` `picked155`): the page sends `meta.chosen155` — the Type / Style / Camera keys tapped (`data-set` clicks or `data-bind` changes, kept in `S.draft.chosen155` / `S.img.chosen155`) or set by a page to a non-default value (`DEF155`: video story / cinematic / dollyIn, image photo; e.g. UGC ads' type). Only those become words ("Made as …", the style line, the camera line); a job without `chosen155` (older page, API/MCP) keeps all, as before. `styleChosen136` also follows `chosen155`.
- **Agents** (`lib/agents.js`): the plan asks for `request` (the brief in English, nothing added or dropped) and rules (start with it, never add a story/twist/people/objects/text/logo, keep a named camera/shot/style; techniques optional; Iris checks nothing was added); `keepRequest155(request, prompt)`: prompt kept if it holds ≥80 % of the request's words (and ≥60 % near the start), request prepended if it holds 50–80 %, the request alone below 50 % (drifted). A short single shot (≤10 s, 1 shot) gets only the Director's and Cinematographer's techniques (`team(…, short)`). `lib/long132.js`: the Writer carries the idea across the parts and adds nothing it doesn't ask for (was "a story with a beginning, a middle and an end").
- Tests: `tests/v155.py` (ports 8463/8464). Updated: `tests/agents.py` (a real clip for the storyboard join and a realistic wait; v136's agent order — both had been failing since v132/v136).

## v156 Agents never stuck; a tap keeps its place; sheets pull down; the box picks characters and pictures; the publishing policy
- **Stuck agents** (page block `v156: …`): `timed156` gives up a request — GET 25 s, `/v1/llm|agents/` 2 min, other sends 5 min, uploads 10 min (`T156`) — by aborting the fetch and rejecting (`timeout156`); applied to `API.req` and to v124's batched job check `JB124.o` (a hung `/v1/jobs/live` or `/v1/jobs/:id` had kept `poll109` busy for good: no later check ran, so a finished film stayed "being made" and Home's agents stayed on). `busyBots` wrapper resets a below-zero `AI_BUSY` (the long-film plan set it to true/false while a counted reply was open → Quill "working" for ever). `stale156()` (every minute): a page job queued/rendering with `remote` for > 6 h, or without `remote` for > 30 min and not being sent (`SEND155`) → failed, `errorType:"stale"`, en/ar message. Server (`lib/jobs.js` `sweep156`, every `SWEEP_MS` = 60 s): a check lock (`_busy`) older than 20 min is let go; a queued/rendering job past `STUCK_MS` (75 min) — `STUCK_LONG_MS` (3 h) for long films, their parts, Motion Control/swaps, sound/merge/edit/replace — fails with the reason (`noFallback`, the hold given back as for any failure).
- **A tap keeps its place**: a capture `scroll` listener remembers the inner lists scrolled in `#main` (`SCR156`); the `renderView` wrapper restores them (key: id, else tag + classes + index) when the view is the same — not inside `.ed143` except the phone `.e-bar`, whose active tool is also scrolled into view. (The engine list in nooi Studio and the editor's tool row went back to the start after every tap.)
- **Sheets pull down** (touch only): `#modalRoot .sheetwrap>.sheet` from anywhere while its scroller is at the top (not on inputs/sliders/canvas), the editor's phone `.e-sheet` by its `.e-ph` bar; follows the finger, closes past 28 % / 140 px or a quick flick (`close156`: clicks the wrapper's `close-sheet`, the editor's `.e-ok`), else springs back. Phones get a grab handle.
- **Sliders in sheets**: `touch-action:none` (a fast sideways drag was taken as a scroll), `#hfdr` 36 px tall, `#hfdnote` min-height; on the first touch on a sheet's slider the sheet's height is fixed (the note's text changed its height and pushed the slider up under the finger).
- **Effects**: `fx156` (after151) moves the selected effect's settings (`.edsel` with `[data-vfxamt]`) above the effects to add, labelled "Selected effect", with "Starts here" / "Ends here" (`edv156`, at the playhead).
- **The box**: `top136` wrappers — the Characters chip first in the row as a tile (`.ch87b.t156`; it was past the row's edge), Picture is a button (`pic156`) opening `picSheet156`: from the device (`.pkup156` input `data-ref130 data-hf136`), your characters (`avatar106`), your finished pictures; a pick is fetched and handed to a hidden `input[data-ref130][data-hf136]` (DataTransfer) so v130/v136 add it as a # picture. The @ list: `pos156` places it in the visual viewport (above the keyboard; below → above → over the bottom), `at87Close` ignores scroll-caused closes (`window.event.type==="scroll"`), repositions on `visualViewport` resize/scroll; a touch on a name picks only on a tap (window-capture touchstart stops v87's pick-on-touchstart; touchend picks if moved < 10 px).
- **Policies**: `LEGAL.publish` "Publishing & Content Policy" (en/ar: your content your responsibility, what you confirm, not allowed, indemnity, reports and removal), in `DOCS` (`legalTabs` wrapper adds its tab). The footer's email moved under the policies (big footer: after Terms · Privacy · Publishing policy; small and landing footers: last). Share to Explore: a confirmation checkbox (`xpagree156`); the page sends `agree:true`; `lib/explore.js` refuses without it (400, `errorAr`) and keeps `agreed156`.
- Tests: `tests/v156.py` (port 8467; CDP touch drags). Updated: `tests/v120.py` (3 policy links). Note: `tests/v144.py` phone "long press drags an effect tile" fails at v155 too (the drag works when checked on its own).

## v157 Character voices in the box; a promo from a picture and a word; the Explore creator program
- **Voices** (page block `v157: …`): a Voice tile in the box's top row (Video) opens `vcSheet157` — 20 library voices (`VOICES91`, ▶ free sample via `/v1/voice/sample`; the pick is kept on the first @character as `voice.vid`), or a voice clip (`M.vc157`, kept in `KEEP_SLOTS`) used as it is ("lips") or cloned ("clone", consent checkbox required), optional words to say (else the "quoted" words of the prompt). The price comes first from `/v1/sound134/quote` with `dur` (a quote ahead, no video yet — `server.js`). On send the first video of the send carries `vc157` (the clip is uploaded at once → `vc157.clip`); a 3-s watcher submits, when that video is complete, a v134 sound job on it (`meta.sound134`: who characters, lips, voice / clip + clipUse + consent; priced by the server first; missing engines → said, nothing sent). Server (`lib/sound134.js`): `clean134` takes `voice` (library id), `clip` (/media path) + `clipUse` lips|clone + `consent`; lips: no voice planning or charge, the clip laid at t0 as the voice track (`clipVoice`), on-screen → the lips step; clone: the clip → mp3 → `cloneVoice` (ElevenLabs), the main on-screen speaker speaks in it (`cloned:true`), dropped after; a library voice is cast for the main speaker. `ready134`: no voice engine needed for lips; "voice clone" missing without ElevenLabs (`canClone` in `lib/voice.js`). `soundJob134`: the clip must be on this server; clone without consent → 400 (en/ar).
- **Promo** (`word157`): a word in "quotes" with a promo word (promo/advert/commercial/teaser/إعلان/دعائي/ترويج…), or after word/slogan/title/كلمة/عبارة/شعار/اسم, is drawn on the page (`titlePng157`, the film's shape, Arabic shaped by the browser, white with a warm glow and a line), uploaded, and sent as `meta.title157 {url, word}` on the first video of the send; the agents' plan gets `title157` (rule: never write that word or any text; an advert; a calm hero frame at the end). Server: `lib/v88.js` adds the no-text line even when the words name a word; `lib/intent.js` adds the advert line; `lib/promo157.js` `title157` (from `labelAI`, before the mark/credentials, never on a part): the last 40 % (0.8–3 s) darkens 35 % and the word fades in (0.7 s), in place; failure → handed over without it (`job.title157.error`).
- **Creator program** (`lib/explore.js`): share with `program:true` (the share sheet's "Join the creator program", on by default, says what it gives) → `program157`, the creator's public id `pid` (never the account id), `uses157`. `POST /v1/explore/:id/use` (Recreate / "Use this prompt"): once per person, not the creator → +2 credits (`CR157.use`, ≤100 a day); featured → +50 once; followers 10/100/1000 → +20/+100/+500 once. `GET /v1/creators/me|:pid`, `POST /v1/creators/:pid/follow` (not yourself), `POST /v1/creators/:pid/message` (message or "collab", moderated, 2–1000 chars, 20 a day), `GET /v1/inbox`, `POST /v1/inbox/read`. Voices/music/sounds can be shared (`kind:"audio"`, `sub`). Account deletion removes the creator profile, follows, messages, uses. Page: Explore bar (followers · credits earned · Messages with unread), Sounds filter, creator badge, sound cards, a post's creator row (Follow / Message / Work together, uses) — not on your own posts, creator page sheet, message sheet, inbox sheet, library share button on voice/music/sfx.
- Tests: `tests/v157.py` (ports 8473/8474; API-token customers "fan" and "pal").

## v159 Characters you pick come out as themselves; photos give the age, look and voice; voice samples say why
- **@characters in prompts** (page block `v159: …`): the agents' plan (`AGP.plan.prompt`) replaced the job's words in the 9457 `addJob` wrapper and dropped v87's photo lines ("Name = the person in reference picture N …"); the first picture of a video got the plan without them. Now an outer `addJob` wrapper writes the plan with the characters re-tagged (`tag159`: the name in the plan gets its `@`, or `@Name — ` goes in front) and marks `meta.plan159`; the 9457 wrapper leaves jobs with `plan159` or `face87` alone. `/v1/agents/plan` and `/v1/agents/storyboard` get `cast:[{name,look}]` (from `charsIn87`); lib/agents.js `cast159()` tells the agents to keep each name exactly (same script, never translated), never re-describe the person, add no one.
- **Home sends** (`HOME159` around `hfSend`; `home159()` around `makeVideo`/`makeImages`): the Video page's `S.draft.charId` and `S.draft.vo` and the Images page's `S.img.charId` are left out only while Home's jobs are made (they used to join every Home video: v87 adds `meta.char` as a character, intent.js speaks `meta.vo`).
- **No main character**: "Use as main character" and the Story badge removed; `S.story.charId` cleared at load, on every render and before a chapter is sent (it was added to every chapter and every scan).
- **Characters from photos**: the new-character input takes several photos (one character each, ≤ 6); `read159` asks `/v1/llm/json` (task "character from photo", `READ159`) for name, gender, age, height, weight, build, skin 0–5, eyes, hair, hairStyle, facial, headwear, outfitStyle, art, look, outfit, persona and the voice's feel; `apply159` checks each value against the page's lists; `autoVoice159` sets the voice gender, voice age (`AGEV159`), tone (`TONE159`) and a library voice (`voiceFor159`: gender → age band → feel) with `voice.auto159`. A picked voice drops `auto159`; while it's set the voice follows a changed gender/age (`follow159`). The page re-finds the character by id after every wait (VIEWS.chars swaps each character for a `normChar` copy). A photo added to a character keeps its name, resets `c.face` (so the new face is uploaded and used). Page: a line under the photo ("From the photo · 34 · Male · Voice: Brian" + play), an optional **Look** box (`#look159`, bound to `char.look`; your words set `lookOwn159` and win), "Change details (optional)" over the old sections; "Extra appearance details" moved up into the Look box.
- **Voice samples**: lib/localize.js `sampleFail159` — the engine out of credits (402 / `NO_CREDITS138`) → 503 `errorType:"quota"` "Voice samples can't be made right now — the AI engine isn't taking new work. Nothing was charged." (+ Arabic, owner alert via `noCredits138`), any other failure → 502 "… Try again in a minute."; staff get `detail`. Page: `sample159` — one at a time (the newest tap wins, a late older one isn't played), stops waiting after `SMP159.ms` (40 s) and says so; `vp91Play` and the prompt box's `vc157-play` use it. (Before, a 5xx without `errorAr` became "The AI provider is temporarily unavailable. Your credits were refunded.")
- Live note (Oct 2026): the WAN 2.7 card "The AI engine can't take new work right now" is v138's out-of-credits message — the engine account answered 402. Top it up; the check-up (`deploy/doctor.sh`) and Admin show the balance.
- Tests: `tests/v159.py` (ports 8480/8481; a stand-in engine that is also the voice engine and a stand-in text AI; en + ar).

## v160 People in the video speak the language you write in
- Owner's report: a prompt in Arabic gave videos whose people spoke English. Causes: the agents write the engine's prompt in English and translated the lines; the translation step (lib/v88.js `toEnglish`) kept only "quoted" text, not a line in (brackets) after a colon; nothing told the engine which language is spoken; Kling 2.6 itself speaks only Chinese and English (its own guide: other languages are turned into English).
- **lib/speech160.js**: `scriptLang` (language from the letters: Arabic/Persian/Urdu, Russian/Ukrainian, Chinese, Japanese, Korean, Hebrew, Hindi, Thai, Greek; null for Latin), `spokenLines` (quotes, `(…)` after a colon, "says:/يقول:/говорит:" to the end of the sentence), `talks`, `speechLang(original, final)` (the lines' script → a language the words name for speech, e.g. "يتحدث بالإنجليزية" → the script of the prompt; Latin-letter lines are left as they are), `withSpeech` adds "Everything said aloud is in Arabic, never English …" (missing lines included in quotes; no "words/text/written" in it, so v88's no-on-screen-text line isn't switched off), `speechRule` for the agents.
- **Translation** (v88 `put`): non-Latin spoken lines are swapped for `⟦n⟧` marks before the text AI sees the prompt and put back as `"…"`; a lost mark → the line is appended; quoted marks aren't kept twice. The v88 hook adds `withSpeech` to video/chapter jobs (not Motion Control, not `audio:false`), reading the original from `meta.said`, `title` (Home agent jobs keep the brief as title) or the prompt — whichever has non-Latin letters.
- **Agents** (lib/agents.js): `speechRule(prompt)` in the plan brief (lines stay as typed in quotes; say the language, accent, tone, gestures in English; people speak the detected language, never English unless asked; a talking person with no lines gets 1–2 lines in that language); the plan prompt passes `withSpeech`; the bible gets "Spoken language: …". Storyboard: "dialogue" is written in the spoken language even when the page is in English.
- intent.js: a voice-over in Arabic letters (no preset voice) → "Voice-over in Arabic, never English". sound134 `clean134`: `lang` from the script of `say` first; `/v1/sound134/quote` returns `lang`.
- Page block `v160: …`: `lang160`/`speech160` mirror; `ONLY160={kling26:["zh","en"]}` — nooi Auto (`pickVideo`) swaps Kling 2.6 for WAN 2.7 / Seedance / Veo 3.1 Fast when the speech is another language; picked by hand, a notice (once a minute) says so and suggests another engine or the character's voice with lip sync; `req157` (character voice) takes the language of its lines or the prompt, not the page's.
- Tests: `tests/v160.py` (ports 8482/8483; a stand-in text AI that translates the line on purpose). Updated nothing else; `tests/v88_api.mjs` still 33/33.
- Deploying (Oct 2026): the owner chose no preview — every push to the working branch goes live through `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` once the VPS secrets are set (until then it only checks the code).

## v161 Kling 4.0 — the agents, the characters and the voices as one system
- Owner's ask: "audio engines that work in harmony with the agents as a single system — don't forget Kling 4, Kie AI has it". Before, `kling40` was Kling 3.0 under another name (billing/page alias). Now it is Kie's **kling-4.0/reference-to-video** (and **kling-4.0/keyframe-to-video** for a first/last frame alone): 3–30 s, 720p/1080p, aspect auto/16:9/9:16/1:1/21:9, its own audio. Schema from docs.kie.ai (market/kling/v4-*) cross-checked with the @apicity/kie client (elements ≤7 with 2–4 image_urls + voice_url; voices ≤3 {name,audio_url}; image_urls ≤10 named @image_N; 15 materials; every @mention must name a material; reserved names image_N/video_N/keyframe_N/object_N/audio_N/first_frame/last_frame).
- **lib/kling161.js** (pure): `cast161(body)` (createJob, before the English step — each "@Name" of a character becomes `#castN`; only kept for Kling 4.0 jobs, a long video with a cast keeps kling40 in every part), `castRoute161` (after routing: not Kling 4.0 → the names come back, cast dropped), `castNames161` (providers/kie.js `fullPrompt` puts names back for every other engine), `keepRefs161` (a start frame switches to keyframe-to-video only when there's no cast, voice or # picture), `kling4Input` (the request: # pictures → @image_N; a character with ≥2 pictures → an element with its voice_url, with 1 → @image_N + a standalone voice `Name_voice`; the box's voice for whoever speaks → `@speaker`; first/last frame as extra @image_N; stray @ removed), `hiRes161`.
- **lib/voices161.js** (jobHooks.before, after v88/intent): each character's library voice → `voiceSample161` (lib/localize.js: the same sample people hear, made once, in the speech language) as `voiceUrl`; the box's voice (`meta.voice161 {voice|clip, who}`) goes to the named character (or the first), else to whoever speaks; the customer's cloned clip (`inputs.vVoice161`) → its sound alone, 20 s mp3; faces no longer on the server are dropped (tags renumbered). Addresses from the page are never used (`cast161` strips `voiceUrl`, `voice161.url`).
- Billing: `ENGINE_COST.video.kling40 = 0.7125` (28.5 Kie credits/s at 720p → 169 credits per 5 s); 1080p+ ×1.5 (42.5/s); alias kling40→kling30 removed. Models name "Kling 4.0"; long132 part max 30; refs130 `REF_STUDIO130` + the page's `REFOK130` include kling40.
- Agents: `engine161(b)` in the plan brief when `b.model === "kling40"` (lines in quotes after who says them, delivery, never the timbre); `cast159` lists each character's chosen voice (`castVoices161` from the page).
- Page block `v161: …`: Kling 4.0 in the lists (M_ALIAS entry removed, HF_TOP, ENG_MAX 30, PART132 30, PRICE_TABLE 169, Pro plan text); `addJob` wrapper (outermost): a kling40 video with @characters that have faces sends `meta.cast161` (faces = the real face + finished looks, ≤4; voice = `c.voice.vid`) and sets `chars87` so v87 makes no first-frame picture; the v157 box voice (preset, or clone with consent) rides as `meta.voice161` (+ the clip in slot `vVoice161`) and `VC157.send` is cleared (no voice step after); "Your clip — the lips follow it" is unchanged. `price157`/`vcSheet157` say no extra credits; `hfCost` 1080p ×1.5; agents get `model` + `castVoices161`.
- Tests: `tests/v161.py` (ports 8484/8485; a token customer for prices — the local user is the owner and never charged). `tests/v130.py` now uses Kling 3.0 as "the engine that can't take # pictures".

## v162 An AI agent (Claude over MCP) makes motion-graphics videos and fixes them
- Owner's ask: try Claude's MCP with nooi.ai, make motion-graphics videos, find the mistakes while making them and fix them in code. A real MCP session (a token, `POST /mcp`) on a local copy found and fixed:
- **MCP gaps** (lib/edit148.js, lib/motion150.js): `nooi_project_new` (an agent can start from nothing — before, only the app could make a project, and only with a clip); `nooi_preview_frame` asks the editor that has the project open to draw the exact frame (motion scenes, texts, effects) — the request rides on the editor's 4-second `GET /v1/projects/:id/ops` (`want162`), the page (`look162`) draws it and posts a JPEG to `POST /v1/projects/:id/look162`; without an open editor (`editorOpen162`: an ops call in the last 15 s) it says what is on screen in words (`onScreen162`) instead of an error; `nooi_export` has the open editor export the file (`edRender139`, uploaded) — call again with `render_id` for `{state, url}`; `nooi_motion_scene_edit` (op `motion_edit`, server + page) changes or removes a scene; `nooi_timeline_read` lists each scene's items/number/colour. Requests live in memory 15 min; a taken request not answered (30 s frame / 10 min video) is offered again; tokens can't answer.
- **Video mistakes seen in the frames**: on 9:16 everything sat in a thin middle band (`mgDraw150` sized from min(W,H)) → scenes ×`clamp(H/W*.75,1,1.4)` (1.33 on reels, width caps kept); long lines shrank to tiny type → `split162`/`lines162` (two balanced lines, words/end/logo); node lines ran through the names → names on a dark tag; small numbers counted slowly (0→8) → land in 0.35 s.
- **Planner with no text AI** (public/editor150/plan.js `plan150`): every line of the brief becomes a scene in its order (before: lines without a number/list/app/call were dropped); `split150` splits at a comma/joining word near the middle (not after two words); a second counter from another line (not a version like "Kling 4.0"); lists "A, B and C" and "A و B و C" (an attached "و" only before "ال" — "واحد" stays whole), the last item loses its tail ("Seedance in one studio" → "Seedance"), lines starting with a number are lists last; Arabic-Indic digits; a closing call to action ("Start free…", "ابدأ…") goes on the end card instead of "Start today". The v150 Lingo brief keeps its exact scenes.
- **Export quality**: mediabunny's `QUALITY_HIGH` became a quantizer the browser's encoder turned into ~0.2 Mbit/s for 1080×1920 (blocks, smeared glow) → `q162` = `new Quality({quality:"high", preferBitrate:true, bitrateMode:"constant"})` (~3.4–3.8 Mbit/s).
- Also: a project opened from the account keeps its name (open139 sets `S.ed.title`; the autosave renamed it "Edit · 1 clips · …"); the toast for an agent's motion ad said nothing after "Your AI agent" (`opSay148` now says "built a motion ad" / "changed a motion scene").
- Tests: `tests/v162.py` (port 8488; an MCP client + the editor open in Playwright: new project, words-only preview, real frame from the editor, live scene edit, export with a real bitrate, name kept, planner and drawing checks). `tests/v150_agents.mjs`: a motion-scene preview with no editor is now words, not an error.

- Owner's asks: "Keep Kling 3, all the models with their icons will appear for the clients to decide", "Generator, Generators = engine", "Why you didn't add the super slow motion, it has to be smooth all the slow motions, like cinematic moves".
- **Engines in view** (page, v163 block): a row of engine icons with names (`row163`, `.ms163`, `translate="no"`) under nooi Auto / Engine in the home box (`hfMRow` wrapper, popular first: `HF_TOP`, Kling 4.0 + Kling 3.0 lead) and in the nooi Studio / Images prompt boxes (`page163`, built from the Engine list's own cards so locks/prices/order stay theirs; a tap clicks the real card). "All" opens the full list. The picked engine is kept in view (also RTL; scroll kept between redraws). Z-Image got its icon. - **Super slow motion** (lib/slowmo163.js): real in-between frames (`minterpolate` mci/aobmc/bidir at 30 ÷ slowest speed per second), then the time is bent along the speed curve (`warp163` → piecewise `setpts` per piece; pieces per core like fps96) and read at 30 fps; no sound. Choices `SLOW163`: slow 0.5× (+2), super 0.25× (+4), ultra 0.125× (+6), ramp (real → 0.25× → real, eased; +4); at most 2 minutes out. Jobs (`lib/jobs.js`): `meta.slow163` is checked in a before-hook (unknown or parts-shown-in-turn → dropped; the engine prompt gets `SLOW_WORDS163` — real-time action, crisp frames), priced in createJob, made in `slowStep163` after the engine (single video in `finish`, long videos after the join), refunded if it can't be made. Editor: Finish job tool `slowmo` (`slowAdapter163`, key `slow163` in adapterByKey) on the client's own file only (`ownsMedia163`), priced like the page (the page's fixed Finish price skips it).
- Page: nooi Studio's Frame rate chip → "Frames & slow motion" with 5 tiles (length before → after, price; too long = off), the Generate price includes it (in VIEWS.video's markup — the coin badge is built from it), a Slow-mo chip in the home box opens the same tiles; the card says "Making the in-between frames…" (stage `slow`). Editor: slowing a clip turns on device smoothing (`smooth:"flow"`) unless switched off; "Smooth with the engine · +N cr" makes the real file and swaps the clip (speed 1, original kept, "Back to the original clip").
